AS01E 佐剂 RSV 前 F 蛋白 疫苗 用于成人肺或肾移植:一项 2b 期随机试验。

AS01E-adjuvanted RSV pre-F protein vaccine in adults with lung or kidney transplant: a phase 2b randomized trial.
作者： Catherine Small, Deepali Kumar, Anum Abbas, Ana Sánchez Fructuoso, Shigeyoshi Yamanaga, Virginia Luz Pérez González, Hristos Karakizlis, Michael D. Nissen, Pyoeng Gyun Choe, Vera Paloschi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
免疫功能低下的成年人患严重 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疾病的风险增加。
设计与对象
这项多国开放标签 2b 期试验评估了 AS01E 佐剂 RSV 预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗(佐剂 RSVPreF3)在 18 岁以上肾移植或肺移植受者中为期 12 个月的免疫原性和安全性,按 1:1 随机分配接受一剂 (n = 131) 或间隔 30-60 天的两剂 (n = 130)。对照组由 50 岁以上的非免疫功能低下成年人 (n = 125) 接受单剂治疗。
核心方法
主要结果是移植受者在接受第二次辅助 RSVPreF3 剂量一个月后,RSV-A 和 RSV-B 中和滴度相对于第一剂的平均几何增加。次要结果包括与对照组相比,移植受者中添加佐剂的 RSVPreF3 的安全性和免疫原性的评估。
主要结果
In transplant recipients receiving two doses, neutralizing titers against RSV-A and RSV-B were numerically higher one month post-dose 2 than post-dose 1 (mean geometric increases: 1.34 [95% CI: 1.20, 1.48] for RSV-A [n = 111] and 1.26 [95% CI: 1.14, 1.40] for RSV-B [n = 111]). A single dose was immunogenic in transplant recipients, but showed slower and generally lower initial neutralizing responses than in non-immunocompromised controls, specifically in individuals receiving mycophenolate. RSVPreF3-specific CD4 + T-cell frequencies increased after vaccination versus baseline in all groups.
解释与创新
两种剂量的 RSVPreF3 佐剂均具有良好的耐受性,并且具有相似且可接受的安全性。总体而言,这些结果支持在肾移植和肺移植受者中添加佐剂的 RSVPreF3 具有良好的获益-风险特征。
科研与公卫意义
免疫应答 后 12 个月内,体液和细胞介导的 免疫应答 保持高于基线。这些发现表明,佐剂 RSVPreF3 疫苗 可以为免疫功能低下的个体提供持续的保护。
局限与证据强度
作者报告了一项随机 2 期试验的结果,显示佐剂 RSVPreF3 疫苗 具有免疫原性,并且在肺和肾移植受者中具有可接受的安全性,支持有利的获益风险概况。然而,该研究是开放标签的,样本量相对较小,这可能会限制研究结果的普遍性。

AS01E-adjuvanted RSV pre-F protein vaccine in adults with lung or kidney transplant: a phase 2b randomized trial.

Authors: Catherine Small, Deepali Kumar, Anum Abbas, Ana Sánchez Fructuoso, Shigeyoshi Yamanaga, Virginia Luz Pérez González, Hristos Karakizlis, Michael D. Nissen, Pyoeng Gyun Choe, Vera Paloschi
Original abstract (English)
Immunocompromised adults are at increased risk of severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease. This multicountry, open-label phase 2b trial (NCT05921903) evaluated the immunogenicity and safety of the AS01E-adjuvanted RSV prefusion F protein vaccine (adjuvanted RSVPreF3) over 12 months in kidney or lung transplant recipients ≥18 years, randomized 1:1 to receive one (n = 131) or two doses 30–60 days apart (n = 130). A control group of non-immunocompromised adults ≥50 years (n = 125) received a single dose. The primary outcome was the mean geometric increase in RSV-A and RSV-B neutralizing titers one month following a second adjuvanted RSVPreF3 dose relative to the first dose in transplant recipients. Secondary outcomes included assessment of the safety and immunogenicity of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in transplant recipients compared with the control group. In transplant recipients receiving two doses, neutralizing titers against RSV-A and RSV-B were numerically higher one month post-dose 2 than post-dose 1 (mean geometric increases: 1.34 [95% CI: 1.20, 1.48] for RSV-A [n = 111] and 1.26 [95% CI: 1.14, 1.40] for RSV-B [n = 111]). A single dose was immunogenic in transplant recipients, but showed slower and generally lower initial neutralizing responses than in non-immunocompromised controls, specifically in individuals receiving mycophenolate. RSVPreF3-specific CD4 + T-cell frequencies increased after vaccination versus baseline in all groups. Humoral and cell-mediated immune responses remained above baseline for 12 months post-vaccination. Both doses of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 were well tolerated and had a similar and acceptable safety profile. Overall, these results support a favorable benefit-risk profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in kidney and lung transplant recipients. The authors report results from a randomized phase 2 trial showing that the adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine was immunogenic and had an acceptable safety profile in lung and kidney transplant recipients, supporting a favorable benefit-risk profile.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Immunocompromised adults are at increased risk of severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease.
Design and population
This multicountry, open-label phase 2b trial evaluated the immunogenicity and safety of the AS01E-adjuvanted RSV prefusion F protein vaccine (adjuvanted RSVPreF3) over 12 months in kidney or lung transplant recipients ≥18 years, randomized 1:1 to receive one (n = 131) or two doses 30–60 days apart (n = 130). A control group of non-immunocompromised adults ≥50 years (n = 125) received a single dose.
Core methods
The primary outcome was the mean geometric increase in RSV-A and RSV-B neutralizing titers one month following a second adjuvanted RSVPreF3 dose relative to the first dose in transplant recipients. Secondary outcomes included assessment of the safety and immunogenicity of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in transplant recipients compared with the control group.
Main results
In transplant recipients receiving two doses, neutralizing titers against RSV-A and RSV-B were numerically higher one month post-dose 2 than post-dose 1 (mean geometric increases: 1.34 [95% CI: 1.20, 1.48] for RSV-A [n = 111] and 1.26 [95% CI: 1.14, 1.40] for RSV-B [n = 111]). A single dose was immunogenic in transplant recipients, but showed slower and generally lower initial neutralizing responses than in non-immunocompromised controls, specifically in individuals receiving mycophenolate. RSVPreF3-specific CD4 + T-cell frequencies increased after vaccination versus baseline in all groups.
Interpretation and novelty
Both doses of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 were well tolerated and had a similar and acceptable safety profile. Overall, these results support a favorable benefit-risk profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in kidney and lung transplant recipients.
Scientific and public-health significance
Humoral and cell-mediated immune responses remained above baseline for 12 months post-vaccination. These findings suggest that the adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine may provide sustained protection in immunocompromised individuals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The authors report results from a randomized phase 2 trial showing that the adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine was immunogenic and had an acceptable safety profile in lung and kidney transplant recipients, supporting a favorable benefit-risk profile. However, the study was open-label and the sample size was relatively small, which may limit the generalizability of the findings.

监测 2024 年澳大利亚免疫接种后不良事件。

Surveillance of adverse events following immunisation in Australia, 2024.
作者： Thuy Nguyen, Nicholas J. Wood, Kristine K. Macartney, Lucy Deng
摘要中文翻译本报告总结了澳大利亚向治疗用品管理局 (TGA) 报告的 2024 年所有 疫苗 免疫接种后不良事件 (AEFI) 的自发 监测 数据。免疫接种后的不良事件定义为免疫接种后发生的任何不良医疗事件,但不一定与 疫苗 的使用有因果关系。AEFI 数据分析持续进行,并且每年进行一次,以检测新的潜在安全问题或已知安全问题的变化。总体而言,2024 年服用 疫苗 的 AEFI 报告为 6,232 份。这意味着年度 AEFI 报告率为每 100,000 人 22.9 例和每 100,000 剂 疫苗 剂量 25.7 例,而每 100,000 例剂量为 20.8 例和每 100,000 例 24.1 例2023 年的剂量。2024 年观察到的 AEFI 报告率略有增加,可能是由于从 2023 年 11 月起在国家免疫计划 (NIP) 中引入了新的重组带状疱疹 疫苗(RZV,Shingrix),取代了活减毒带状疱疹 疫苗(ZLV,Zostavax),以及带状疱疹合格人群的扩大ZXQ术语MKQXZ。2024 年,RZV (Shingrix) 与报告的 AEFI 数量最多。免疫接种后最常报告的不良事件是用药错误;超敏反应;胃肠道非特异性症状;发热;和注射部位反应。在2024年新推出的疫苗中,最常见的不良事件是用药错误和COVID-19 疫苗的非特异性胃肠道症状(Comirnaty XBB.1.5和Spikevax XBB.1.5);对 DTPa-HepB-IPV-Hib 疫苗 (Vaxelis) 过敏和发热;妊娠期间佐剂重组 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 融合前 F 蛋白 疫苗 (Arexvy) 的用药错误和暴露;RZV (Shingrix) 过敏和非特异性胃肠道症状。2024 年 疫苗接种 后报告的死亡人数(28 份报告)少于 2023 年(34 份报告)。2024 年报告的 28 例死亡事件中,没有一例表明出现了安全信号。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本报告解决了监测免疫接种后不良事件 (AEFI) 的需要,以确保 疫苗 的安全,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗。该研究旨在评估 2024 年施用的 疫苗 的安全性,重点关注与 RSV 相关的不良事件。
设计与对象
该研究是 2024 年在澳大利亚进行的国家 监测 报告,涵盖该国管理的所有 疫苗。它包括来自治疗用品管理局 (TGA) 的有关人群报告的不良事件的数据。
核心方法
AEFI 数据是通过向 TGA 自发报告来收集的,并每年进行持续分析,以识别新的安全问题或已知安全问题的变化。分析重点是与 ZX​​QTERMAAQXZ 相关的不良事件的频率和类型,包括 RSV 疫苗。
主要结果
2024 年,服用 疫苗 的 AEFI 报告为 6,232 份,其中 RZV (Shingrix) 与报告的 AEFI 数量最多。最常报告的不良事件是用药错误、过敏、胃肠道非特异性症状、发热和注射部位反应。
解释与创新
作者解释说,2024 年 AEFI 报告率略有增加可能是由于新的重组带状疱疹 疫苗 (RZV) 的引入以及带状疱疹 疫苗接种 合格人群的扩大。这一发现凸显了监测新 疫苗 安全信号的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为正在进行的 疫苗 安全性(特别是 RSV 疫苗)的 监测 提供了信息,并支持需要持续监测不良事件,以确保公共卫生安全并改进 疫苗 计划。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于自发报告,这可能会引入偏见和漏报。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于观察数据而不是对照实验,并且解释基于观察到的趋势。

Surveillance of adverse events following immunisation in Australia, 2024.

Authors: Thuy Nguyen, Nicholas J. Wood, Kristine K. Macartney, Lucy Deng
Original abstract (English)
This report summarises Australia's spontaneous surveillance data for adverse events following immunisation (AEFI) for all vaccines administered in 2024, reported to the Therapeutic Goods Administration (TGA). An adverse event(s) following immunisation is defined as any untoward medical occurrence which follows immunisation but does not necessarily have a causal relationship with use of the vaccine. Analyses of AEFI data are done continuously and also annually to detect new potential safety issues or changes to known safety issues. Overall, there were 6,232 AEFI reports for vaccines administered in 2024. This represents an annual AEFI reporting rate of 22.9 per 100,000 population and 25.7 per 100,000 vaccine doses, compared with 20.8 per 100,000 population and 24.1 per 100,000 doses in 2023. The slight increase in the AEFI reporting rates observed in 2024 was likely driven by the introduction of a new recombinant zoster vaccine (RZV, Shingrix) which replaced the live attenuated zoster vaccine (ZLV, Zostavax) on the National Immunisation Program (NIP) from November 2023, along with the expansion of the eligible population for zoster vaccination. In 2024, RZV (Shingrix) was associated with the highest number of reported AEFIs. The most frequently reported adverse events following immunisation were medication errors; hypersensitivity; gastrointestinal nonspecific symptoms; pyrexia; and injection site reaction. Among vaccines newly introduced in 2024, the most commonly reported adverse events were medication errors and nonspecific gastrointestinal symptoms for COVID-19 vaccines (Comirnaty XBB.1.5 and Spikevax XBB.1.5); hypersensitivity and pyrexia for the DTPa-HepB-IPV-Hib vaccine (Vaxelis); medication errors and exposure during pregnancy for the adjuvanted recombinant respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) pre-fusion F protein vaccine (Arexvy); and hypersensitivity and nonspecific gastrointestinal symptoms for RZV (Shingrix). Fewer deaths were reported following vaccination in 2024 (28 reports) than in 2023 (34 reports). None of the 28 reported deaths in 2024 indicated an emerging safety signal.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This report addresses the need to monitor adverse events following immunisation (AEFI) to ensure vaccine safety, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccines. The study aimed to evaluate the safety profile of vaccines administered in 2024, with a focus on RSV-related adverse events.
Design and population
The study is a national surveillance report conducted in Australia during 2024, encompassing all vaccines administered in the country. It includes data from the Therapeutic Goods Administration (TGA) on adverse events reported across the population.
Core methods
AEFI data were collected through spontaneous reporting to the TGA and analysed continuously and annually to identify new safety concerns or changes in known safety issues. The analyses focused on the frequency and types of adverse events associated with vaccines, including RSV vaccines.
Main results
In 2024, there were 6,232 AEFI reports for vaccines administered, with RZV (Shingrix) associated with the highest number of reported AEFIs. The most frequently reported adverse events were medication errors, hypersensitivity, gastrointestinal nonspecific symptoms, pyrexia, and injection site reactions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the slight increase in AEFI reporting rates in 2024 was likely due to the introduction of the new recombinant zoster vaccine (RZV) and the expansion of the eligible population for zoster vaccination. This finding highlights the importance of monitoring new vaccines for safety signals.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the ongoing surveillance of vaccine safety, particularly for RSV vaccines, and support the need for continued monitoring of adverse events to ensure public health safety and improve vaccine programs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on spontaneous reporting, which may introduce bias and underreporting. The evidence is moderate as it relies on observational data rather than controlled experiments, and the interpretation is grounded in the observed trends.

Foldon 三聚化结构域的免疫原性以及对其进行屏蔽的努力。

Immunogenicity of the Foldon trimerization domain and efforts to shield it.
作者： John V. Dzimianski, Spencer R. Pierce, Sara M. O'Rourke, Ian J. A. Kunkel, Kevin Delgado-Cunningham, Aaron D. Gingerich, Jarrod J. Mousa, Ted M. Ross, Rebecca M. DuBois, Sara M. O’Rourke
摘要中文翻译编码与噬菌体 T4 fibritin 三聚化结构域融合的病毒糖蛋白抗原的蛋白质亚基和 mRNA 疫苗 越来越多地在人体临床试验中进行测试。AREXVY 和 ABRYSVO® 亚基 疫苗 由与 Foldon 融合的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预融合 F 糖蛋白组成,现已获得 FDA 批准并定期给药。然而,施用含有 Foldon 结构域的 疫苗 抗原有可能引发脱靶抗体反应。在这里,我们证明蛋白质亚基 疫苗 抗原 RSV F Foldon 和流感血凝素 (HA) Foldon 会引发针对 Foldon 结构域的抗体。为了抑制 Foldon 诱导的抗体反应,我们在 Foldon 的三个不同位点设计了具有 N-糖基化基序 突变 的流感计算优化的广泛反应性抗原 (COBRA) HA 蛋白。质谱分析证实 N-糖基化修饰在三个预定位点中的两个位点被部分占据。与野生型 COBRA HA Foldon 相比,三种 COBRA HA Foldon 突变抗原中的两种表现出抗 Foldon 单克隆和多克隆抗体的结合减少。然而,用COBRA HA Foldon突变抗原免疫的小鼠抗Foldon抗体的引发并没有显着减少。进一步的分析表明,观察到的抗体与突变型 Foldon 抗原结合的一些差异可归因于蛋白质序列的差异。总而言之,这些数据表明,虽然 N-糖基化修饰可能部分保护 Foldon 免受反应性抗体的影响,但这种屏蔽并不会显着降低其在初始环境中的免疫原性。重要的是,这些数据强调了 Foldon 三聚化结构域具有免疫原性,在含有 Foldon 的 疫苗 临床试验中应考虑这一点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 疫苗 中 Foldon 三聚化结构域的潜在免疫原性及其对引发脱靶抗体反应的影响。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在 Foldon 结构域中设计带有 N-糖基化基序 突变 的流感 HA 蛋白,并评估其在小鼠中的免疫原性。
核心方法
研究人员使用质谱分析 N-糖基化修饰,并使用针对 Foldon 结构域的单克隆和多克隆抗体评估抗体结合。
主要结果
三种 COBRA HA Foldon 突变体抗原中的两种显示出抗 Foldon 抗体的结合减少,但用这些突变体免疫的小鼠抗 Foldon 抗体的激发并没有显着减少。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,虽然 N-糖基化修饰可能部分屏蔽 Foldon 结构域,但它们不会显着降低其在初始环境中的免疫原性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果应该为包含 Foldon 的 疫苗 的临床试验设计提供信息,强调需要考虑其免疫原性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于小鼠,并未考虑人类 免疫应答,这可能有所不同。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Immunogenicity of the Foldon trimerization domain and efforts to shield it.

Authors: John V. Dzimianski, Spencer R. Pierce, Sara M. O'Rourke, Ian J. A. Kunkel, Kevin Delgado-Cunningham, Aaron D. Gingerich, Jarrod J. Mousa, Ted M. Ross, Rebecca M. DuBois, Sara M. O’Rourke
Original abstract (English)
Protein subunit and mRNA vaccines encoding viral glycoprotein antigens fused to the bacteriophage T4 fibritintrimerization domain are increasingly being tested in human clinical trials. AREXVY and ABRYSVO®, subunit vaccines composed of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prefusion F glycoprotein(s) fused to Foldon, are now FDA-approved and regularly administered. However, administration of vaccine antigens containing the Foldon domain has the potential to elicit off-target antibody responses. Here, we demonstrate that protein subunit vaccine antigens RSV F Foldon and influenza hemagglutinin (HA) Foldon elicit antibodies against the Foldon domain. To dampen Foldon-induced antibody responses, we engineered influenza computationally optimized broadly reactive antigen (COBRA) HA proteins with N-glycosylation motif mutations at three different sites in Foldon. Mass spectrometry analyses confirmed partial occupancy of N-glycosylation modifications at two of the three intended sites. Two of the three COBRA HA Foldon mutant antigens demonstrated reduced binding by anti-Foldon monoclonal and polyclonal antibodies compared to the wild-type COBRA HA Foldon. However, mice immunized with the COBRA HA Foldon mutant antigens did not have a significant reduction in the elicitation of anti-Foldon antibodies. Further analysis suggested that some of the observed differences in antibody binding to mutant Foldon antigens can be attributed to differences in protein sequence alone. Altogether, these data suggest that while the N-glycosylation modifications may partially shield the Foldon from reactive antibodies, this shielding does not significantly reduce its immunogenicity in a naïve setting. Importantly, these data highlight that the Foldon trimerization domain is immunogenic, which should be considered during clinical trials of vaccines that contain Foldon.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the potential immunogenicity of the Foldon trimerization domain in vaccines and its impact on eliciting off-target antibody responses.
Design and population
The study involved engineered influenza HA proteins with N-glycosylation motif mutations in the Foldon domain and evaluated their immunogenicity in mice.
Core methods
The researchers used mass spectrometry to analyze N-glycosylation modifications and assessed antibody binding using monoclonal and polyclonal antibodies against the Foldon domain.
Main results
Two of the three COBRA HA Foldon mutant antigens showed reduced binding by anti-Foldon antibodies, but mice immunized with these mutants did not have a significant reduction in anti-Foldon antibody elicitation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that while N-glycosylation modifications may partially shield the Foldon domain, they do not significantly reduce its immunogenicity in a naïve setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results should inform the design of clinical trials for vaccines containing Foldon, highlighting the need to consider its immunogenicity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mice and does not account for human immune responses, which may differ. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

临床与社区呼吸道病原体的基因组多样性 监测:华盛顿州 SARS-CoV-2、RSV 和流感病毒的比较分析

Genomic diversity of respiratory pathogens in clinical versus community surveillance: a comparative analysis of SARS-CoV-2, RSV, and influenza viruses in Washington State
作者： Joshua M. Kirsch, Anna Elias-Warren, Lauren A. Frisbie, Christina M. Lockwood, Ana A. Weil, Allison Black, Helen Y. Chu, Pavitra Roychoudhury, Kirsch, J. M., Weil, A. A.
摘要中文翻译背景呼吸道病毒的基因组监测通常依赖于寻求医疗保健的个体的序列,并且这些序列是否代表社区中传播的病毒尚不清楚。我们的目的是比较临床和社区样本之间流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的多样性、比例和动态。方法在这项回顾性比较基因组分析中,我们使用了来自西雅图地区基于社区的 基因组 研究的全 基因组 序列,以及来自华盛顿州卫生部的临床序列。我们比较了临床和社区样本之间的进化枝比例和动态、序列簇多样性、非同义 突变 以及 Ct 值。研究结果我们分析了 2019 年至 2021 年收集的 538 个 RSV 序列(278 个社区,260 个临床),2019 年至 2020 年收集的 1506 个流感序列(260 个社区,1246 个临床),以及 2020 年至 2022 年收集的 8660 个 SARS-CoV-2 序列(5923 个社区,2737 个临床)。进化枝比例没有差异RSV 或流感的临床样本和社区样本之间存在差异,但 SARS-CoV-2 存在差异:社区样本中的进化枝 21J (Delta) 和 21K (Omicron BA.1) 更为常见,而临床样本中的进化枝 22B (Omicron BA.5) 则更为常见。SARS-CoV-2 临床序列簇比社区簇更加多样化,而 RSV 和流感则没有显着差异。与单克隆抗体耐药性相关的几个尖峰 突变 在 SARS-CoV-2 社区样本中更为普遍。多个 SARS-CoV-2、RSV 和流感分支的临床样本中的 Ct 值较低。解释季节性呼吸道病毒在临床和社区样本中具有相似的动态和多样性,而大流行的 SARS-CoV-2 在不同环境中则不同。基因组监测 计划应结合临床和社区采样,特别是在大流行期间,以便更好地捕获病毒多样性。资助本分析由华盛顿州卫生部西北病原体基因组学卓越中心资助(华盛顿州卫生部合同号 HED29377-1,华盛顿大学联邦拨款号 NU50CK000630)。基础研究由盖茨风险投资公司资助。CONTEXTO_ST_ABS 研究之前的证据 C_ST_ABS 呼吸道病毒的基因组 监测 在 COVID-19 大流行期间大幅扩展,此后已扩展到 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和流感,但大多数序列来自寻求医疗护理的人。我们使用术语“基因组监测”、“社区 监测”、“呼吸道合胞病毒”、“流感”、“SARS-CoV-2”、“整个 基因组 测序”的组合检索了 PubMed 中截至 2026 年 9 月以英文发表的研究,并纳入了对来自特定临床或社区人群的呼吸道病毒进行测序的研究。之前的工作对住院患者进行了采样,对随机阳性样本进行了测序,或对废水进行了测序,并表明这些数据可以跟踪变异的出现、进化枝替换和医院 传播。然而,很少有研究比较同一人群和时期内同一病毒的临床和社区采样,因此尚不清楚临床衍生的序列是否代表社区中传播的病毒。这项研究的附加值据我们所知,这是第一项比较同一地区和重叠时间段内三种呼吸道病毒的临床和社区采样之间的病毒多样性的研究。使用来自华盛顿州社区 监测 研究和州临床 监测 的 10,000 多个完整 基因组,我们发现 RSV 和流感来源之间的进化枝比例和动态相似,但 SARS-CoV-2 不同。在更精细的遗传分辨率下,临床 SARS-CoV-2 序列比群落序列形成更多样化的簇,并且与单克隆抗体耐药性相关的几个尖峰 突变 在群落样本中更为普遍。所有可用证据的含义对于季节性呼吸道病毒,对寻求护理的人进行采样似乎可以捕获社区中传播的大部分多样性,这可能使资源有限的 监测 项目能够依赖临床标本。与具有一定免疫力的人群中的季节性循环相反,在大流行水平的传播期间,当很大程度上易感人群中的大量人在短时间内被感染时,临床和社区样本不可互换,并且仅对临床样本进行测序的程序存在缺失社区中传播的变异的风险,包括与抗病毒药物和单克隆抗体耐药性相关的突变。这些差异可能部分反映了可用的 SARS-CoV-2 基因组 数量大得多,以及大流行期间多种变体的异质混合和共同循环。因此,监测 计划应结合临床和社区抽样,未来的研究应测试这些模式是否适用于其他地区、季节和病原体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景呼吸道病毒的基因组监测通常依赖于寻求医疗保健的个体的序列,并且这些序列是否代表社区中传播的病毒尚不清楚。
设计与对象
我们的目的是比较临床和社区样本之间流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和 SARS-CoV-2 的多样性、比例和动态。
核心方法
方法在这项回顾性比较基因组分析中,我们使用了来自西雅图地区基于社区的 基因组 研究的全 基因组 序列,以及来自华盛顿州卫生部的临床序列。
主要结果
研究结果我们分析了 2019 年至 2021 年收集的 538 个 RSV 序列(278 个社区,260 个临床),2019 年至 2020 年收集的 1506 个流感序列(260 个社区,1246 个临床),以及 2020 年至 2022 年收集的 8660 个 SARS-CoV-2 序列(5923 个社区,2737 个临床)。
解释与创新
解释季节性呼吸道病毒在临床和社区样本中具有相似的动态和多样性,而大流行的 SARS-CoV-2 在不同环境中则不同。
科研与公卫意义
基因组监测 计划应结合临床和社区采样,特别是在大流行期间,以便更好地捕获病毒多样性。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Genomic diversity of respiratory pathogens in clinical versus community surveillance: a comparative analysis of SARS-CoV-2, RSV, and influenza viruses in Washington State

Authors: Joshua M. Kirsch, Anna Elias-Warren, Lauren A. Frisbie, Christina M. Lockwood, Ana A. Weil, Allison Black, Helen Y. Chu, Pavitra Roychoudhury, Kirsch, J. M., Weil, A. A.
Original abstract (English)
BackgroundGenomic surveillance of respiratory viruses typically relies on sequences from individuals seeking health care, and whether these sequences represent the viruses circulating in the community is not well-characterized. We aimed to compare the diversity, proportion, and dynamics of influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and SARS-CoV-2 between clinical and community samples. MethodsIn this retrospective comparative genomic analysis, we used whole-genome sequences from community-based surveillance studies in the Seattle area, and clinical sequences from the Washington State Department of Health. We compared clade proportions and dynamics, sequence-cluster diversity, non-synonymous mutations, and Ct values between clinical and community samples. FindingsWe analyzed 538 RSV sequences collected between 2019 and 2021 (278 community, 260 clinical), 1506 influenza sequences (260 community, 1246 clinical) between 2019 and 2020, and 8660 SARS-CoV-2 sequences (5923 community, 2737 clinical) from 2020 to 2022. Clade proportions did not differ between clinical and community samples for RSV or influenza, but differed for SARS-CoV-2: clades 21J (Delta) and 21K (Omicron BA.1) were more frequent in community samples and clade 22B (Omicron BA.5) in clinical samples. SARS-CoV-2 clinical sequence clusters were more diverse than community clusters, whereas RSV and influenza showed no significant difference. Several spike mutations associated with monoclonal antibody resistance were more prevalent in community samples for SARS-CoV-2. Ct values were lower in clinical samples for multiple SARS-CoV-2, RSV, and influenza clades. InterpretationSeasonal respiratory viruses had similar dynamics and diversity in clinical and community samples, whereas pandemic SARS-CoV-2 differed between settings. Genomic surveillance programs should combine clinical and community sampling, particularly during pandemics in order to better capture viral diversity. FundingThis analysis was funded by the Washington State Department of Health Northwest Pathogen Genomics Center of Excellence (WA DOH contract number HED29377-1, federal grant number NU50CK000630 to University of Washington). The underlying studies were funded by Gates Ventures. RESEARCH IN CONTEXTO_ST_ABSEvidence before this studyC_ST_ABSGenomic surveillance of respiratory viruses expanded substantially during the COVID-19 pandemic and has since been extended to respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and influenza, but most sequences come from people who sought medical care. We searched PubMed for studies published in English up to September 2026 using combinations of terms "genomic surveillance", "community surveillance", "respiratory syncytial virus", "influenza", "SARS-CoV-2", "whole genome sequencing", and included studies that sequenced respiratory viruses from defined clinical or community populations. Previous work has sampled hospitalized patients, sequenced random positive specimens, or sequenced wastewater, and has shown that such data can track variant emergence, clade replacement, and nosocomial transmission. However, few studies compared clinical and community sampling for the same virus in the same population and period, so it remains unclear whether clinically derived sequences represent the viruses circulating in the community. Added value of this studyTo our knowledge, this is the first study to compare viral diversity between clinical and community sampling for three respiratory viruses in the same region and overlapping time periods. Using more than 10,000 whole genomes from community-based surveillance studies and state clinical surveillance in Washington State, we found that clade proportions and dynamics were similar between sources for RSV and influenza, but differed for SARS-CoV-2. At finer genetic resolution, clinical SARS-CoV-2 sequences formed more diverse clusters than community sequences, and several spike mutations associated with monoclonal antibody resistance were more prevalent in community samples. Implications of all the available evidenceFor seasonal respiratory viruses, sampling people who seek care appears to capture much of the diversity circulating in the community, which may allow surveillance programs with limited resources to rely on clinical specimens. In contrast with seasonal circulation in a population with some pre-existing immunity, during pandemic-level transmission, when very large numbers of people in a largely susceptible population are infected over a short period, clinical and community samples are not interchangeable, and programs that sequence only clinical specimens risk missing variants circulating in the community, including mutations relevant to antiviral and monoclonal antibody resistance. These differences may partly reflect the much larger number of SARS-CoV-2 genomes available, as well as heterogeneous mixing and co-circulation of multiple variants during the pandemic. Surveillance programs should therefore combine clinical and community sampling, and future studies should test whether these patterns hold in other regions, seasons, and pathogens.

基于 PtCo/UiO-66-NH2@luminol 的增强型电化学发光免疫传感器,用于高灵敏度检测重组 RSV F 蛋白。

An enhanced electrochemiluminescence immunosensor based on PtCo/UiO-66-NH2@luminol for highly sensitive detection of recombinant RSV F protein.
作者： Xinyi Yu, Yingqiang Zhang, Qingyang Sun, Jun Cheng, Yuzhu Dai, Weizhong Wang, Zhen Guo
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 会导致婴儿和幼儿严重的下呼吸道感染。因此,开发灵敏、可靠的RSV蛋白检测方法对于疾病预防和控制具有重要意义。在本研究中,开发了一种夹心型电化学发光(ECL)生物传感器,用于高灵敏度检测重组RSV F蛋白。用金纳米粒子 (Au NP) 修饰玻碳电极 (GCE),以利用其优异的导电性和生物相容性。Au NP 不仅促进了电极界面上的电子转移,而且还为固定捕获抗体 (Ab 1 ) 提供了稳定的平台。该免疫传感器的制备方法是将壳聚糖和 Ab 1 依次组装在 Au NPs/GCE 上,用 BSA 封闭,捕获 RSV F 蛋白,并引入 Ab 2 /PtCo/UiO-66-NH 2 @luminol 探针,并通过物理吸附固定 Ab 1。进一步设计了多功能PtCo/UiO-66-NH 2 纳米复合材料,其中PtCo提供类似过氧化物酶的催化活性,UiO-66-NH 2 提供丰富的吸附位点和氨基官能团。该复合材料能够有效地逐步加载鲁米诺分子和检测抗体(Ab 2 ),从而在H 2 O 2 共反应体系中实现ECL信号的级联放大。在重组RSV F蛋白存在下,形成稳定的“电极-Ab 1 -RSV F蛋白-Ab 2 -探针”夹心复合物。在优化条件下,ECL强度与重组RSV F蛋白浓度在0.1 pg mL -1 至10 ng mL -1 范围内表现出良好的线性关系,计算检测限为0.025 pg mL -1,经该浓度实验验证(n = 6)。所提出的生物传感器表现出优异的稳定性、特异性和重现性,为重组RSV F蛋白的检测提供了灵敏且可靠的概念验证平台,可作为未来临床验证的基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种灵敏可靠的方法来检测重组 RSV F 蛋白,满足改进诊断方法以预防和控制婴儿和幼儿 RSV 感染的需求。
设计与对象
采用金纳米粒子修饰的玻碳电极和多功能 PtCo/UiO-66-NH2 纳米复合材料,开发了一种夹心型电化学发光 (ECL) 生物传感器,用于检测重组 RSV F 蛋白。
核心方法
该生物传感器是通过在金纳米颗粒上依次组装壳聚糖和抗体、用 BSA 封闭、捕获 RSV F 蛋白并引入带有另一种抗体的探针来制造的,所有这些都是为了实现 ECL 信号的级联放大。
主要结果
该生物传感器在0.1 pg mL-1至10 ng mL-1范围内与重组RSV F蛋白浓度呈现良好的线性关系,检测限为0.025 pg mL-1,并表现出优异的稳定性、特异性和重现性。
解释与创新
作者将该生物传感器解释为检测重组 RSV F 蛋白的敏感且可靠的平台,可作为未来临床验证的基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改进 RSV 诊断工具的开发提供信息,有助于疾病预防和控制,特别是婴儿和幼儿。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括需要进一步的临床验证以及关注重组蛋白而不是天然病毒,这可能会限制普遍性。

An enhanced electrochemiluminescence immunosensor based on PtCo/UiO-66-NH2@luminol for highly sensitive detection of recombinant RSV F protein.

Authors: Xinyi Yu, Yingqiang Zhang, Qingyang Sun, Jun Cheng, Yuzhu Dai, Weizhong Wang, Zhen Guo
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes severe lower respiratory tract infections in infants and young children. The development of sensitive and reliable methods for the detection of RSV proteins is therefore of great significance for disease prevention and control. In this study, a sandwich-type electrochemiluminescence (ECL) biosensor was developed for the highly sensitive detection of recombinant RSV F protein. A glassy carbon electrode (GCE) was modified with gold nanoparticles (Au NPs) to leverage their excellent conductivity and biocompatibility. The Au NPs not only facilitated electron transfer at the electrode interface but also provided a stable platform for immobilizing the capture antibody (Ab 1 ). The immunosensor was fabricated by sequentially assembling chitosan and Ab 1 on the Au NPs/GCE, blocking with BSA, capturing RSV F protein, and introducing an Ab 2 /PtCo/UiO-66-NH 2 @luminol probe, with Ab 1 immobilized by physical adsorption. A multifunctional PtCo/UiO-66-NH 2 nanocomposite was further designed, with PtCo providing peroxidase-like catalytic activity and UiO-66-NH 2 supplying abundant adsorption sites and amino functional groups. This composite enabled efficient stepwise loading of luminol molecules and detection antibodies (Ab 2 ), thereby achieving cascaded amplification of ECL signals in an H 2 O 2 coreactant system. In the presence of recombinant RSV F protein, a stable "electrode-Ab 1 -RSV F protein-Ab 2 -probe" sandwich complex was formed. Under optimized conditions, the ECL intensity showed a good linear relationship with recombinant RSV F protein concentration in the range of 0.1 pg mL -1 to 10 ng mL -1 , with a calculated detection limit of 0.025 pg mL -1 , experimentally verified at this concentration (n = 6). The proposed biosensor exhibited excellent stability, specificity, and reproducibility, providing a sensitive and reliable proof-of-concept platform for the detection of recombinant RSV F protein, which may serve as a foundation for future clinical validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a sensitive and reliable method for detecting recombinant RSV F protein, addressing the need for improved diagnostics to prevent and control RSV infections in infants and young children.
Design and population
A sandwich-type electrochemiluminescence (ECL) biosensor was developed for the detection of recombinant RSV F protein, using a glassy carbon electrode modified with gold nanoparticles and a multifunctional PtCo/UiO-66-NH2 nanocomposite.
Core methods
The biosensor was fabricated by sequentially assembling chitosan and an antibody on the gold nanoparticles, blocking with BSA, capturing RSV F protein, and introducing a probe with another antibody, all designed to achieve cascaded amplification of ECL signals.
Main results
The biosensor showed a good linear relationship with recombinant RSV F protein concentration in the range of 0.1 pg mL-1 to 10 ng mL-1, with a detection limit of 0.025 pg mL-1, and exhibited excellent stability, specificity, and reproducibility.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the biosensor as a sensitive and reliable platform for detecting recombinant RSV F protein, which may serve as a foundation for future clinical validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of improved diagnostic tools for RSV, aiding in disease prevention and control, particularly in infants and young children.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the need for further clinical validation and the focus on recombinant protein rather than native virus, which may limit generalizability.

西班牙呼吸道病毒感染的医院负担、临床严重程度和经济影响:一项使用医院数据的基于人群的研究(2019-2023)。

Hospital burden, clinical severity, and economic impact of respiratory viral infections in Spain: A population-based study using hospital data (2019-2023).
作者： Blanca Lopez-Villalba, Camino Prada-García, Jose Javier Castrodeza Sanz, J. C. Castrodeza-Sanz
摘要中文翻译简介:呼吸道病毒感染是西班牙住院、发病、死亡率 和医疗资源使用的主要原因。尽管流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 造成了大部分医疗负担,但其他呼吸道病毒的贡献却很少被研究。本研究旨在分析 2019-2023 年期间这些感染的医院负担、临床严重程度和经济影响。方法:使用西班牙最低基本数据集 (CMBD) 进行一项基于人群的回顾性研究,包括 2019 年至 2023 年间因呼吸道病毒感染 诊断 住院的情况。分析了流感、RSV、腺病毒、偏肺病毒、副流感和鼻病毒。结果:总共纳入了 154,753 名住院患者(平均年龄:39 岁;48.6% 为女性)。流感和呼吸道合胞病毒占住院治疗和总费用的大部分。2021 年住院人数显着减少,随后在 2022-2023 年恢复。RSV、偏肺病毒、鼻病毒和腺病毒主要影响儿童群体,而流感和副流感对老年人的影响更大。总体而言,6.5% 的患者需要入住 ICU,住院 死亡率 为 2.7%,其中流感患者最高(4.2%)。偏肺病毒和鼻病毒的 ICU 入住率最高。平均住院时间为 6.1 天(ICU 住院时间为 7.4 天)。尽管流感和呼吸道合胞病毒产生的总成本最高,但其他病毒每次发作的成本更高。结论:呼吸道病毒感染造成了巨大的医疗保健和经济负担,根据病毒类型和年龄组的不同,其影响也各不相同,这凸显了综合 监测、预防和医疗保健规划策略的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒感染是西班牙住院、发病、死亡率 和医疗资源使用的主要原因。尽管流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 造成了大部分医疗负担,但其他呼吸道病毒的贡献却很少被研究。
设计与对象
使用西班牙最低基本数据集 (CMBD) 进行了一项基于人群的回顾性研究,包括 2019 年至 2023 年间因呼吸道病毒感染 诊断 住院的患者。总共纳入了 154,753 例住院患者(平均年龄:39 岁;48.6% 为女性)。
核心方法
分析了流感、RSV、腺病毒、偏肺病毒、副流感和鼻病毒。该研究使用医院数据来评估这些感染的负担、临床严重程度和经济影响。
主要结果
流感和呼吸道合胞病毒占住院治疗和总费用的大部分。2021 年住院人数显着减少,随后在 2022-2023 年恢复。RSV、偏肺病毒、鼻病毒和腺病毒主要影响儿童群体,而流感和副流感对老年人的影响更大。总体而言,6.5% 的患者需要入住 ICU,住院 死亡率 为 2.7%,其中流感患者最高(4.2%)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,呼吸道病毒感染造成了巨大的医疗保健和经济负担,根据病毒类型和年龄组的不同,其影响也各不相同。这项研究为不同呼吸道病毒对住院和费用的相对贡献提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为呼吸道病毒感染的综合 监测、预防和医疗保健规划策略提供信息,特别强调流感和 RSV 对不同年龄组的独特影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于医院数据,可能无法涵盖所有​​呼吸道病毒感染。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于回顾性分析,不包括详细的实验室或临床数据。

Hospital burden, clinical severity, and economic impact of respiratory viral infections in Spain: A population-based study using hospital data (2019-2023).

Authors: Blanca Lopez-Villalba, Camino Prada-García, Jose Javier Castrodeza Sanz, J. C. Castrodeza-Sanz
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Respiratory viral infections are a major cause of hospitalization, morbidity, mortality, and healthcare resource use in Spain. Although influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) account for most of the healthcare burden, the contribution of other respiratory viruses has been less studied. This study aimed to analyze the hospital burden, clinical severity, and economic impact of these infections during the 2019-2023 period. METHODS: A retrospective population-based study was conducted using the Spanish Minimum Basic Data Set (CMBD), including hospitalizations with a diagnosis of respiratory viral infection between 2019 and 2023. Influenza, RSV, adenovirus, metapneumovirus, parainfluenza, and rhinovirus were analyzed. RESULTS: A total of 154,753 hospitalizations were included (mean age: 39 years; 48.6% female). Influenza and RSV accounted for most hospitalizations and total costs. A marked reduction in hospitalizations was observed in 2021, followed by recovery in 2022-2023. RSV, metapneumovirus, rhinovirus, and adenovirus predominantly affected the pediatric population, whereas influenza and parainfluenza had a greater impact on older adults. Overall, 6.5% of patients required ICU admission and in-hospital mortality was 2.7%, highest for influenza (4.2%). Metapneumovirus and rhinovirus showed the highest ICU admission rates. Mean hospital stay was 6.1 days (7.4 days in ICU). Although influenza and RSV generated the highest total costs, other viruses showed higher costs per episode. CONCLUSIONS: Respiratory viral infections impose a substantial healthcare and economic burden, with variable impact according to virus type and age group, highlighting the need for integrated surveillance, prevention, and healthcare planning strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viral infections are a major cause of hospitalization, morbidity, mortality, and healthcare resource use in Spain. Although influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) account for most of the healthcare burden, the contribution of other respiratory viruses has been less studied.
Design and population
A retrospective population-based study was conducted using the Spanish Minimum Basic Data Set (CMBD), including hospitalizations with a diagnosis of respiratory viral infection between 2019 and 2023. A total of 154,753 hospitalizations were included (mean age: 39 years; 48.6% female).
Core methods
Influenza, RSV, adenovirus, metapneumovirus, parainfluenza, and rhinovirus were analyzed. The study used hospital data to assess the burden, clinical severity, and economic impact of these infections.
Main results
Influenza and RSV accounted for most hospitalizations and total costs. A marked reduction in hospitalizations was observed in 2021, followed by recovery in 2022-2023. RSV, metapneumovirus, rhinovirus, and adenovirus predominantly affected the pediatric population, whereas influenza and parainfluenza had a greater impact on older adults. Overall, 6.5% of patients required ICU admission and in-hospital mortality was 2.7%, highest for influenza (4.2%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that respiratory viral infections impose a substantial healthcare and economic burden, with variable impact according to virus type and age group. This study provides novel insights into the relative contributions of different respiratory viruses to hospitalization and cost.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform integrated surveillance, prevention, and healthcare planning strategies for respiratory viral infections, particularly highlighting the distinct impacts of influenza and RSV on different age groups.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on hospital data and may not capture all respiratory viral infections. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on retrospective analysis and does not include detailed laboratory or clinical data.

基于三年聚合酶链反应的成人呼吸道感染 监测 和季节性模式。

Three-Year Polymerase Chain Reaction-Based Surveillance of Respiratory Tract Infections in Adults and Seasonal Patterns.
作者： Ling Xue, Yu-Hui Ye, Yuan-Xiang Li
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了江西省赣州市2023-2025年成人呼吸道感染的病原学构成、季节变化及危险因素。
设计与对象
2023-2025年期间,共有8,493名三级医院有呼吸道症状的成年人(≥18岁)入组。
核心方法
使用针对六类七种呼吸道病原体(包括 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV))的多重实时聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 面板对口咽和鼻咽拭子进行了测试。通过单变量分析和多变量逻辑回归分析数据。
主要结果
The overall pathogen detection rate was 4.14% (352/8,493), comprising 325 single infections (92.33%) and 27 mixed infections (7.67%). Among single infections, RSV (16.00%) predominated. Infections peaked in winter-spring (December-March). Age ≥65 years (OR = 2.35, 95% CI: 1.78-3.09), underlying diseases (OR = 2.41, 95% CI: 1.89-3.08) and emergency department admission (OR = 1.58, 95% CI: 1.24-2.01) were independent risk factors.
解释与创新
该研究强调了成人中与 呼吸道合胞病毒 相关的季节性模式和风险因素,为其流行病学行为提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持针对 65 岁以上成年人和患有基础疾病的人的季节性目标 监测 和优先 疫苗接种、扩大的诊断组合和基于早期 PCR 的 诊断。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一家三级医院和仅限摘要的证据,这可能会影响普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Three-Year Polymerase Chain Reaction-Based Surveillance of Respiratory Tract Infections in Adults and Seasonal Patterns.

Authors: Ling Xue, Yu-Hui Ye, Yuan-Xiang Li
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: This study investigated the aetiological composition, seasonal variation and risk factors of adult respiratory tract infections in Ganzhou, Jiangxi Province, during 2023-2025. METHODS: A total of 8,493 adults (≥18 years) with respiratory symptoms were enrolled from a tertiary hospital during 2023-2025. Oropharyngeal and nasopharyngeal swabs were tested using multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction (PCR) panels targeting six categories of seven respiratory pathogens: influenza A and B viruses, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), parainfluenza virus types I and III, adenovirus and Mycoplasma pneumoniae. Mixed infections (two or more pathogens) were reported separately. Data were analysed by univariate analysis and multivariate logistic regression. RESULTS: The overall pathogen detection rate was 4.14% (352/8,493), comprising 325 single infections (92.33%) and 27 mixed infections (7.67%). Among single infections, M. pneumoniae (39.69%), influenza A virus (28.00%) and RSV (16.00%) predominated. Infections peaked in winter-spring (December-March). Age ≥65 years (OR = 2.35, 95% CI: 1.78-3.09), underlying diseases (OR = 2.41, 95% CI: 1.89-3.08) and emergency department admission (OR = 1.58, 95% CI: 1.24-2.01) were independent risk factors. CONCLUSION: Adult respiratory infections in Ganzhou show clear winter-spring seasonality, with M. pneumoniae, influenza A virus and RSV as leading pathogens. These findings support seasonally targeted surveillance - with enhanced monitoring for M. pneumoniae and influenza during winter-spring months - and prioritised vaccination, expanded diagnostic panels and early PCR-based diagnosis for adults aged ≥65 years and those with underlying diseases, thereby strengthening local respiratory infection prevention and control strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigated the aetiological composition, seasonal variation and risk factors of adult respiratory tract infections in Ganzhou, Jiangxi Province, during 2023-2025.
Design and population
A total of 8,493 adults (≥18 years) with respiratory symptoms were enrolled from a tertiary hospital during 2023-2025.
Core methods
Oropharyngeal and nasopharyngeal swabs were tested using multiplex real-time polymerase chain reaction (PCR) panels targeting six categories of seven respiratory pathogens, including respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Data were analysed by univariate analysis and multivariate logistic regression.
Main results
The overall pathogen detection rate was 4.14% (352/8,493), comprising 325 single infections (92.33%) and 27 mixed infections (7.67%). Among single infections, RSV (16.00%) predominated. Infections peaked in winter-spring (December-March). Age ≥65 years (OR = 2.35, 95% CI: 1.78-3.09), underlying diseases (OR = 2.41, 95% CI: 1.89-3.08) and emergency department admission (OR = 1.58, 95% CI: 1.24-2.01) were independent risk factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the seasonal patterns and risk factors associated with respiratory syncytial virus in adults, providing insights into its epidemiological behavior.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support seasonally targeted surveillance and prioritised vaccination, expanded diagnostic panels and early PCR-based diagnosis for adults aged ≥65 years and those with underlying diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single tertiary hospital and abstract-only evidence, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

尼塞韦单抗 对有严重 RSV 风险的 8-23 个月儿童 RSV 住院治疗的有效性

Nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV hospitalizations in children 8-23 months at risk of severe RSV
作者： Regina M. Simeone, Margaret M. Newhams, Amanda B. Payne, Amber O. Orzel-Lockwood, Jemima Calixte, Natasha Halasa, Satoshi Kamidani, Hillary Crandall, Katherine Irby, Aline B. Maddux
摘要中文翻译重要性:尼塞韦单抗(呼吸道合胞病毒,RSV)是高风险婴幼儿在第二次RSV流行季期间住院的主要原因之一。尼塞韦单抗是一种长效单克隆抗体,针对RSV,推荐用于某些在第二次RSV流行季期间面临严重RSV风险的儿童,以预防严重RSV疾病。由于第二次流行季尼塞韦单抗的适用条件有限,评估其有效性具有挑战性。目的:我们评估了尼塞韦单抗在第二次RSV流行季期间,适用于接受尼塞韦单抗的高风险婴幼儿中,针对RSV相关住院的有效性。设计:这项回顾性病例对照研究采用测试阴性设计,于2024-2025和2025-2026年RSV流行季期间进行。背景:研究对象来自美国24个州的26家医院。参与者:在第二次RSV流行季期间符合接受尼塞韦单抗条件(根据2023年8月的推荐)的婴幼儿被纳入研究。年龄在8至23个月之间的尼塞韦单抗适用儿童,因呼吸道症状住院。病例患者RSV检测呈阳性,对照患者RSV检测呈阴性。暴露因素:在第二次RSV流行季之前或期间以及症状出现前至少7天接受尼塞韦单抗。结果和测量:RSV相关住院。我们使用多变量逻辑回归分析,调整年龄、住院月份和年份、人口普查地区和性别,比较病例和对照患者中第二次流行季尼塞韦单抗接受率的比值,以估计尼塞韦单抗的有效性。结果:分析了106例病例患者和125例对照患者。病例和对照患者在年龄、性别、种族和民族以及地区分布上相似。大多数病例和对照患者因早产慢性肺病而符合尼塞韦单抗的适用条件(分别为84.0%和69.6%)。总体而言,21例(19.8%)病例患者和53例(42.4%)对照患者接受了尼塞韦单抗。调整后的尼塞韦单抗对RSV相关住院的有效性为78.1%(95%置信区间,55.2%-89.3%);从接受尼塞韦单抗到症状出现的中位时间间隔为66.5天(四分位距为30-96天)。如果尼塞韦单抗在症状出现前8至59天接受,其有效性估计为88.5%(95%置信区间,63.4%-96.4%)。结论:本分析提供了真实世界中尼塞韦单抗对美国高风险儿童在第二次RSV流行季期间RSV相关住院的显著有效性估计。这些数据支持将尼塞韦单抗作为该人群预防策略的推荐。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是高危幼儿在第二个 RSV 季节住院的主要原因。我们评估了 尼塞韦单抗 在第二个 RSV 季节期间有资格接受 尼塞韦单抗 的幼儿对 RSV 相关住院治疗的有效性。
设计与对象
这项采用测试阴性设计的回顾性病例对照研究是在 2024-2025 和 2025-2026 RSV 季节进行的。招募对象为 24 名美国 26 家医院,其中包括在第二个 RSV 季节(按照 2023 年 8 月的建议)符合 尼塞韦单抗 条件的儿童。
核心方法
我们使用多变量逻辑回归估计了 尼塞韦单抗 的有效性,并根据年龄、住院月份和年份、人口普查地区和性别进行了调整,比较了病例和对照患者中接受第二季 尼塞韦单抗 的几率。
主要结果
分析了106例病例患者和125例对照患者。总体而言,21例(19.8%)病例患者和53例(42.4%)对照患者接受了尼塞韦单抗。调整后的尼塞韦单抗对RSV相关住院的有效性为78.1%(95%置信区间,55.2%-89.3%);从接受尼塞韦单抗到症状出现的中位时间间隔为66.5天(四分位距为30-96天)。
解释与创新
该分析提供了 尼塞韦单抗 对美国高危儿童在第二个 RSV 季节因 RSV 相关住院治疗的有效性的实际估计。
科研与公卫意义
这些数据支持将 尼塞韦单抗 作为该人群预防策略的建议。
局限与证据强度
大多数病例和对照患者因早产儿慢性肺病而符合 尼塞韦单抗 的条件(分别为 84.0% 和 69.6%)。所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。

Nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV hospitalizations in children 8-23 months at risk of severe RSV

Authors: Regina M. Simeone, Margaret M. Newhams, Amanda B. Payne, Amber O. Orzel-Lockwood, Jemima Calixte, Natasha Halasa, Satoshi Kamidani, Hillary Crandall, Katherine Irby, Aline B. Maddux
Original abstract (English)
Importance: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of hospitalization in high-risk young children during their second RSV season. Nirsevimab, a long-acting monoclonal antibody against RSV, is recommended for some children who are at increased risk for severe RSV and entering their second RSV season to prevent severe RSV disease. Because of limited eligibility criteria for second season nirsevimab, estimating its effectiveness is challenging. Objective: We evaluated nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalizations among young children eligible to receive nirsevimab during their second RSV season. Design: This retrospective case control study with a test-negative design was conducted during the 2024-2025 and 2025-2026 RSV seasons. Setting: Enrollment was in 26 hospitals across 24 U.S. Participants: Children eligible for nirsevimab during their second RSV season (as recommended in August 2023) were included. Nirsevimab-eligible children 8-23 months old hospitalized with respiratory symptoms were enrolled. Case-patients tested positive for RSV; control-patients tested negative for RSV. Exposures: Receipt of nirsevimab before or during the second RSV season and at least 7 days before symptom onset. Outcomes and measures: RSV-associated hospitalization. We estimated nirsevimab effectiveness using multivariable logistic regression adjusted for age, month and year of hospitalization, census region, and sex comparing the odds of second season nirsevimab receipt among case- and control-patients. Results: One hundred six case-patients and 125 control-patients were analyzed. Distributions of age, sex, race and ethnicity, and region were similar in case- and control-patients. Most case- and control-patients were eligible for nirsevimab because of chronic lung disease of prematurity (84.0% and 69.6%, respectively). Overall, 21 (19.8%) of case-patients and 53 (42.4%) of control-patients received nirsevimab. Adjusted nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 78.1% (95% CI, 55.2%-89.3%); the median interval from nirsevimab receipt to symptom onset was 66.5 days (IQR = 30-96 days). Effectiveness was estimated to be higher if nirsevimab was received 8-59 days before symptom onset (88.5%, 95% CI, 63.4%-96.4%). Conclusions: This analysis provides real-world estimates of substantial nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalizations for high-risk U.S. children in their second RSV season These data support recommendations for nirsevimab as a preventive strategy in this population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of hospitalization in high-risk young children during their second RSV season. We evaluated nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalizations among young children eligible to receive nirsevimab during their second RSV season.
Design and population
This retrospective case control study with a test-negative design was conducted during the 2024-2025 and 2025-2026 RSV seasons. Enrollment was in 26 hospitals across 24 U.S. Children eligible for nirsevimab during their second RSV season (as recommended in August 2023) were included.
Core methods
We estimated nirsevimab effectiveness using multivariable logistic regression adjusted for age, month and year of hospitalization, census region, and sex comparing the odds of second season nirsevimab receipt among case- and control-patients.
Main results
One hundred six case-patients and 125 control-patients were analyzed. Overall, 21 (19.8%) of case-patients and 53 (42.4%) of control-patients received nirsevimab. Adjusted nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalizations was 78.1% (95% CI, 55.2%-89.3%); the median interval from nirsevimab receipt to symptom onset was 66.5 days (IQR = 30-96 days).
Interpretation and novelty
This analysis provides real-world estimates of substantial nirsevimab effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalizations for high-risk U.S. children in their second RSV season.
Scientific and public-health significance
These data support recommendations for nirsevimab as a preventive strategy in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
Most case- and control-patients were eligible for nirsevimab because of chronic lung disease of prematurity (84.0% and 69.6%, respectively). The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design.

2022-2023 年津巴布韦急性呼吸道疾病成人中 A/B 型流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 的联合检测:COVID-19 疫苗 有效性研究的子研究

Influenza A/B, RSV and SARS-CoV-2 Co-Detection Among Adults with Acute Respiratory Illness in Zimbabwe, 2022–2023: A Sub-Study of a COVID-19 Vaccine Effectiveness Study
作者： Gamuchirai Mary Kavhumbura, Patricia Tapiwa Gundidza, Margaret Gurumani, Norest Kumbirai Beta, Vinie Kouamou, Tariro Makadzange, Azure Tariro Makadzange
摘要中文翻译背景:/目标:流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 会引起重叠的呼吸道疾病,但津巴布韦成人多病原体数据仍然有限。我们描述了参加 ZIMCOVVAR 研究的成年人中流感和 RSV 的检测以及与 SARS-CoV-2 的共同检测。方法:这项回顾性子研究包括 2022 年至 2023 年在津巴布韦六个省份招募的患有急性呼吸道疾病的成年人的鼻咽标本。排除重复和未关联的记录后,对 1,429 名参与者进行了分析。通过多重实时逆转录聚合酶链反应 (PCR) 检测甲型流感、乙型流感和 RSV;记录的目标周期阈值(Ct)≤30 暂时归类为信号。有效的特定目标分母仍未解决。检查了比例、置信区间、每月模式以及与 SARS-CoV-2 状态的关联。结果:分析 队列研究 包括 281 名 SARS-CoV-2 阳性参与者和 1,148 名阴性参与者;中位年龄为 35 岁,其中 68.2% 为女性。Ct 定义的甲型流感、RSV 和乙型流感信号分别出现在 191/1,429 (13.4%)、53/1,429 (3.7%) 和 2/1,429 (0.1%) 中。68/281 (24.2%) SARS-CoV-2 阳性参与者记录了甲型流感,而 123/1,148 (10.7%) 阴性参与者记录了甲型流感。RSV 记录分别为 3/281 (1.1%) 和 50/1,148 (4.4%)。这些是每个链接记录的临时信号,不是有效结果的积极性估计;测试组排除逆转了调整后的关联。结论:选定的成人 队列研究 显示记录的流感和 RSV 检测以及 SARS-CoV-2,支持对有症状成人中的多种呼吸道病原体的继续研究。由于抽样不具代表性且测试组效应尚未解决,研究结果需要谨慎解释;需要有质量保证的多季节 监测 来为当地预防规划提供信息。

问题与背景
流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 会引起重叠的呼吸道疾病,但津巴布韦成人多病原体数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
我们描述了参加 ZIMCOVVAR 研究的成年人中流感和 RSV 的检测以及与 SARS-CoV-2 的共同检测。这项回顾性子研究包括 2022 年至 2023 年在津巴布韦六个省份招募的患有急性呼吸道疾病的成年人的鼻咽标本。
核心方法

主要结果

由于抽样不具代表性且测试组效应尚未解决,研究结果需要谨慎解释;需要有质量保证的多季节 监测 来为当地预防规划提供信息。
局限与证据强度
这些是每个链接记录的临时信号,不是有效结果的积极性估计;测试组排除逆转了调整后的关联。

Influenza A/B, RSV and SARS-CoV-2 Co-Detection Among Adults with Acute Respiratory Illness in Zimbabwe, 2022–2023: A Sub-Study of a COVID-19 Vaccine Effectiveness Study

Authors: Gamuchirai Mary Kavhumbura, Patricia Tapiwa Gundidza, Margaret Gurumani, Norest Kumbirai Beta, Vinie Kouamou, Tariro Makadzange, Azure Tariro Makadzange
Original abstract (English)
Background: /Objectives: Influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) cause overlapping respiratory illness, but Zimbabwean adult multipathogen data remain limited. We described influenza and RSV detection and co-detection with SARS-CoV-2 among adults enrolled in the ZIMCOVVAR study. Methods: This retrospective sub-study included nasopharyngeal specimens from adults with acute respiratory illness recruited in six Zimbabwean provinces during 2022-2023. Following exclusion of duplicate and unlinked records, 1,429 participants were analyzed. Influenza A, influenza B, and RSV were tested by multiplex real-time reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction (PCR); recorded target cycle thresholds (Ct) ≤30 were provisionally classified as signals. Valid target-specific denominators remained unresolved. Proportions, confidence intervals, monthly patterns, and associations with SARS-CoV-2 status were examined. Results: The analytic cohort comprised 281 SARS-CoV-2-positive and 1,148 negative participants; median age was 35 years and 68.2% were female. Ct-defined influenza A, RSV, and influenza B signals occurred in 191/1,429 (13.4%), 53/1,429 (3.7%), and 2/1,429 (0.1%), respectively. Influenza A was recorded in 68/281 (24.2%) SARS-CoV-2-positive versus 123/1,148 (10.7%) negative participants. RSV was recorded in 3/281 (1.1%) versus 50/1,148 (4.4%), respectively. These are provisional signals per linked record, not valid-result positivity estimates; testing-group exclusion reversed the adjusted association. Conclusions: The selected adult cohort showed recorded influenza and RSV detections alongside SARS-CoV-2, supporting continued investigation of multiple respiratory pathogens in symptomatic adults. The findings require cautious interpretation because of nonrepresentative sampling and unresolved testing-group effects; quality-assured, multi-season surveillance is needed to inform local prevention planning.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) cause overlapping respiratory illness, but Zimbabwean adult multipathogen data remain limited.
Design and population
We described influenza and RSV detection and co-detection with SARS-CoV-2 among adults enrolled in the ZIMCOVVAR study. This retrospective sub-study included nasopharyngeal specimens from adults with acute respiratory illness recruited in six Zimbabwean provinces during 2022-2023.
Core methods

Proportions, confidence intervals, monthly patterns, and associations with SARS-CoV-2 status were examined.
Main results

Ct-defined influenza A, RSV, and influenza B signals occurred in 191/1,429 (13.4%), 53/1,429 (3.7%), and 2/1,429 (0.1%), respectively. Influenza A was recorded in 68/281 (24.2%) SARS-CoV-2-positive versus 123/1,148 (10.7%) negative participants. RSV was recorded in 3/281 (1.1%) versus 50/1,148 (4.4%), respectively.

Limitations and evidence strength

使用不同口罩进行超声洁牙期间气溶胶和飞溅传播的比较:与呼吸道感染相关的初步研究 传播

Comparison of Dissemination of Aerosol and Splatter during Ultrasonic Scaling using Different Masks: A Pilot Study with Relevance to Respiratory Infection Transmission
作者： Samidha Vivek Jambhekar
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:牙医经常暴露在大量的微生物负荷下,因为口腔是众所周知的巨大细菌负荷的来源。此外,由于COVID-19最近影响了全世界,因此口罩被认为是抑制传播的综合措施策略。然而,气溶胶和呼吸道感染控制的相关性超出了 COVID-19 的范围,因为包括季节性流感病毒(例如甲型 H1N1)pdm09 流感(通常称为猪流感)、甲型流感(H3N2)、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和其他呼吸道病毒在内的呼吸道病原体继续循环,并可能通过呼吸道颗粒传播。目的:比较使用不同面罩进行超声洁治时气溶胶和飞溅的传播情况。方法:在本研究中,我们通过预先标记的一厘米见方的刻度尺计算滤纸的表面积,评估了四种口罩的传播情况,即N-95带呼吸器、N-95不带呼吸器、布口罩和N-95不带呼吸器的组合以及普通外科口罩。结果:结合使用双重口罩(无过滤器的 N-95 加布口罩)可提供最佳保护,其次是单独的 N-95 口罩,最后是外科口罩。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决牙科环境中有效感染控制措施的需求,因为牙医经常暴露在高微生物负荷下。背景强调了在 COVID-19 背景下气溶胶和呼吸道感染控制的相关性,因为 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等呼吸道病原体继续循环,并可能通过呼吸道颗粒传播。
设计与对象
该试点研究评估了使用不同面罩进行超声洁牙期间气溶胶和飞溅的传播情况。该研究是在牙科环境中进行的,参与者是参与该手术的牙医。
核心方法
该研究比较了四种口罩的有效性:带呼吸器的 N-95、不带呼吸器的 N-95、布口罩和不带呼吸器的 N-95 的组合以及普通外科口罩。使用先前标记的一厘米见方的刻度来计算滤纸的表面积,以评估气溶胶和飞溅的传播。
主要结果
结果表明,双重口罩的组合使用提供了最好的保护——无过滤器的N-95加布口罩,其次是单独的N-95口罩,然后是外科口罩。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明双重遮蔽在牙科手术过程中提供了针对气溶胶传播的卓越保护。这是一种在呼吸道病原体存在问题的环境中控制感染的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对于改善牙科环境中的感染控制策略具有具体意义,特别是在减少 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等呼吸道病原体的 传播 方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项试点研究,限制了结果的普遍性。该证据基于评估气溶胶传播的单一方法,该方法可能无法完全捕捉现实场景中病原体 传播 的复杂性。

Comparison of Dissemination of Aerosol and Splatter during Ultrasonic Scaling using Different Masks: A Pilot Study with Relevance to Respiratory Infection Transmission

Authors: Samidha Vivek Jambhekar
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: Dentists are routinely exposed to a high amount of micro-organism load due to primarily being responsible for the treatment of the structures if the mouth – which is a well-known source of huge bacterial loads. Also, as COVID-19 recently affected the entire world, masks thus were considered to act as a comprehensive strategy of measures to suppress transmission. However, the relevance of aerosol and respiratory infection control extends beyond COVID-19, as respiratory pathogens including seasonal influenza viruses such as influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, commonly referred to as swine flu, influenza A(H3N2), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), and other respiratory viruses continue to circulate and may be transmitted through respiratory particles. Aim: To compare the dissemination of aerosol and splatter during ultrasonic scaling using different masks. Method: In this study we evaluated the dissemination of four masks that is N-95 with respirator, N-95 without respirator, a combination of cloth mask and N-95 without respirator, and normal surgical mask by calculating the surface are of filter paper with the help of a previously marked scale which was divided into one-centimeter squares. Results: Best protection was seen to be offered by the combined use of the double masking – N-95 without filter plus cloth mask, followed by N-95 mask alone and then the surgical mask.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for effective infection control measures in dental settings, where dentists are routinely exposed to high microbial loads. The background highlights the relevance of aerosol and respiratory infection control beyond the context of COVID-19, as respiratory pathogens such as respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) continue to circulate and may be transmitted through respiratory particles.
Design and population
This pilot study evaluated the dissemination of aerosol and splatter during ultrasonic scaling using different masks. The study was conducted in a dental setting, and the participants were dentists involved in the procedure.
Core methods
The study compared the effectiveness of four masks: N-95 with respirator, N-95 without respirator, a combination of cloth mask and N-95 without respirator, and normal surgical mask. The surface area of filter paper was calculated using a previously marked scale divided into one-centimeter squares to assess the dissemination of aerosol and splatter.
Main results
The results indicated that the best protection was provided by the combined use of double masking – N-95 without filter plus cloth mask, followed by the N-95 mask alone, and then the surgical mask.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that double masking offers superior protection against aerosol dissemination during dental procedures. This is a novel approach to infection control in settings where respiratory pathogens are a concern.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have specific implications for improving infection control strategies in dental environments, particularly in reducing the transmission of respiratory pathogens such as respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a pilot study, which limits the generalizability of the results. The evidence is based on a single method of assessing aerosol dissemination, which may not fully capture the complexity of pathogen transmission in real-world scenarios.

环境对多病原体呼吸道阳性的滞后影响:中国四川七家哨点医院的分布式滞后非线性分析

Lagged environmental effects on multi-pathogen respiratory positivity: a distributed-lag nonlinear analysis of seven Sichuan sentinel hospitals, China
作者： Fengmiao Hu, Xingyu Zhou, Lijun Zhou, Zhirui Li, Shuang Dong, Biao Yang, Chongkun Xiao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查环境对呼吸道阳性的滞后影响,特别关注环境因素与 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 检测之间的关系。
设计与对象
该研究使用了2024年至2026年间中国四川七家哨点医院的病例级数据,每周汇总了总共23,014例急性呼吸道感染病例进行分析。
核心方法
应用滞后 6 周的准泊松分布滞后非线性模型,结合趋势样条、城市效应和对数偏移来估计温度、相对湿度和六种空气污染物的累积相对风险,同时控制病原体内的错误发现率。
主要结果
At least one pathogen was detected in 51.9% of cases, with co-detection occurring in 18.2% of cases. Haemophilus influenzae and influenza were predominant, and relative humidity showed the most stable single-exposure association with RSV positivity (RR 2.01, 95% CI 1.06–3.82).
解释与创新
湿度-RSV 信号对于模型规范来说是稳健的,但在共同变化的暴露下却很脆弱,这凸显了在暴露科学和环境 流行病学 中考虑环境相互作用的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对影响 RSV 阳性的环境因素的见解,这可以为 监测 和呼吸道感染的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
湿度与 RSV 的关联因温度协同调节而无效,这表明存在潜在的混杂因素,并且该研究的结果基于七家哨点医院的单一数据集,这可能会限制普遍性。

Lagged environmental effects on multi-pathogen respiratory positivity: a distributed-lag nonlinear analysis of seven Sichuan sentinel hospitals, China

Authors: Fengmiao Hu, Xingyu Zhou, Lijun Zhou, Zhirui Li, Shuang Dong, Biao Yang, Chongkun Xiao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Acute respiratory infections (ARI) impose a large global burden and show marked seasonality that implicates environmental drivers, yet the lagged effects of these exposures on pathogen-specific positivity remain poorly characterised. Using case-level data from seven sentinel hospitals in Sichuan, China (2024–2026) aggregated to a weekly panel of 23 014 ARI cases, we applied quasi-Poisson distributed-lag non-linear models (cross-basis, 6-week lag; trend spline; city effects; log offset) to estimate cumulative relative risks (90th versus 10th percentile) for temperature, relative humidity and six air pollutants, with within-pathogen false-discovery-rate control. At least one pathogen was detected in 51.9% of cases and co-detection in 18.2%; Haemophilus influenzae and influenza predominated. Relative humidity gave the most stable single-exposure association with respiratory syncytial virus positivity ( RR 2.01, 95% CI 1.06–3.82); it failed FDR control (adjusted P = 0.130 ) and was nullified by temperature co-adjustment ( RR 1.05). influenzae rose with temperature and ozone and fell with PM2.5; SARS-CoV-2 tracked carbon monoxide. Twelve of 40 estimates survived FDR control. The humidity–RSV signal is robust to model specification yet fragile under co-varying exposures — the cautionary lesson — and the reusable output is a transparent screening framework that exposes such fragility, serving exposure science and environmental epidemiology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the lagged environmental effects on respiratory positivity, particularly focusing on the relationship between environmental factors and the detection of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Design and population
The study used case-level data from seven sentinel hospitals in Sichuan, China, between 2024 and 2026, with a total of 23,014 weekly ARI cases aggregated for analysis.
Core methods
Quasi-Poisson distributed-lag non-linear models were applied with a 6-week lag, incorporating trend splines, city effects, and log offsets to estimate cumulative relative risks for temperature, relative humidity, and six air pollutants, while controlling for within-pathogen false-discovery-rate.
Main results
At least one pathogen was detected in 51.9% of cases, with co-detection occurring in 18.2% of cases. Haemophilus influenzae and influenza were predominant, and relative humidity showed the most stable single-exposure association with RSV positivity (RR 2.01, 95% CI 1.06–3.82).
Interpretation and novelty
The humidity–RSV signal is robust to model specification yet fragile under co-varying exposures, highlighting the importance of considering environmental interactions in exposure science and environmental epidemiology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the environmental factors influencing RSV positivity, which can inform surveillance and public health strategies for respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The humidity–RSV association was nullified by temperature co-adjustment, indicating potential confounding, and the study's results are based on a single dataset from seven sentinel hospitals, which may limit generalisability.

使用美国 监测 公开数据对儿科 RSV 和流感住院负担进行自适应预测和警报评估:一项时间序列研究

Adaptive forecasting and alert evaluation for pediatric RSV and influenza hospitalization burden using public U.S. surveillance data: a time-series study
作者： zhang jingrong, Yu-Ying Zhang, Yu-Hong Lin, Shao-Mei Lin, Hong-Wei Zheng, Si-Wen Chen, Jing-Rong Zhang
摘要中文翻译小儿流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 住院人数激增给医院和公共卫生机构带来压力。大流行后季节性的变化和 监测 实践削弱了固定的季节性基线。我们评估了公共 监测 信号是否可以支持对儿科流感和 RSV 住院负担的透明预警评估。我们使用 2018 年 10 月至 2026 年 3 月期间汇总的美国 RESP-NET 住院情况 监测 和相关公共信号流进行了一项回顾性生态时间序列研究。结果是 0-4 岁和 5-17 岁儿童每周因流感和 RSV 住院的比率。我们使用季节性/自回归项和可行的公共信号(包括急诊科 (ED) 就诊百分比指标)将去年同周的季节性朴素基线与扩展窗脊模型进行了比较。预测通过滚动源验证进行评估,最终数据敏感性不包括最近 4 周的数据。我们总结了训练分位数警报阈值下的平均绝对误差 (MAE)、警报效用、起始提前时间和预期损失。对于 0-4 岁儿童的 RSV,ED 可行岭模型在最终数据敏感性下,与水平 1 的季节性朴素基线相比,改进了匹配周预测(配对 MAE 改进 3.49;95% 置信区间 [CI] 1.59 至 5.69;配对 n = 104)。流感收益较小且不确定(0-4岁,水平1:配对MAE改善0.60;95% CI -0.24至1.24;配对n = 51)。使用与不使用 ED 项的自回归/季节岭模型的直接匹配比较并未显示出稳定的独立 ED 点误差增益。在固定的第 90 个训练分位数警报阈值和漏报:误报成本比为 2.0 时,相对于季节性基线的预期损失比率对于 0-4 岁的 RSV 为 0.280,对于 0-4 岁的流感为 0.545。这些发现支持针对特定病原体、本地校准的儿科呼吸道病毒住院警告策略。相对于固定的前一年基线,自适应山脊模型改进了 RSV 警告指标,而 ED 术语并未提高超出 AR+季节术语的匹配点误差精度。因此,ED 信号是警报策略评估的可行综合层,而不是独立预测增益的证据。

问题与背景
小儿流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 住院人数激增给医院和公共卫生机构带来压力。大流行后季节性的变化和 监测 实践削弱了固定的季节性基线。
设计与对象
我们进行了一项回顾性生态时间序列研究,使用美国汇总结果,即 0-4 岁和 5-17 岁儿童每周因流感和 RSV 住院的比率。
核心方法
我们使用季节性/自回归项和可行的公共信号(包括急诊科 (ED) 就诊百分比指标)将去年同周的季节性朴素基线与扩展窗脊模型进行了比较。
主要结果
对于 0-4 岁儿童的 RSV,ED 可行岭模型在最终数据敏感性下,与水平 1 的季节性朴素基线相比,改进了匹配周预测(配对 MAE 改进 3.49;95% 置信区间 [CI] 1.59 至 5.69;配对 n = 104)。流感收益较小且不确定(0-4岁,水平1:配对MAE改善0.60;95% CI -0.24至1.24;配对n = 51)。

这些发现支持针对特定病原体、本地校准的儿科呼吸道病毒住院警告策略。相对于固定的前一年基线,自适应山脊模型改进了 RSV 警告指标,而 ED 术语并未提高超出 AR+季节术语的匹配点误差精度。
科研与公卫意义
因此,ED 信号是警报策略评估的可行综合层,而不是独立预测增益的证据。
局限与证据强度
使用与不使用 ED 项的自回归/季节岭模型的直接匹配比较并未显示出稳定的独立 ED 点误差增益。

Adaptive forecasting and alert evaluation for pediatric RSV and influenza hospitalization burden using public U.S. surveillance data: a time-series study

Authors: zhang jingrong, Yu-Ying Zhang, Yu-Hong Lin, Shao-Mei Lin, Hong-Wei Zheng, Si-Wen Chen, Jing-Rong Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Pediatric influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) hospitalization surges strain hospitals and public health agencies. Post-pandemic changes in seasonality and surveillance practice have weakened fixed seasonal baselines. We evaluated whether public surveillance signals can support transparent warning evaluation for pediatric influenza and RSV hospitalization burden. We conducted a retrospective ecological time-series study using aggregated U.S. RESP-NET hospitalization surveillance and related public signal streams from October 2018 through March 2026. Outcomes were weekly pediatric hospitalization rates for influenza and RSV among children aged 0–4 and 5–17 years. We compared a prior-year same-week seasonal naïve baseline with expanding-window ridge models using seasonal/autoregressive terms and feasible public signals, including emergency department (ED) percent-visit indicators. Forecasts were evaluated by rolling-origin validation, with a finalized-data sensitivity excluding the most recent 4 weeks. We summarized mean absolute error (MAE), alert utility, onset lead time, and expected loss under training-quantile alert thresholds. For RSV among children aged 0–4 years, an ED-feasible ridge model improved matched-week forecasts versus the seasonal naïve baseline at horizon 1 under the finalized-data sensitivity (paired MAE improvement 3.49; 95% confidence interval [CI] 1.59 to 5.69; paired n = 104). Influenza gains were smaller and uncertain (ages 0–4 years, horizon 1: paired MAE improvement 0.60; 95% CI -0.24 to 1.24; paired n = 51). Direct matched comparisons of autoregressive/seasonal ridge models with versus without ED terms did not show a stable independent ED point-error gain. At a fixed 90th training-quantile alert threshold and miss: false-alarm cost ratio of 2.0, expected-loss ratios relative to the seasonal baseline were 0.280 for RSV ages 0–4 years and 0.545 for influenza ages 0–4 years. These findings support pathogen-specific, locally calibrated strategies for pediatric respiratory-virus hospitalization warning. Adaptive ridge models improved RSV warning metrics relative to a fixed prior-year baseline, whereas ED terms did not improve matched point-error accuracy beyond AR+season terms. ED signals are therefore a feasible syndromic layer for alert-policy evaluation, not evidence of an independent forecasting gain.

Influenza gains were smaller and uncertain (ages 0–4 years, horizon 1: paired MAE improvement 0.60; 95% CI -0.24 to 1.24; paired n = 51).

尼塞韦单抗 免疫计划的有效性是否会从一个 RSV 季节到下一个季节下降?来自意大利东北部的真实证据

Does the Effectiveness of the Nirsevimab Immunization Programs Decline from One RSV Season to the Next? Real-World Evidence from Northeastern Italy
作者： Francesca Valent, Giulia Degani
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估 尼塞韦单抗 免疫计划的有效性是否从一个 RSV 季节到下一个季节下降,解决其持续影响的有限现实证据。
设计与对象
在意大利乌迪内省进行了一项基于人群的回顾性 队列研究 研究,分析了 2025 年 4 月 1 日至 2026 年 3 月 31 日之间在 2025-2026 年 RSV 季节和随后的 RSV 季节期间出生的婴儿。
核心方法
使用多变量逻辑回归和随时间变化的免疫状态的 Cox 比例风险模型来估计有效性,同时从出院诊断中确定与 RSV 相关的住院治疗。
主要结果
Of 2620 infants, 2293 (87.5%) received nirsevimab, with 13 immunized and 7 non-immunized infants hospitalized for RSV-related conditions (5.67 per 1000 vs. 21.41 per 1000, p = 0.008). Adjusted analyses showed significantly lower odds and hazard of RSV-related hospitalization among immunized infants (OR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09–0.57; HR 0.27, 95% CI 0.11–0.69).
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,在第二个季节活动期间,使用 尼塞韦单抗 进行婴儿普遍免疫对于预防 RSV 相关住院治疗仍然非常有效,但在随后的 RSV 季节中没有观察到残留保护的证据。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,尼塞韦单抗 在随后的 RSV 季节中保持有效性,支持其在免疫计划中的使用,同时强调需要继续使用 监测 来监测更广泛的呼吸道病毒感染模式。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项生态分析,并非旨在建立因果关系,这限制了有关非 RSV 呼吸道病毒的结论的强度。

Does the Effectiveness of the Nirsevimab Immunization Programs Decline from One RSV Season to the Next? Real-World Evidence from Northeastern Italy

Authors: Francesca Valent, Giulia Degani
Original abstract (English)
Background: Nirsevimab has demonstrated high effectiveness in preventing severe respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) disease in infants. However, real-world evidence from subsequent seasonal immunization campaigns remains limited. We evaluated the effectiveness of universal infant immunization during a second RSV season and assessed protection during the following season after immunization in the previous year. Methods: We conducted a population-based retrospective cohort study in the Province of Udine, Italy, using linked anonymized regional healthcare databases. All infants born between 1 April 2025 and 31 March 2026 were included in the analysis of the 2025–2026 RSV season. RSV-related hospitalizations were identified from hospital discharge diagnoses. Effectiveness was estimated using multivariable logistic regression and a Cox proportional hazards model with time-varying immunization status. We also assessed RSV-related hospitalizations during the subsequent season among infants who had received nirsevimab during the previous campaign. Seasonal hospitalization risks for RSV and other respiratory viral infections were compared across pre- and post-implementation seasons. Results: Of 2620 infants born during the second campaign, 2293 (87.5%) received nirsevimab. Twenty infants were hospitalized for RSV-related conditions, including 13/2293 (5.67 per 1000) immunized and 7/327 (21.41 per 1000) non-immunized infants (p = 0.008). Adjusted analyses showed significantly lower odds and hazard of RSV-related hospitalization among immunized infants (OR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09–0.57; HR 0.27, 95% CI 0.11–0.69), corresponding to estimated effectiveness of 78% and 73%, respectively. During the following RSV season, six infants from the previous birth cohort were hospitalized with RSV-related conditions, all of whom had received nirsevimab during the preceding campaign (cohort coverage: 78.5%). Hospitalization rates for non-RSV respiratory viruses were lower in the two post-implementation seasons than in the preceding seasons, although this ecological analysis was not designed to establish causality. Conclusions: Universal infant immunization with nirsevimab remained highly effective in preventing RSV-related hospitalization during the second seasonal campaign, achieving high population coverage. No evidence of residual protection was observed during the subsequent RSV season after immunization in the previous year. Continued real-world surveillance is warranted to monitor nirsevimab effectiveness and the broader epidemiology of respiratory viral infections following widespread implementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether the effectiveness of nirsevimab immunization programs declines from one RSV season to the next, addressing the limited real-world evidence on its sustained impact.
Design and population
A population-based retrospective cohort study was conducted in the Province of Udine, Italy, analyzing infants born between 1 April 2025 and 31 March 2026 during the 2025–2026 RSV season and the subsequent RSV season.
Core methods
Effectiveness was estimated using multivariable logistic regression and a Cox proportional hazards model with time-varying immunization status, while RSV-related hospitalizations were identified from hospital discharge diagnoses.
Main results
Of 2620 infants, 2293 (87.5%) received nirsevimab, with 13 immunized and 7 non-immunized infants hospitalized for RSV-related conditions (5.67 per 1000 vs. 21.41 per 1000, p = 0.008). Adjusted analyses showed significantly lower odds and hazard of RSV-related hospitalization among immunized infants (OR 0.22, 95% CI 0.09–0.57; HR 0.27, 95% CI 0.11–0.69).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that universal infant immunization with nirsevimab remained highly effective in preventing RSV-related hospitalizations during the second seasonal campaign, but no evidence of residual protection was observed in the subsequent RSV season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that nirsevimab maintains effectiveness in subsequent RSV seasons, supporting its use in immunization programs, while highlighting the need for continued surveillance to monitor broader respiratory viral infection patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was an ecological analysis and not designed to establish causality, which limits the strength of conclusions regarding non-RSV respiratory viruses.
