扩大 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防、延长窗口和追赶对婴儿 RSV 住院治疗的影响:意大利艾米利亚-罗马涅的一项为期三个季节、多中心、基于人群的研究

Impact of expanding nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis, extended window and catch-up, on RSV hospitalizations in infants: a three-season, multicentre, population-based study in Emilia-Romagna, Italy
作者： Susanna Maria Roberta Esposito, Stefano Bendoni, Giuseppe Maglietta, Alessandro De Fanti, Chiara Ghizzi, Giacomo Biasucci, Federico Marchetti, Melodie Olivia Loredana Rosa Aricò, Gianluca Vergine, Marcello Stella
摘要中文翻译背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿期住院的主要原因,尤其是在生命的最初几个月。在早期的现实世界研究中,尼塞韦单抗 已大大减少了与 RSV 相关的住院治疗,但将已建立的季节性计划扩大到更广泛的出生 队列研究 所带来的增量人口水平效益仍不确定。在意大利艾米利亚-罗马涅,尼塞韦单抗 作为季节性出生时免疫预防在 2024-25 年 RSV 季节引入,并在 2025-26 年扩大到包括对 2025 年 8 月至 2026 年 3 月出生的婴儿进行出生时免疫预防,以及对 2025 年 4 月至 7 月出生的婴儿进行补种免疫。我们评估了这种扩大的策略是否进一步减少了 RSV 兼容的病例住院治疗。方法 我们使用 11 个中心的出院记录进行了一项多中心、基于人群的前后观察研究,涵盖约 69% 的 12 个月以下婴儿住院病例。对三个季节的 RSV 兼容住院情况进行了比较:尼塞韦单抗 之前 (2023-24)、季节性 (2024-25) 和延长季节性 + 追赶 (2025-26)。发病率 比率 (IRR) 通过调整性别和年龄的负二项式回归来估计。还计算了净避免住院。结果 年龄标准化的 RSV 兼容住院率从 2023-24 年的每 100,000 人日 21.9 例下降到 2024-25 年的 10.1 例和 2025-26 年的 7.8 例。与季节性策略相比,延长季节性+追赶策略与住院风险进一步降低 22% 相关(IRR 0.78;95% CI 0.69–0.88;p<0.001),与 尼塞韦单抗 前相比降低 64%(IRR 0.36;95% CI 0.31–0.42)。与 2024-25 年相比,估计避免了 103.3 次额外住院治疗,与 尼塞韦单抗 之前相比,避免了 677.8 次住院治疗,分别相当于每 10,000 名婴儿中大约有 52 次和 339 次避免住院治疗。0-180 天婴儿的研究结果是一致的。结论 与仅季节性给药相比,扩大 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防与 RSV 相容性住院治疗的进一步显着减少相关。鉴于观察设计,因果推断应保持谨慎。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿期住院的主要原因,尤其是在生命的最初几个月。在早期的现实世界研究中,尼塞韦单抗 已大大减少了与 RSV 相关的住院治疗,但将已建立的季节性计划扩大到更广泛的出生 队列研究 所带来的增量人口水平效益仍不确定。
设计与对象
尼塞韦单抗 是在 2024-25 RSV 季节作为季节性出生免疫预防引入的,并在 2025-26 年扩大到包括对 2025 年 8 月至 2026 年 3 月出生的婴儿进行出生免疫预防,以及对 2025 年 4 月至 7 月出生的婴儿进行补种免疫。该研究在意大利艾米利亚-罗马涅的 11 个中心进行,覆盖约 69% 12 个月以下婴儿的住院率。
核心方法
该研究采用了多中心、基于人群的前后观察设计。发病率 比率 (IRR) 通过调整性别和年龄的负二项式回归来估计。还计算了净避免住院。
主要结果
Age-standardized RSV-compatible hospitalization rates declined from 21.9 per 100,000 person-days in 2023–24 to 10.1 in 2024–25 and 7.8 in 2025–26. Compared with the Seasonal strategy, the Extended seasonal + catch-up strategy was associated with a further 22% reduction in hospitalization risk (IRR 0.78; 95% CI 0.69–0.88; p<0.001) and a 64% reduction versus Pre-Nirsevimab (IRR 0.36; 95% CI 0.31–0.42).
解释与创新
扩大 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防与 RSV 相容性住院治疗的进一步显着减少相关,超过仅季节性给药所实现的效果。0-180 天婴儿的研究结果是一致的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果证明,扩大 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防可以进一步减少婴儿 RSV 相关住院治疗,这对旨在预防易感人群中严重 RSV 疾病的公共卫生策略具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
鉴于观察设计,因果推断应保持谨慎。该研究的结果基于出院记录,并根据性别和年龄进行调整,但其他混杂因素可能尚未得到充分考虑。

Impact of expanding nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis, extended window and catch-up, on RSV hospitalizations in infants: a three-season, multicentre, population-based study in Emilia-Romagna, Italy

Authors: Susanna Maria Roberta Esposito, Stefano Bendoni, Giuseppe Maglietta, Alessandro De Fanti, Chiara Ghizzi, Giacomo Biasucci, Federico Marchetti, Melodie Olivia Loredana Rosa Aricò, Gianluca Vergine, Marcello Stella
Original abstract (English)
Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of hospitalization in infancy, particularly during the first months of life. Nirsevimab has substantially reduced RSV-related hospitalizations in early real-world studies, but the incremental population-level benefit of expanding an established seasonal programme to broader birth cohorts remains uncertain. In Emilia-Romagna, Italy, nirsevimab was introduced for the 2024–25 RSV season as seasonal at-birth immunoprophylaxis and expanded in 2025–26 to include at-birth immunoprophylaxis for infants born from August 2025 through March 2026 and catch-up immunization for infants born from April through July 2025. We assessed whether this expanded strategy provided an additional reduction in RSV-compatible hospitalizations. Methods We conducted a multicentre, population-based observational before–after study using hospital discharge records from eleven centres, covering approximately 69% of hospitalizations among infants aged <12 months. RSV-compatible hospitalizations were compared across three seasons: Pre-Nirsevimab (2023–24), Seasonal (2024–25), and Extended seasonal + catch-up (2025–26). Incidence rate ratios (IRRs) were estimated by negative binomial regression adjusted for sex and age. Net avoided hospitalizations were also calculated. Results Age-standardized RSV-compatible hospitalization rates declined from 21.9 per 100, 000 person-days in 2023–24 to 10.1 in 2024–25 and 7.8 in 2025–26. Compared with the Seasonal strategy, the Extended seasonal + catch-up strategy was associated with a further 22% reduction in hospitalization risk (IRR 0.78; 95% CI 0.69–0.88; p<0.001) and a 64% reduction versus Pre-Nirsevimab (IRR 0.36; 95% CI 0.31–0.42). An estimated 103.3 additional hospitalizations were avoided versus 2024–25 and 677.8 versus Pre-Nirsevimab, corresponding to approximately 52 and 339 avoided hospitalizations per 10, 000 infants, respectively. Findings were consistent in infants aged 0–180 days. Conclusions Expanding nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis was associated with a further significant reduction in RSV-compatible hospitalizations beyond that achieved with seasonal-only administration. Given the observational design, causal inference should remain cautious.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of hospitalization in infancy, particularly during the first months of life. Nirsevimab has substantially reduced RSV-related hospitalizations in early real-world studies, but the incremental population-level benefit of expanding an established seasonal programme to broader birth cohorts remains uncertain.
Design and population
Nirsevimab was introduced for the 2024–25 RSV season as seasonal at-birth immunoprophylaxis and expanded in 2025–26 to include at-birth immunoprophylaxis for infants born from August 2025 through March 2026 and catch-up immunization for infants born from April through July 2025. The study was conducted in Emilia-Romagna, Italy, across eleven centres, covering approximately 69% of hospitalizations among infants aged <12 months.
Core methods
The study used a multicentre, population-based observational before–after design. Incidence rate ratios (IRRs) were estimated by negative binomial regression adjusted for sex and age. Net avoided hospitalizations were also calculated.
Main results
Age-standardized RSV-compatible hospitalization rates declined from 21.9 per 100,000 person-days in 2023–24 to 10.1 in 2024–25 and 7.8 in 2025–26. Compared with the Seasonal strategy, the Extended seasonal + catch-up strategy was associated with a further 22% reduction in hospitalization risk (IRR 0.78; 95% CI 0.69–0.88; p<0.001) and a 64% reduction versus Pre-Nirsevimab (IRR 0.36; 95% CI 0.31–0.42).
Interpretation and novelty
Expanding nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis was associated with a further significant reduction in RSV-compatible hospitalizations beyond that achieved with seasonal-only administration. Findings were consistent in infants aged 0–180 days.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide evidence that expanding nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis can further reduce RSV-compatible hospitalizations in infants, which has important implications for public health strategies aimed at preventing severe RSV disease in vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
Given the observational design, causal inference should remain cautious. The study's results are based on hospital discharge records and adjusted for sex and age, but other confounding factors may not have been fully accounted for.

112例毛细支气管炎病原学检测患儿病原谱及其与住院时间的相关性——河北省儿童医院132例住院病例毛细支气管炎的回顾性研究

Pathogen spectrum and its association with hospital length of stay in 112 pathogen-tested children with bronchiolitis: a retrospective study from a cohort of 132 hospitalized cases in Hebei Children's Hospital
作者： Xiao-Juan Liu, Jiang-Hong Yan, Bei-Bei Cao, Ying-Xue Wang, Hui Xu, Ying-Qian Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查河北省儿童医院住院儿童毛细支气管炎的病原谱和流行病学特征,分析不同病原体或混合感染与临床康复速度的关系,为我国北方地区婴儿毛细支气管炎的预防和毛细支气管炎提供临床参考。
设计与对象
对2022年9月至2025年2月河北儿童医院收治的132例毛细支气管炎患儿的临床资料进行回顾性研究。患者分为病原检测组(112例)和病原未检测组(20例)。
核心方法
入院24 h内采集痰液或鼻咽分泌物标本,进行多重聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测常见呼吸道病原体。
主要结果
132 名 毛细支气管炎 儿童中,中位年龄为 6.6 个月,中位住院时间 (LOS) 为 6.0 天。在 112 名接受病原体检测的患者中,6 个月以下婴儿占快速恢复组的 55.8%,而延迟恢复组则占 41.7%(P = 0.1363);RSV阳性率为50.0% vs. 58.3%(P=0.3771),HRV阳性率为30.8% vs. 31.7%(P=0.9186),混合感染率为13.5% vs. 25.0%(P=0.1253);这些差异均未达到统计学显着性。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,RSV和HRV是该地区住院毛细支气管炎的核心优势病原体。他们还指出,5月份出现了非典型的淡季疫情高峰,但其同比稳定性有待进一步确认。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果深入了解了中国北方RSV和HRV的病原体谱和季节模式,可为监测和婴儿毛细支气管炎的针对性预防策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和潜在选择偏倚的限制,但敏感性分析发现测试组和未测试组之间没有统计学上的显着差异,表明残余选择偏倚的风险有限。

Pathogen spectrum and its association with hospital length of stay in 112 pathogen-tested children with bronchiolitis: a retrospective study from a cohort of 132 hospitalized cases in Hebei Children's Hospital

Authors: Xiao-Juan Liu, Jiang-Hong Yan, Bei-Bei Cao, Ying-Xue Wang, Hui Xu, Ying-Qian Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Objective To investigate the pathogen spectrum and epidemiological characteristics of hospitalized children with bronchiolitis admitted to Hebei Children's Hospital, and to analyze the relationship between different pathogens or mixed infections and clinical recovery speed, providing a clinical reference for the prevention and treatment of infant bronchiolitis in northern China. Methods Based on the electronic medical record system, the clinical data of 132 children hospitalized with bronchiolitis at Hebei Children's Hospital from September 2022 to February 2025 were retrospectively analyzed. Sputum or nasopharyngeal secretion specimens were collected within 24 h of admission, and multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) was performed to detect common respiratory pathogens. Patients were divided into a pathogen-tested group (112 cases) and a pathogen-untested group (20 cases). The tested group comprised a positive subgroup of 99 cases (77 single infections and 22 mixed infections) and a negative subgroup of 13 cases. Clinical recovery evaluation: clinical recovery speed was evaluated using the hospital length of stay (LOS); patients were categorized into a rapid-recovery group (LOS ≤ 5 days) and a delayed-recovery group (LOS > 5 days). To avoid selection bias arising from missing pathogen data, all analyses examining the association between pathogen profile and LOS were restricted to the 112 pathogen-tested patients; descriptive demographic characteristics (Section 3.1 ) were based on the full cohort of 132 patients. A subsequent sensitivity analysis (Section 3.1.1 ) found no statistically significant differences between the 20 untested and 112 tested patients in sex, age, LOS, or rapid-/delayed-recovery group composition, suggesting a limited risk of residual selection bias from this approach. Results Among the 132 children with bronchiolitis, the median age was 6.6 months and the median LOS was 6.0 days. In the tested group (112 cases), the overall positive rate was 88.4% (99/112), with single infection accounting for 68.8% (77/112), mixed infection accounting for 19.6% (22/112), and negative results accounting for 11.6% (13/112). The top three detected pathogens were respiratory syncytial virus (RSV, 54.5%), human rhinovirus (HRV, 31.3%), and parainfluenza virus (PIV, 6.3%), with RSV/HRV (11 cases) being the most common mixed infection. RSV and mixed infections exhibited a bimodal epidemic feature during the study period: in addition to the traditional winter-spring heating season (November to March of the following year), an atypical off-season epidemic peak emerged in May, with a single-month RSV detection rate of 82.1% (23/28). In contrast, hospitalized cases were relatively sparse in other months of the summer-autumn season (June to October). Because case ascertainment was not a complete census in every year and sample sizes were limited, the year-to-year stability of this off-season peak was not confirmed; see Section 2.4 and the Discussion. Within the 112 pathogen-tested patients (rapid-recovery group, n = 52; delayed-recovery group, n = 60), infants < 6 months accounted for 55.8% (29/52) of the rapid-recovery group versus 41.7% (25/60) of the delayed-recovery group ( P = 0.1363); RSV positivity was 50.0% (26/52) vs. 58.3% (35/60) ( P = 0.3771), HRV positivity was 30.8% (16/52) vs. 31.7% (19/60) ( P = 0.9186), and mixed-infection rate was 13.5% (7/52) vs. 25.0% (15/60) ( P = 0.1253); none of these differences reached statistical significance. Conclusion RSV and HRV are the core dominant pathogens of hospitalized bronchiolitis in this region. In addition to the traditional winter-spring heating-season peak, an atypical off-season epidemic peak occurred in May, although its year-to-year stability requires further confirmation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the pathogen spectrum and epidemiological characteristics of hospitalized children with bronchiolitis in Hebei Children's Hospital, and to analyze the relationship between different pathogens or mixed infections and clinical recovery speed, providing a clinical reference for the prevention and treatment of infant bronchiolitis in northern China.
Design and population
A retrospective study was conducted using clinical data from 132 children hospitalized with bronchiolitis at Hebei Children's Hospital from September 2022 to February 2025. Patients were divided into a pathogen-tested group (112 cases) and a pathogen-untested group (20 cases).
Core methods
Sputum or nasopharyngeal secretion specimens were collected within 24 h of admission, and multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) was performed to detect common respiratory pathogens.
Main results
Among the 132 children with bronchiolitis, the median age was 6.6 months and the median length of stay (LOS) was 6.0 days. Within the 112 pathogen-tested patients, infants < 6 months accounted for 55.8% of the rapid-recovery group versus 41.7% of the delayed-recovery group (P = 0.1363); RSV positivity was 50.0% vs. 58.3% (P = 0.3771), HRV positivity was 30.8% vs. 31.7% (P = 0.9186), and mixed-infection rate was 13.5% vs. 25.0% (P = 0.1253); none of these differences reached statistical significance.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that RSV and HRV are the core dominant pathogens of hospitalized bronchiolitis in this region. They also noted an atypical off-season epidemic peak in May, although its year-to-year stability requires further confirmation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the pathogen spectrum and seasonal patterns of RSV and HRV in northern China, which can inform surveillance and targeted prevention strategies for infant bronchiolitis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its retrospective design and potential selection bias, but a sensitivity analysis found no statistically significant differences between the tested and untested groups, suggesting limited risk of residual selection bias.

通过捕捉 传播 中学校假期、公共假期和季节变化的影响来改进呼吸道病毒预测

Improving respiratory virus forecasts by capturing the effects of school holidays, public holidays and seasonal changes in transmission
作者： Ruarai J Tobin, Oliver Eales, Michael J Plank, Dylan J Morris, James M McCaw, Freya M Shearer, Tobin, R. J., Plank, M. J., Morris, D. J., McCaw, J. M.
摘要中文翻译呼吸道病毒在全球范围内造成了沉重的发病率和 死亡率 负担,预测这一负担的模型被广泛用于支持公共卫生决策。然而,这些预测的公共卫生效用可能会因预测准确性差而受到限制。在这项工作中,我们展示了如何在 传播 中捕获学校假期、公共假期和季节变化的影响,从而提高呼吸道病毒的预测性能。我们构建了一个贝叶斯更新模型,其中有效再生数 R(t) 由对数加法成分组成,包括学校和公共假期效应以及每年定期的高斯过程。该模型还允许相对报告率随学校和公共假期而变化。我们使用该模型来预测澳大利亚五个司法管辖区 28 天范围内的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 病例 发病率。应用于 2025 年冬季,我们的预测在所有设置下都优于随机游走基线预测。我们发现,假期和季节性 传播 效应的纳入提高了三种病原体的预测性能。我们发现,学校假期与人口范围内 R(t) 下降约 5-15% 相关,但通常与报告变化无关。总的来说,我们的结果为将假期和季节性影响纳入未来呼吸道病毒预测模型提供了支持。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒在全球范围内造成了沉重的发病率和 死亡率 负担,预测这一负担的模型被广泛用于支持公共卫生决策。
设计与对象
该研究构建了贝叶斯更新模型来预测 2025 年冬季澳大利亚五个辖区的呼吸道病毒病例 发病率。
核心方法
该模型在 传播 中纳入了学校假期、公共假期和季节变化的对数累加组件,并允许这些期间的相对报告率发生变化。
主要结果
我们的预测在所有设置下都优于随机游走基线预测,并且纳入假期和季节性影响提高了对三种病原体的预测性能。
解释与创新
研究结果为将假期和季节性影响纳入未来呼吸道病毒预测模型提供了支持,突显了它们提高预测准确性的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,结合假期和季节性 传播 效应可以提高呼吸道病毒预测的准确性,有助于公共卫生决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一个冬季和五个司法管辖区,但结果得到了应用于现实世界数据的贝叶斯模型的支持。

Improving respiratory virus forecasts by capturing the effects of school holidays, public holidays and seasonal changes in transmission

Authors: Ruarai J Tobin, Oliver Eales, Michael J Plank, Dylan J Morris, James M McCaw, Freya M Shearer, Tobin, R. J., Plank, M. J., Morris, D. J., McCaw, J. M.
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory viruses impose a substantial burden of morbidity and mortality globally, and models that forecast this burden are widely used to support public health decision-making. However, the public health utility of these forecasts can be limited by poor predictive accuracy. In this work, we show how capturing the effects of school holidays, public holidays and seasonal changes in transmission can improve respiratory virus forecasting performance. We construct a Bayesian renewal model where the effective reproduction number R(t) is composed of log-additive components including school and public holiday effects and an annually-periodic Gaussian process. The model also allows for the relative rate of reporting to vary with school and public holidays. We use this model to forecast respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza and SARS-CoV-2 case incidence across a 28-day horizon over five jurisdictions in Australia. Applied to the 2025 winter season, our forecasts outperform a random-walk baseline forecast across all settings. We find that the inclusion of holiday and seasonal transmission effects improved forecasting performance across the three pathogens. We show that the school holidays are associated with population-wide reductions in R(t) on the order of 5-15% but are generally not associated with changes in reporting. Collectively, our results provide support for the inclusion of holiday and seasonal effects in future respiratory virus forecasting models.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viruses impose a substantial burden of morbidity and mortality globally, and models that forecast this burden are widely used to support public health decision-making.
Design and population
The study constructs a Bayesian renewal model to forecast respiratory virus case incidence across five Australian jurisdictions during the 2025 winter season.
Core methods
The model incorporates log-additive components for school holidays, public holidays, and seasonal changes in transmission, and allows for variations in the relative rate of reporting during these periods.
Main results
Our forecasts outperform a random-walk baseline forecast across all settings, and the inclusion of holiday and seasonal effects improved forecasting performance across the three pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The results provide support for the inclusion of holiday and seasonal effects in future respiratory virus forecasting models, highlighting their potential to enhance predictive accuracy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that incorporating holiday and seasonal transmission effects can improve the accuracy of respiratory virus forecasts, aiding public health decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single winter season and five jurisdictions, but the results are supported by a Bayesian model applied to real-world data.

基于脂质体和补体系统的新型均质替代病毒中和测试形式

A New Homogeneous Surrogate Virus Neutralization Test Format Based on Liposomes and the Complement System
作者： Kilian Hoecherl, Jens Konrad, Sebastian Einhauser, Patrick Neckermann, Christina Reiner, Simon Streif, Diana Pauly, Ralf Wagner, Miriam Breunig, Antje J. Baeumner
摘要中文翻译摘要 中和抗体是预防 SARS-CoV-2、流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 等常见病毒感染的关键介质,使其成为评估免疫状态和 疫苗 功效的诊断测定的重要目标。在这里,我们提出了一种新颖的平台技术,以简单的均质免洗形式专门检测 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体,作为替代病毒中和测试 (sVNT)。装载有猝灭荧光团的脂质体用血管紧张素转换酶 2 (ACE2) 受体进行修饰,血管紧张素转换酶 2 (ACE2) 受体是 SARS-CoV-2 进入的主要受体。基于 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白受体结合域 (RBD) 的病毒替代物被开发出来,它与 ACE2 脂质体结合,并含有触发部分,可激活补体系统,导致脂质体裂解。中和抗体抑制这种结合事件,从而防止荧光信号的产生。使用 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体和混合人血清进行了原理验证,生成可与其他检测形式进行比较的 IC50 值。对 20 份 SARS-CoV-2 抗体阴性血清和 24 份来自有 SARS-CoV-2 感染和/或 疫苗接种 病史的个体的血清进行检测,结果特异性为 95%,敏感性为 96%,并且与假病毒中和试验中确定的抗体滴度具有很强的相关性(Spearman r = 0.78,Pearson r = 0.77)。该检测平台可以轻松适应其他目标,为血清学免疫诊断和其他竞争性检测格式提供快速、直接的方法。测定的简单性、避免洗涤和固定步骤使得这种形式非常适合高通量筛选和护理点设置。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种新的替代病毒中和测试(sVNT)形式,用于检测 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体,因为这些抗体对于预防 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 等病毒感染至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究引入了一种新颖的平台技术,旨在使用经过 ACE2 受体修饰的脂质体和激活补体系统的触发部分,以均质免洗形式检测 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体。
核心方法
该方法涉及基于 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白的受体结合域 (RBD) 创建病毒替代物,该蛋白与 ACE2 脂质体结合并触发补体系统激活,导致脂质体裂解。中和抗体抑制这种结合事件,防止荧光信号的产生。
主要结果
对 20 份 SARS-CoV-2 抗体阴性血清和 24 份来自有 SARS-CoV-2 感染和/或 疫苗接种 病史的个体的血清进行的检测显示,特异性为 95%,敏感性为 96%,并且与假病毒中和测试的抗体滴度具有很强的相关性(Spearman r = 0.78,Pearson r = 0.77)。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明了他们的新 sVNT 格式用于检测 SARS-CoV-2 中和抗体的可行性,为现有检测格式提供了一种快速、直接的替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种新的检测形式可以改善 SARS-CoV-2 的血清学免疫诊断,支持 监测、诊断 和 疫苗 功效评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于使用 SARS-CoV-2 特异性样本进行原理验证演示,并且由于缺乏跨不同人群或环境的更广泛验证,证据强度中等。

A New Homogeneous Surrogate Virus Neutralization Test Format Based on Liposomes and the Complement System

Authors: Kilian Hoecherl, Jens Konrad, Sebastian Einhauser, Patrick Neckermann, Christina Reiner, Simon Streif, Diana Pauly, Ralf Wagner, Miriam Breunig, Antje J. Baeumner
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Neutralizing antibodies are key mediators of protection against common viral infections such as SARS-CoV-2, influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), making them an important target for diagnostic assays assessing immune status and vaccine efficacy. Here we present a novel platform technology that specifically detects SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibodies in a simple homogeneous no-wash format as a surrogate virus neutralization test (sVNT). Liposomes loaded with a quenched fluorophore were modified with the angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2) receptor, the main receptor for SARS-CoV-2 entry. Virus surrogates based on the receptor binding domain (RBD) of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein were developed that bind to ACE2-liposomes and contain a trigger moiety, which activates the complement system leading to liposome lysis. Neutralizing antibodies inhibit this binding event and thus prevent the generation of a fluorescent signal. The proof-of-principle was demonstrated using SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibodies and pooled human serum, generating IC50 values that can be compared to other assay formats. Testing of 20 SARS-CoV-2 antibody negative sera and 24 sera from individuals with SARS-CoV-2 infection and/or vaccination history, resulted in a specificity of 95%, sensitivity of 96% and a strong correlation (Spearman r = 0.78, Pearson r = 0.77) with antibody titers determined in a pseudovirus neutralization test. The assay platform can be readily adapted to other targets, offering a rapid and straightforward approach to serological immunodiagnostics and in general other competitive assay formats. The simplicity of the assay, the avoidance of washing and immobilization steps renders this format ideally suited for high-throughput screening and point-of-care settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a new surrogate virus neutralization test (sVNT) format for detecting SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibodies, as these antibodies are critical for protection against viral infections like respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza, and SARS-CoV-2.
Design and population
The study introduced a novel platform technology designed to detect SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibodies in a homogeneous no-wash format, using liposomes modified with the ACE2 receptor and a trigger moiety that activates the complement system.
Core methods
The method involved creating virus surrogates based on the receptor binding domain (RBD) of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein, which bind to ACE2-liposomes and trigger complement system activation, leading to liposome lysis. Neutralizing antibodies inhibit this binding event, preventing fluorescent signal generation.
Main results
Testing of 20 SARS-CoV-2 antibody negative sera and 24 sera from individuals with SARS-CoV-2 infection and/or vaccination history showed a specificity of 95%, sensitivity of 96%, and a strong correlation (Spearman r = 0.78, Pearson r = 0.77) with antibody titers from a pseudovirus neutralization test.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating the feasibility of their new sVNT format for detecting SARS-CoV-2 neutralizing antibodies, offering a rapid and straightforward alternative to existing assay formats.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this new assay format could improve serological immunodiagnostics for SARS-CoV-2, supporting surveillance, diagnosis, and vaccine efficacy assessments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a proof-of-principle demonstration using SARS-CoV-2-specific samples, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of broader validation across diverse populations or settings.

RSM01,一种半衰期延长的 RSV 单克隆抗体,具有高体外耐药屏障

RSM01, an extended half-life RSV monoclonal antibody with a high barrier to in vitro resistance
作者： Jonne Terstappen, Emanuele C. Gustani Buss, Cassandra A. L. Simonich, Jason S. McLellan, Vicky-Lynne Baillie, Jesse D. Bloom, Louis J. Bont, Mauricio T. Caballero, Klasina Chappin, Janissa J. A. van Duijn
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 会导致大量婴儿发病率和 死亡率,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家 (LMIC)。RSM01 是一种针对 LMIC 开发的长效 RSV 单克隆抗体 (mAb)。RSM01 抗性突变体的体外产生此前并未成功。我们系统地评估了 RSM01 的体外耐药屏障以及一组全球当代菌株对 RSM01 的敏感性。单克隆抗体抗性突变体 (MARM) 是通过在 RSM01、尼塞韦单抗 或帕利珠单抗的压力下使用 HEp-2 细胞连续传代三重噬菌斑纯化的 RSV-A2 和 RSV-B1 来产生的,然后进行表型和基因型表征。中度耐药性定义为与亲代病毒相比,IC 50 增加> 3倍,高耐药性定义为IC 50 增加> 30倍。通过中和试验测试了当代全球临床菌株对 RSM01 的敏感性。RSV-A 和 B 株对帕利珠单抗和 尼塞韦单抗 产生高耐药性,而 RSM01 压力则诱导中度耐药。没有 MARM 显示出健身优势。RSM01 中和了全球当代菌株。总之,体外 RSM01 耐药性罕见且中等,并且 RSM01 对循环菌株具有广泛的活性。RSM01 的高耐药屏障的确认令人鼓舞,并且在当前许可的抗 RSV 单克隆抗体广泛使用而出现耐药性时提供了替代方案。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于 RSV 引起的婴儿发病率和 死亡率 较高,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家,该研究旨在评估 RSM01(一种延长半衰期的 RSV 单克隆抗体)的耐药屏障。
设计与对象
该研究在体外评估了 RSM01 对一组全球当代 RSV 菌株的耐药屏障。
核心方法
该研究涉及通过在 RSM01、尼塞韦单抗 或帕利珠单抗的压力下连续传代 RSV 菌株来产生耐药突变体,然后进行表型和基因型表征。
主要结果
RSM01 在 RSV 菌株中诱导中度耐药性,而帕利珠单抗和 尼塞韦单抗 诱导高耐药性。在抗性突变体中没有观察到适应性优势。
解释与创新
该研究证实了 RSM01 的高耐药性,为当前获得许可的抗 RSV 单克隆抗体提供了一种替代方案,以防出现耐药性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSM01 可能是预防 RSV 感染的有价值的替代品,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括耐药性评估的体外性质以及体内可能产生不同结果。

RSM01, an extended half-life RSV monoclonal antibody with a high barrier to in vitro resistance

Authors: Jonne Terstappen, Emanuele C. Gustani Buss, Cassandra A. L. Simonich, Jason S. McLellan, Vicky-Lynne Baillie, Jesse D. Bloom, Louis J. Bont, Mauricio T. Caballero, Klasina Chappin, Janissa J. A. van Duijn
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes substantial infant morbidity and mortality, particularly in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs). RSM01 is a long-acting RSV monoclonal antibody (mAb) in development for LMICs. In vitro generation of RSM01 resistant mutants has previously been unsuccessful. We systematically assessed the in vitro resistance barrier of RSM01 and the susceptibility of a panel of global contemporary strains to RSM01. Monoclonal antibody resistant mutants (MARMs) were generated by serially passaging triple plaque-purified RSV-A2 and RSV-B1 using HEp-2 cells under pressure from RSM01, nirsevimab, or palivizumab, followed by phenotypic and genotypic characterisation. Moderate resistance was defined as > 3-fold and high resistance as > 30-fold increase in IC 50 compared with parental virus. The susceptibility of contemporary global clinical strains to RSM01 was tested in a neutralisation assay. RSV-A and B strains developed high resistance to palivizumab and nirsevimab, while RSM01 pressure induced moderate resistance. No MARMs demonstrated a fitness advantage. RSM01 neutralised global contemporary strains. In conclusion, in vitro RSM01 resistance was infrequent and moderate and RSM01 had broad activity against circulating strains. The confirmation of the high barrier to resistance of RSM01 is encouraging and provides an alternative should resistance emerge with widespread use of current licensed anti-RSV mAbs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the resistance barrier of RSM01, an extended half-life RSV monoclonal antibody, given the substantial infant morbidity and mortality caused by RSV, particularly in low- and middle-income countries.
Design and population
The study evaluated the resistance barrier of RSM01 against a panel of global contemporary RSV strains in vitro.
Core methods
The study involved generating resistant mutants by serially passaging RSV strains under pressure from RSM01, nirsevimab, or palivizumab, followed by phenotypic and genotypic characterisation.
Main results
RSM01 induced moderate resistance in RSV strains, while palivizumab and nirsevimab induced high resistance. No fitness advantage was observed in resistant mutants.
Interpretation and novelty
The study confirmed the high barrier to resistance of RSM01, providing an alternative to current licensed anti-RSV mAbs in case of resistance emergence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSM01 could be a valuable alternative for the prevention of RSV infection, especially in low- and middle-income countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the in vitro nature of the resistance assessment and the potential for different outcomes in vivo.

ROCK2 抑制剂贝卢莫舒地尔治疗慢性移植物抗 宿主 疾病的上市后安全信号:基于 FAERS 数据库的真实世界不成比例分析

Post-marketing safety signals of the ROCK2 inhibitor Belumosudil for chronic graft-versus-host disease: a real-world disproportionality analysis based on the FAERS database
作者： Yulan Zeng, Tao� Zhang, Xueting Wang, Xiyue Tan, Hao Wu, Ruixia Wang, Yuxia Liu, Junguo Duan, Fei Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在利用 FAERS 数据库描述选择性 ROCK2 抑制剂 Belumosudil 在慢性移植物抗 宿主 疾病 (cGVHD) 中的真实上市后安全性特征,满足了解其在临床实践中安全性的需要。
设计与对象
使用 FAERS 数据库中 2021 年第三季度至 2025 年第四季度的不良事件报告进行了现实世界的不成比例分析,重点关注疾病限制性 cGVHD 和造血干细胞移植背景,有 921,529 条记录。
核心方法
不良事件报告从 FAERS 中提取,并通过预先指定的临床方案进行过滤。使用具有错误发现率 (FDR) 验证的四种共识算法(ROR、PRR、BCPNN、MGPS)来识别信号,并使用 Fisher 精确检验评估子组漏洞。
主要结果
在 1,797 份主要可疑贝鲁莫舒地尔报告(6,056 起不良事件)中,初步筛选确定了 61 项共识积极的首选术语 (PT);60 保持 FDR 显着性 (q < 0.05)。经过系统排除后,三层中保留了 45 个核心 PT。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,贝鲁莫舒地尔的安全性主要表现为肌肉骨骼紧张和水肿、粘膜和病毒感染以及转氨酶/免疫球蛋白波动,没有新的全身器官毒性。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了定制临床 监测 的必要性,特别是监测严重疾病中的肺功能衰退、老年接受者的关节僵硬以及女性的肝转氨酶。
局限与证据强度
双重敏感性分析证实了信号的稳健性,但该研究仅限于上市后安全信号,不包括直接的临床结果或因果机制。

Post-marketing safety signals of the ROCK2 inhibitor Belumosudil for chronic graft-versus-host disease: a real-world disproportionality analysis based on the FAERS database

Authors: Yulan Zeng, Tao� Zhang, Xueting Wang, Xiyue Tan, Hao Wu, Ruixia Wang, Yuxia Liu, Junguo Duan, Fei Wang
Original abstract (English)
Objective To characterize the real-world post-marketing safety profile, evidence hierarchy, and demographic vulnerabilities of the selective ROCK2 inhibitor Belumosudil in chronic graft-versus-host disease (cGVHD) using FAERS. Methods Adverse event reports (Q3 2021–Q4 2025) were extracted from FAERS. To control indication confounding, primary disproportionality analysis was anchored to a disease-restricted cGVHD and hematopoietic stem cell transplantation background ( n = 921 , 529 ) using four consensus algorithms (ROR, PRR, BCPNN, MGPS) with False Discovery Rate (FDR) validation. Signals were filtered via a prespecified clinical protocol and stratified into a three-tier evidence framework. Subgroup vulnerabilities (prognostic mortality, age, sex) were evaluated using Fisher’s exact tests. Dual sensitivity analyses evaluated polypharmacy (monotherapy cohort with standardized mean difference [SMD] balance) and denominator stability (whole-database comparator). Results Among 1,797 primary suspect Belumosudil reports (6,056 adverse events), initial screening identified 61 consensus-positive Preferred Terms (PTs); 60 maintained FDR significance ( q < 0.05 ). After systematically excluding 16 confounded administrative and indication-synonymous terms, 45 core PTs were retained across three tiers (Tier 1 trial verification, n = 6 ; Tier 2 novel mechanistic signals, n = 6 ; Tier 3 clinical confounders, n = 33 ). Pharmacological toxicities clustered into three dimensions: musculoskeletal symptoms (muscle tightness, ROR = 10.25; skin swelling, ROR = 19.32), mucosal/viral infections (respiratory syncytial virus, ROR = 4.02; viral gastroenteritis, ROR = 4.81), and laboratory abnormalities (hepatic enzyme increased, ROR = 5.64; blood immunoglobulin G decreased, ROR = 5.75). Subgroup stratification identified distinct clinical vulnerabilities: fatal cases were enriched for obliterative bronchiolitis and muscle atrophy; elderly patients ( ≥ 65 years) exhibited predilection for joint stiffness and lip pain ( P < 0.05 ); and females displayed heightened susceptibility to transaminase elevation, skin atrophy, and oral mucosal blistering ( P < 0.05 ). Monotherapy sensitivity analysis ( N = 1 , 255 ; almost all baseline SMDs < 0.10 ) and whole-database comparison confirmed signal robustness. Conclusion Belumosudil’s real-world safety landscape is dominated by musculoskeletal tightness and edema, mucosal and viral infections, and transaminase/immunoglobulin fluctuations, without novel systemic organ toxicities. Findings underscore the necessity of tailored clinical surveillance: monitoring pulmonary decline in severe disease, joint stiffness in elderly recipients, and hepatic transaminases in females.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the real-world post-marketing safety profile of Belumosudil, a selective ROCK2 inhibitor, in chronic graft-versus-host disease (cGVHD) using the FAERS database, addressing the need for understanding its safety in clinical practice.
Design and population
A real-world disproportionality analysis was conducted using adverse event reports from the FAERS database spanning Q3 2021 to Q4 2025, focusing on a disease-restricted cGVHD and hematopoietic stem cell transplantation background with 921,529 records.
Core methods
Adverse event reports were extracted from FAERS and filtered via a prespecified clinical protocol. Four consensus algorithms (ROR, PRR, BCPNN, MGPS) with False Discovery Rate (FDR) validation were used to identify signals, and subgroup vulnerabilities were evaluated using Fisher’s exact tests.
Main results
Among 1,797 primary suspect Belumosudil reports (6,056 adverse events), initial screening identified 61 consensus-positive Preferred Terms (PTs); 60 maintained FDR significance (q < 0.05). After systematic exclusion, 45 core PTs were retained across three tiers.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that Belumosudil’s safety profile is dominated by musculoskeletal tightness and edema, mucosal and viral infections, and transaminase/immunoglobulin fluctuations, without novel systemic organ toxicities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the necessity of tailored clinical surveillance, particularly for monitoring pulmonary decline in severe disease, joint stiffness in elderly recipients, and hepatic transaminases in females.
Limitations and evidence strength
Dual sensitivity analyses confirmed signal robustness, but the study is limited to post-marketing safety signals and does not include direct clinical outcomes or causal mechanisms.

莆田市19382名住院儿童11种呼吸道病原体流行病学特征

Epidemiological characteristics of 11 respiratory pathogens among 19,382 hospitalized children in Putian city
作者： Yusi Xin, 俞柳敏, Qi Zeng, 林星宇, Youdan Ruan, 林华, Zhanfei Chen
摘要中文翻译目的分析莆田市急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)住院儿童常见呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,为区域小儿呼吸道感染的预防提供科学依据。方法 回顾性分析2023年1月1日至2025年12月30日莆田学院附属医院住院的19 382例儿童的临床资料,采用多重聚合酶链式反应(PCR)结合毛细管电泳片段分析法检测11种常见呼吸道病原菌。结果共纳入住院儿童19382例,呼吸道病原总体阳性率为69.40%。学龄前儿童的阳性率最高,青少年最低。肺炎支原体(Mp)在学龄儿童中检出率最高(38.57%),而人呼吸道合胞病毒(HRSV)在婴幼儿中检出率最高(19.53%)。男性病原体阳性率为70.24%,女性病原体阳性率为68.20%,性别间差异有统计学意义。19382例ARTI儿童中,单一病原检测为主,占58.89%;双病原检测占9.93%,多病原检测占0.57%。呼吸道病原体阳性率呈现季节性波动,峰值出现在2023年冬季(73.98%)、2024年春季(77.85%)和2025年秋季(74.17%)。此外,甲型流感病毒(InfA)、乙型流感病毒(InfB)的阳性率随温度升高而降低,而HRSV、人类冠状病毒(HCOV)的阳性率随温度升高而升高。共记录病原体检测事件15601起;三种最常见的病原体是人鼻病毒(HRV;24.66%)、Mp(23.68%)和HRSV(14.52%)。结论 莆田市ARTIs住院儿童呼吸道病原体总体阳性率较高(69.40%),且具有明显的年龄和季节特征。Mp 在学龄儿童中占主导地位,而 HRSV 在婴儿和幼儿中占主导地位。病原体检测高峰期为2023年秋冬、2024年春季和2025年秋季。HRV、Mp和HRSV是三大主要病原体。双重和多重检测组的中位住院时间为 6 天,而病原体阴性和单一检测组的中位住院时间为 5 天。这些研究结果为优化该地区小儿呼吸道感染临床诊断和治疗提供了科学依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析莆田市急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)住院儿童呼吸道病原菌流行特征,为区域小儿呼吸道感染预防提供科学依据。
设计与对象
对2023年1月1日至2025年12月30日莆田学院附属医院住院的19382名儿童的临床资料进行回顾性分析。
核心方法
采用多重聚合酶链反应(PCR)结合毛细管电泳片段分析检测11种常见呼吸道病原体。
主要结果
共纳入19382名住院儿童,呼吸道病原体总体阳性率为69.40%。学龄前儿童的阳性率最高,青少年最低。肺炎支原体(Mp)在学龄儿童中检出率最高(38.57%),而人呼吸道合胞病毒(HRSV)在婴幼儿中检出率最高(19.53%)。
解释与创新
该研究强调了呼吸道病原体与年龄相关和季节性的独特特征,HRSV 在婴儿和幼儿中的检出率很高。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究结果为优化该地区小儿呼吸道感染临床诊断和治疗提供了科学依据。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性数据,可能会引入偏差,且证据仅限于摘要,导致置信度评估适中。

Epidemiological characteristics of 11 respiratory pathogens among 19,382 hospitalized children in Putian city

Authors: Yusi Xin, 俞柳敏, Qi Zeng, 林星宇, Youdan Ruan, 林华, Zhanfei Chen
Original abstract (English)
Objective To analyze the epidemiological characteristics of common respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs) in Putian city with the aim of providing a scientific basis for regional pediatric respiratory infection prevention. Methods A retrospective analysis was conducted on clinical data from 19,382 children hospitalized at the Affiliated Hospital of Putian University between January 1, 2023, and December 30, 2025. Multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) combined with capillary electrophoresis fragment analysis was employed to detect 11 common respiratory pathogens. Results A total of 19,382 hospitalized children were enrolled, with an overall respiratory pathogen positivity rate of 69.40%. The positivity rate was highest among preschool-aged children and lowest among adolescents. Mycoplasma pneumoniae (Mp) exhibited the highest detection rate in school-aged children (38.57%), while human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) demonstrated the highest detection rate in infants and toddlers (19.53%). The pathogen positivity rate was 70.24% in males and 68.20% in females, with a statistically significant difference between sexes. Among the 19,382 children with ARTIs, single-pathogen detection predominated, accounting for 58.89%; dual-pathogen detection accounted for 9.93%, and multiple-pathogen detection accounted for 0.57%. Respiratory pathogen positivity rates showed seasonal fluctuations, peaking in winter 2023 (73.98%), spring 2024 (77.85%), and autumn 2025 (74.17%). In addition, the positivity rates of influenza A virus (InfA), influenza B virus (InfB) decreased with increasing temperature, whereas those of HRSV, human coronavirus (HCOV) increased with increasing temperature. A total of 15,601 pathogen detection events were recorded; the three most frequently detected pathogens were human rhinovirus (HRV; 24.66%), Mp (23.68%), and HRSV (14.52%). Conclusion The overall positivity rate of respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with ARTIs in Putian was high (69.40%), with distinct age-related and seasonal characteristics. Mp predominated in school-aged children, whereas HRSV predominated in infants and toddlers. Pathogen detection peaked in autumn–winter 2023, spring 2024, and autumn 2025. HRV, Mp, and HRSV were the three dominant pathogens. The dual- and multiple-detection groups had median hospital stays of 6 days, compared with 5 days in the pathogen-negative and single-detection groups. These findings provide a scientific basis for optimizing clinical diagnosis and treatment of pediatric respiratory infections in this region.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze the epidemiological characteristics of respiratory pathogens among children hospitalized with acute respiratory tract infections (ARTIs) in Putian city to provide a scientific basis for regional pediatric respiratory infection prevention.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis was conducted on clinical data from 19,382 children hospitalized at the Affiliated Hospital of Putian University between January 1, 2023, and December 30, 2025.
Core methods
Multiplex polymerase chain reaction (PCR) combined with capillary electrophoresis fragment analysis was used to detect 11 common respiratory pathogens.
Main results
A total of 19,382 hospitalized children were enrolled, with an overall respiratory pathogen positivity rate of 69.40%. The positivity rate was highest among preschool-aged children and lowest among adolescents. Mycoplasma pneumoniae (Mp) exhibited the highest detection rate in school-aged children (38.57%), while human respiratory syncytial virus (HRSV) demonstrated the highest detection rate in infants and toddlers (19.53%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights distinct age-related and seasonal characteristics of respiratory pathogens, with HRSV showing a high detection rate in infants and toddlers.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide a scientific basis for optimizing clinical diagnosis and treatment of pediatric respiratory infections in this region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective data, which may introduce bias, and the evidence is limited to the abstract, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

2025-2026年呼吸道合胞病毒毛细支气管炎新生儿肺炎治疗结果及相关因素分析

KẾT QUẢ ĐIỀU TRỊ VIÊM PHỔI SƠ SINH NHIỄM VIRUS HỢP BÀO HÔ HẤP VÀ MỘT SỐ YẾU TỐ LIÊN QUAN TẠI BỆNH VIỆN NHI TRUNG ƯƠNG
作者： Thảo Đặng Thu, Dat Tran Huu, Nhung Bui Thi Quynh
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病毒感染者hợp bào hô hấp (RSV) gây ra tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương trong giai đoạn 2025-2026。
设计与对象
这项研究是一项描述性纵向研究,对象为202例在2025年10月至2026年7月期间于首都儿科研究所新生儿中心接受住院治疗的RSV阳性新生儿肺炎患儿。
核心方法
Nghiên cứu sử dụng dữ liệu từ các trẻ sơ sinh được chẩn đoán viêm phổi RSV và điều trị tại bệnh viện,bao我建议您使用 SpO2、CRP 和 CRP。
主要结果
98,0% trẻ sơ sinh có triệu chứng giảm/khỏi, 2,0% đỡ chuyển tuyến, 0% tử vong và 53,5% cần hỗ trợ hô hấp. Thời gian nằm viện trung vị là 7,0 ngày. Thời gian hỗ trợ hô hấp tăng dần theo phương thức hỗ trợ hô hấp cao nhất được dùng.
解释与创新
研究结果显示治疗成功率较高,但呼吸支持时间和住院时间取决于疾病的严重程度以及SpO2水平、年龄和合并疾病等因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为RSV所致新生儿肺炎的预后和危险因素提供了重要信息,有助于制定患儿的护理和随访计划。
局限与证据强度
该研究为描述性研究,缺乏更深入的统计数据或具体原因分析,因此证据强度被评估为中等。

KẾT QUẢ ĐIỀU TRỊ VIÊM PHỔI SƠ SINH NHIỄM VIRUS HỢP BÀO HÔ HẤP VÀ MỘT SỐ YẾU TỐ LIÊN QUAN TẠI BỆNH VIỆN NHI TRUNG ƯƠNG

Authors: Thảo Đặng Thu, Dat Tran Huu, Nhung Bui Thi Quynh
Original abstract (English)
Mục tiêu: Nhận xét kết quả điều trị và một số yếu tố liên quan đến viêm phổi sơ sinh nhiễm virus hợp bào hô hấp (Respiratory Syncytial Virus - RSV) tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương năm 2025-2026. Đối tượng và phương pháp: Nghiên cứu mô tả tiến cứu 202 trẻ sơ sinh viêm phổi RSV dương tính, điều trị nội trú tại Trung tâm Sơ sinh, Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương từ 10/2025-7/2026. Kết quả: 98,0% đỡ, giảm/khỏi; 2,0% đỡ chuyển tuyến; 0% tử vong, 53,5% cần hỗ trợ hô hấp. Thời gian nằm viện trung vị 7,0 ngày. Thời gian hỗ trợ hô hấp tăng dần theo phương thức hỗ trợ hô hấp cao nhất được dùng. Yếu tố độc lập liên quan hỗ trợ hô hấp: SpO2 thấp, độ tuổi, thời gian diễn biến bệnh trước nhập viện dài. Yếu tố độc lập liên quan thời gian nằm viện: bệnh kèm theo (tim bẩm sinh), CRP >= 10mg/L, độ tuổi (p < 0,05). Kết luận: Kết quả điều trị khả quan. SpO2 thấp, tuổi nhỏ, tim bẩm sinh và CRP tăng là yếu tố quan trọng trong tiên lượng.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nghiên cứu được thực hiện để đánh giá kết quả điều trị và các yếu tố liên quan đến viêm phổi sơ sinh do virus hợp bào hô hấp (RSV) gây ra tại Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương trong giai đoạn 2025-2026.
Design and population
Đây là một nghiên cứu mô tả tiến cứu được thực hiện trên 202 trẻ sơ sinh mắc viêm phổi RSV dương tính, được điều trị nội trú tại Trung tâm Sơ sinh, Bệnh viện Nhi Trung ương từ tháng 10 năm 2025 đến tháng 7 năm 2026.
Core methods
Nghiên cứu sử dụng dữ liệu từ các trẻ sơ sinh được chẩn đoán viêm phổi RSV và điều trị tại bệnh viện, bao gồm theo dõi thời gian nằm viện, mức độ hỗ trợ hô hấp và các yếu tố liên quan như SpO2, độ tuổi và CRP.
Main results
98,0% trẻ sơ sinh có triệu chứng giảm/khỏi, 2,0% đỡ chuyển tuyến, 0% tử vong và 53,5% cần hỗ trợ hô hấp. Thời gian nằm viện trung vị là 7,0 ngày. Thời gian hỗ trợ hô hấp tăng dần theo phương thức hỗ trợ hô hấp cao nhất được dùng.
Interpretation and novelty
Kết quả cho thấy tỷ lệ điều trị thành công cao, nhưng thời gian hỗ trợ hô hấp và thời gian nằm viện phụ thuộc vào mức độ nghiêm trọng của bệnh và các yếu tố như SpO2, độ tuổi và tình trạng bệnh kèm theo.
Scientific and public-health significance
Kết quả cung cấp thông tin quan trọng về tiên lượng và các yếu tố nguy cơ trong điều trị viêm phổi sơ sinh do RSV, hỗ trợ trong việc lập kế hoạch chăm sóc và theo dõi bệnh nhân.
Limitations and evidence strength
Nghiên cứu mang tính mô tả, không có dữ liệu thống kê sâu hơn hoặc phân tích nguyên nhân cụ thể, do đó mức độ tin cậy được đánh giá là trung bình.

增强比利时的呼吸道病毒 监测:在 2024/2025 年冬季对基于废水的流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2 监测 进行验证

Enhancing respiratory viruses surveillance in Belgium: a validation of the wastewater-based surveillance of influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 in the 2024/2025 winter season
作者： Raphael Janssens, Hadrien Maloux, Anke Meyers, Bavo Verhaegen, Géraldine De Muylder, Laurane De Mot, Sarah Denayer, Nathalie Bossuyt, Natalia Bustos Sierra, Peter Delputte
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过与传统的 监测 方法进行比较,验证基于废水的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的 监测 数据,因为比利时在 2024/2025 年冬季经历了 发病率 流感样疾病的高发期。
设计与对象
The study was conducted in Belgium during the 2024/2025 winter season, involving wastewater samples from 30 treatment plants covering over 4.5 million inhabitants, which represents 38% of the Belgian population.
核心方法
使用四分位法计算基于废水的流行病学阈值,并将数据与来自哨点实验室的传统 监测 数据进行比较,以验证基于废水的方法的有效性。
主要结果
在整个季节中,废水数据与哨点实验室确认的病例之间观察到高度相关性,这表明基于废水的阈值可以有效监测病毒传播。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明基于废水的数据为监测 RSV、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 提供了附加值,提供了大的人口覆盖范围、短的报告时间以及大流行防范的功能。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为针对 RSV、流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的基于废水的 监测 的实施提供参考,从而加强公共卫生准备和对呼吸道病毒 暴发 的应对。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖哨点实验室数据进行比较而受到限制,并且证据有限,因为它不包括详细的统计分析或长期验证。

Enhancing respiratory viruses surveillance in Belgium: a validation of the wastewater-based surveillance of influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 in the 2024/2025 winter season

Authors: Raphael Janssens, Hadrien Maloux, Anke Meyers, Bavo Verhaegen, Géraldine De Muylder, Laurane De Mot, Sarah Denayer, Nathalie Bossuyt, Natalia Bustos Sierra, Peter Delputte
Original abstract (English)
Methods The innovative program monitors over 4.5 million inhabitants, representing 38% of the Belgian population, on a weekly basis by analysing samples from 30 wastewater treatment plants. The present study aims to validate wastewater data on influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 by comparing it with conventional surveillances. Wastewater-based epidemiological thresholds were computed using a quartile method. Results Throughout the entire season, high levels of correlation were observed between wastewater data and cases confirmed by sentinel laboratories. The study adhered to the Declaration of Helsinki.Wastewater data are anonymized as samples included in the present work cover treatment plants with a minimum of 10 000 inhabitant equivalent. Abstract Background During the 2024/2025 respiratory virus season, Belgium recorded the highest incidence of general practitioner visits for influenza-like illness since the COVID-19 pandemic. The pressure on healthcare systems was driven by epidemics of influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), while SARS-CoV-2 activity remained at low levels. This exceptional situation was observed through conventional surveillance as well as via a novel wastewater-based surveillance program integrating influenza, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 measurements. Wastewater-based thresholds effectively monitored viruses circulation. Conclusions Wastewater-based data was assessed to provide information of added value for monitoring SARS-CoV-2 through wastewater compared to conventional surveillance, offering a large and general population coverage, a short and constant reporting time, and a feature for pandemic preparedness. Several perspectives are proposed in this work to increase the quality of RSV and influenza wastewater monitoring. Abbreviations - ARI: -...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to validate wastewater-based surveillance data for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza and SARS-CoV-2 by comparing it with conventional surveillance methods, as Belgium experienced a high incidence of influenza-like illness during the 2024/2025 winter season.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Belgium during the 2024/2025 winter season, involving wastewater samples from 30 treatment plants covering over 4.5 million inhabitants, which represents 38% of the Belgian population.
Core methods
Wastewater-based epidemiological thresholds were computed using a quartile method, and the data were compared with conventional surveillance data from sentinel laboratories to validate the effectiveness of the wastewater-based approach.
Main results
High levels of correlation were observed between wastewater data and cases confirmed by sentinel laboratories throughout the entire season, indicating that wastewater-based thresholds effectively monitored virus circulation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that wastewater-based data provides added value for monitoring RSV, influenza and SARS-CoV-2, offering large population coverage, short reporting time, and a feature for pandemic preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the implementation of wastewater-based surveillance for RSV, influenza and SARS-CoV-2, enhancing public health preparedness and response to respiratory virus outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on sentinel laboratory data for comparison, and the evidence is moderate as it does not include detailed statistical analyses or long-term validation.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

加拿大安大略省不断上升的结核病报告不能用社区 SARS-CoV-2 活动来解释:一项基于人群的时间序列研究

Rising Tuberculosis Presentations in Ontario, Canada Are Not Explained by Community SARS-CoV-2 Activity: A Population-Based Time-Series Study
作者： N. J. Wilson, Tuite, A., C. E. Lee, R. J. Berkowitz, Wiebe, B., Hall, J., Boushy, D., Fisman, D., Wilson, N. J., Lee, C. E.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估大流行期间结核病发病是否增加,以及社区 SARS CoV 2 活动是否会导致分枝杆菌疾病增加。自 2020 年以来,一些高收入国家的结核病通知有所增加,提出的解释包括与大流行相关的 治疗 对潜伏性结核病的破坏、人口构成的变化以及 SARS CoV 2 感染后的免疫功能障碍。
设计与对象
一项基于人群的生态时间序列研究在安大略省大多伦多/汉密尔顿地区扩展区(GTHA+)开展,时间为2011年4月至2024年3月,重点分析了约11.2百万人口中首次结核病(TB)和非结核分枝杆菌(NTM)编码的医院、急诊科和门诊诊所就诊情况。
核心方法
该研究使用负二项式投影和全系列模型来估计三种替代趋势假设下大流行时代的变化。使用分布式滞后非线性模型估计了 0 至 12 周内标准化社区病毒活动的累积率比,敏感性分析将最大滞后期延长至 26 周和 52 周。流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是预先指定的比较物,在初步分析后获得了另外五种病毒暴露的数据,并进行了探索性比较分析。
主要结果
The study identified 3,985 first TB-coded and 5,214 first NTM-coded acute-care presentations. Estimated pandemic-era TB increases ranged from 3% to 22%, while no model suggested an increase in NTM. Community SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with TB (cumulative rate ratio per 1-SD increase over 0 to 12 weeks 0.909, 95% CI 0.822 to 1.006) or NTM (0.953, 0.868 to 1.047). No positive associations were identified in subgroup analyses or with maximum lags of 26 or 52 weeks. Influenza was not associated with either outcome, while RSV was associated with TB and NTM, but only the RSV/NTM association met the Bonferroni threshold among the prespecified comparisons, and no exploratory associations met the experiment wide threshold.
解释与创新
该研究发现,加拿大安大略省的结核病编码急性护理报告在大流行期间略有增加,尽管其估计规模取决于对基本趋势的假设。NTM 编码的演示文稿没有增加。社区 SARS-CoV-2 活动与随后长达一年的结核病或 NTM 表现没有正相关,并且没有为人群水平结核病的增加提供时间解释。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,社区 SARS-CoV-2 活动并不是加拿大安大略省人口水平结核病增加的时间解释。该研究为大流行期间推动结核病发病增加的因素提供了重要见解,并强调需要进一步研究其他潜在的解释。
局限与证据强度
该研究存在局限性,包括对生态时间序列数据的依赖以及可能存在未测量的混杂因素。证据强度中等,因为该研究提供了重要的见解,但不排除个人层面的影响。

Rising Tuberculosis Presentations in Ontario, Canada Are Not Explained by Community SARS-CoV-2 Activity: A Population-Based Time-Series Study

Authors: N. J. Wilson, Tuite, A., C. E. Lee, R. J. Berkowitz, Wiebe, B., Hall, J., Boushy, D., Fisman, D., Wilson, N. J., Lee, C. E.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Tuberculosis (TB) notifications have increased in several high-income countries since 2020. Proposed explanations include pandemic related disruption of treatment for latent tuberculosis, changes in population composition, and immune dysfunction following SARS CoV 2 infection. We assessed whether TB presentations increased during the pandemic era and then tested whether community SARS CoV 2 activity was followed by increased mycobacterial disease. Methods: We conducted a population-based ecological time-series study of first TB- and nontuberculous mycobacterial (NTM)-coded hospital, emergency-department and ambulatory clinic presentations in the Greater Toronto/Hamilton Area Plus (GTHA+), Ontario (2024 population approximately 11.2 million), from April 2011 through March 2024. NTM served as a comparator because it shares diagnostic pathways with TB but differs epidemiologically. Negative binomial projection and full-series models estimated pandemic-era changes under three alternative trend assumptions. Distributed lag non linear models estimated cumulative rate ratios for standardized community viral activity over 0 to 12 weeks; sensitivity analyses extended the maximum lag to 26 and 52 weeks. Influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) were prespecified comparators; data for five additional viral exposures were obtained after the primary analysis was completed and analysed as exploratory comparisons. Results: We identified 3,985 first TB-coded and 5,214 first NTM-coded acute-care presentations. Across six models, estimated pandemic-era TB increases ranged from 3% to 22%. No model suggested an increased in NTM. SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with TB (cumulative rate ratio per 1-SD increase over 0 to 12 weeks 0.909, 95% CI 0.822 to 1.006) or NTM (0.953, 0.868 to 1.047). No positive associations were identified in subgroup analyses or with maximum lags of 26 or 52 weeks. Influenza was not associated with either outcome. RSV was associated with TB and NTM; only the RSV/NTM association met the Bonferroni threshold among the prespecified comparisons, and no exploratory associations met the experiment wide threshold. Conclusions: We identified a modest pandemic era increase in TB-coded acute-care presentations in Ontario, Canada, although its estimated size depended on assumptions about underlying trends. NTM-coded presentations did not increase. Community SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with subsequent TB or NTM presentations at lags of up to one year and did not provide a temporal explanation for the population-level TB increase. Individual-level effects cannot be excluded.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether TB presentations increased during the pandemic era and whether community SARS CoV 2 activity was followed by increased mycobacterial disease. Tuberculosis notifications have increased in several high-income countries since 2020, with proposed explanations including pandemic-related disruption of treatment for latent tuberculosis, changes in population composition, and immune dysfunction following SARS CoV 2 infection.
Design and population
A population-based ecological time-series study was conducted in the Greater Toronto/Hamilton Area Plus (GTHA+), Ontario, from April 2011 through March 2024, focusing on first TB- and nontuberculous mycobacterial (NTM)-coded hospital, emergency-department, and ambulatory clinic presentations in a population of approximately 11.2 million.
Core methods
The study used negative binomial projection and full-series models to estimate pandemic-era changes under three alternative trend assumptions. Cumulative rate ratios for standardized community viral activity over 0 to 12 weeks were estimated using distributed lag non-linear models, with sensitivity analyses extending the maximum lag to 26 and 52 weeks. Influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) were prespecified comparators, and data for five additional viral exposures were obtained after the primary analysis and analysed as exploratory comparisons.
Main results
The study identified 3,985 first TB-coded and 5,214 first NTM-coded acute-care presentations. Estimated pandemic-era TB increases ranged from 3% to 22%, while no model suggested an increase in NTM. Community SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with TB (cumulative rate ratio per 1-SD increase over 0 to 12 weeks 0.909, 95% CI 0.822 to 1.006) or NTM (0.953, 0.868 to 1.047). No positive associations were identified in subgroup analyses or with maximum lags of 26 or 52 weeks. Influenza was not associated with either outcome, while RSV was associated with TB and NTM, but only the RSV/NTM association met the Bonferroni threshold among the prespecified comparisons, and no exploratory associations met the experiment wide threshold.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found a modest pandemic-era increase in TB-coded acute-care presentations in Ontario, Canada, although its estimated size depended on assumptions about underlying trends. NTM-coded presentations did not increase. Community SARS-CoV-2 activity was not positively associated with subsequent TB or NTM presentations at lags of up to one year, and did not provide a temporal explanation for the population-level TB increase.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that community SARS-CoV-2 activity is not a temporal explanation for the population-level TB increase in Ontario, Canada. The study provides important insights into the factors driving the increase in TB presentations during the pandemic era and highlights the need for further research into other potential explanations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study has limitations, including the reliance on ecological time-series data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study provides important insights but does not rule out individual-level effects.

多样化但不趋同:国家儿童呼吸道病原体谱随着多样化而出现分歧,1990-2023 年

Diversification without convergence: national childhood respiratory pathogen spectra diverge as they diversify, 1990-2023
作者： Li, D., Chen, H., Wang, X., Shen, C.
摘要中文翻译背景 几乎所有国家的儿童呼吸道病原体谱系都在多样化——1990 年至 2023 年间,204 个国家中的 203 个国家的国内多样性有所增加——但各国是朝着共同谱系多样化还是沿着不同路径多样化尚不清楚。我们量化了同一时期国家病原体谱的国家间组成距离。方法 我们根据 2023 年全球疾病负担研究下呼吸道感染病因归因(26 个病原体,204 个国家,年龄 0-19 岁)在 1990 年至 2023 年的五个时间点建立了国家病原体份额 媒介。国家间距离通过所有成对的 Jensen-Shannon 散度(JSD;主要)和 Bray-Curtis 差异进行测量,并采用 Baselga 和 Jaccard 分解;稳健性通过指标、病原体小组、低计数阈值和 107 个国家的平衡小组进行了评估。结果 平均成对 JSD 从 1990 年的 0.0084 上升至 2023 年的 0.0283(+238%;趋势 p = 0.030),于 2021 年达到峰值(+283%),并于 2023 年部分回落。Bray-Curtis 差异增加了 +120%,平衡面板增加了 +423%。分歧是完全平衡的变异(份额重新分配),26 种病原体的谱丰富度从 18.5 上升到 21.1。流感(变异系数 0.03 至 0.55)和 呼吸道合胞病毒(变异系数 0.08 至 0.48)的离散度上升最快。每个可计算的 GBD 超级区域(七个中的五个)的区域内距离都增加了:分歧发生在区域内部,而不是集团之间。国家级引导程序证实了这一增长(+238%,95% CI +165 至 +331),分段趋势分析发现没有受支持的断点,分歧以每年 +4.1% 的速度稳定进行(95% CI 2.0-6.3),而不是分阶段进行。结论 随着 疫苗 可预防的主导地位以不同的速度消退,国家频谱正在沿着国家特定轴重新排序。多样化是普遍的,但缺乏趋同性:病因谱水平的转变是异步的和路径依赖的,对经验性的 治疗 政策和病原体 监测 具有影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查国家儿童呼吸道病原体谱系是否朝着共同谱系多样化或沿着不同路径多样化,因为 1990 年至 2023 年间,204 个国家中有 203 个国家的国内多样性有所增加。
设计与对象
该研究利用 2023 年全球疾病负担研究中关于下呼吸道感染病因的数据,分析了 204 个国家和 1990 年至 2023 年五个时间点的国家病原体谱。
核心方法
国家病原体份额 媒介 根据 2023 年全球疾病负担研究数据构建,并使用 Jensen-Shannon 散度和 Bray-Curtis 差异来测量国家之间的组成距离,并评估多个指标和病原体面板的稳健性。
主要结果
平均成对 Jensen-Shannon 散度从 1990 年的 0.0084 增加到 2023 年的 0.0283(+238%;趋势 p = 0.030),并于 2021 年达到峰值(+283%),并在 2023 年部分回调。26 种病原体的谱丰富度从 18.5 上升至 21.1。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明国家谱系正在沿着国家特定轴重新排序,疫苗 可预防的主导地位以不同的速度消退,表明病因谱水平上存在异步且路径依赖的转变。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果对 治疗 经验政策和病原体 监测 具有影响,强调需要采取量身定制的方法来解决各国呼吸道病原体谱系的不同多样化问题。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅基于抽象数据,并且分歧的增加得到了国家级引导程序的证实,由于缺乏详细的统计程序和数据中的潜在偏差,可信度适中。

Diversification without convergence: national childhood respiratory pathogen spectra diverge as they diversify, 1990-2023

Authors: Li, D., Chen, H., Wang, X., Shen, C.
Original abstract (English)
Background National childhood respiratory pathogen spectra are diversifying nearly everywhere - within-country diversity rose in 203 of 204 countries between 1990 and 2023 - yet whether countries are diversifying toward a common spectrum or along divergent paths is unknown. We quantified between-country compositional distance of national pathogen spectra over the same period. Methods We built national pathogen share vectors from Global Burden of Disease Study 2023 lower respiratory infection etiologic attributions (26 pathogens, 204 countries, ages 0-19 years) at five timepoints spanning 1990-2023. Between-country distance was measured as all pairwise Jensen-Shannon divergences (JSD; primary) and Bray-Curtis dissimilarities, with Baselga and Jaccard decompositions; robustness was assessed across metrics, pathogen panels, low-count thresholds and a balanced panel of 107 countries. Results Mean pairwise JSD rose from 0.0084 in 1990 to 0.0283 in 2023 (+238%; trend p = 0.030), peaking in 2021 (+283%) with a partial 2023 pullback. Bray-Curtis dissimilarity rose +120% and the balanced panel +423%. Divergence was entirely balanced variation (share reallocation), with spectrum richness rising from 18.5 to 21.1 of 26 pathogens. Dispersion rose fastest for influenza (coefficient of variation 0.03 to 0.55) and respiratory syncytial virus (0.08 to 0.48). Within-region distance rose in every computable GBD super-region (five of seven): divergence occurs within regions, not between blocs. A country-level bootstrap confirmed the increase (+238%, 95% CI +165 to +331), and segmented trend analysis found no supported breakpoint, with divergence proceeding steadily at +4.1% per year (95% CI 2.0-6.3) rather than in stages. Conclusions National spectra are re-sorting along country-specific axes as vaccine-preventable dominance recedes at different speeds. Diversification is universal, but convergence is absent: the transition at the etiologic-spectrum level is asynchronous and path-dependent, with implications for empirical treatment policy and pathogen surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether national childhood respiratory pathogen spectra are diversifying toward a common spectrum or along divergent paths, as within-country diversity has risen in 203 of 204 countries between 1990 and 2023.
Design and population
The study analyzed national pathogen spectra across 204 countries and five timepoints spanning 1990-2023, using data from the Global Burden of Disease Study 2023 on lower respiratory infection etiologic attributions.
Core methods
National pathogen share vectors were constructed from the Global Burden of Disease Study 2023 data, and between-country compositional distance was measured using Jensen-Shannon divergences and Bray-Curtis dissimilarities, with robustness assessed across multiple metrics and pathogen panels.
Main results
The mean pairwise Jensen-Shannon divergence increased from 0.0084 in 1990 to 0.0283 in 2023 (+238%; trend p = 0.030), peaking in 2021 (+283%) with a partial 2023 pullback. Spectrum richness rose from 18.5 to 21.1 of 26 pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that national spectra are re-sorting along country-specific axes, with vaccine-preventable dominance receding at different speeds, indicating an asynchronous and path-dependent transition at the etiologic-spectrum level.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for empirical treatment policy and pathogen surveillance, highlighting the need for tailored approaches to address the divergent diversification of respiratory pathogen spectra across countries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on abstract-only data, and the increase in divergence was confirmed by a country-level bootstrap, with a moderate confidence level due to the lack of detailed statistical procedures and potential biases in the data.

Journal: Infectious diseases of poverty  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-14  |  DOI: 10.1186/s40249-026-01520-y  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 15, 1, 117

呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 在上市后环境中的成本效益:系统评价和荟萃分析。

Cost-effectiveness of respiratory syncytial virus vaccines in post-marketing settings: a systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Mengyao Mu, Yang Deng, Zhanwei Du, Yi Zhao
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性下呼吸道感染、住院和死亡的主要原因,特别是在全世界的婴儿和老年人中。本研究旨在通过汇总市售 RSV 疫苗 的增量净效益 (INB) 估计值来总结成本效益证据。方法 在这篇综述中,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 20 日的 PubMed、Embase、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆。还检索了灰色文献和参考文献列表。分别对老年人和婴儿进行随机效应荟萃分析。使用特定人群的亚组分析和单变量元回归探讨了异质性的潜在来源。结果 在系统评价纳入的 17 项研究中,11 项参与了主要荟萃分析,另外一项研究仅参与了敏感性分析。在老年人中,汇总 INB 为 105.05 美元 [95% 置信区间 (CI):41.89–168.21 美元;I 2 = 93.2%]。对于婴儿,汇总 INB 为 5.81 美元(95% CI:USD − 35.44–47.07;I 2 = 89.4%)。在欧洲亚组中,老年人的汇总 INB 为 123.63 美元(95% CI:USD 41.23–206.02;I 2 = 89.4%),而婴儿的 INB 为 15.00 美元(95% CI:USD − 4.33–34.34;I 2 = 43.4%)。留一法敏感性分析显示,老年人中的汇总 INB 很稳健,而婴儿的估计值对研究排除很敏感。结论 在纳入研究中使用的阈值下,RSV 疫苗接种 对于上市后的老年人来说可能具有成本效益,而孕产妇 疫苗接种 的证据仍不确定。描述性研究结果表明,针对老年人和季节性母亲 疫苗接种 基于年龄和风险的目标可能会产生更高的 INB 估计值。研究间的显着异质性需要对汇总估计值进行谨慎解释,并强调在制定 RSV 流行病学 政策时需要考虑当地 流行病学 和实施条件。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是导致急性下呼吸道感染、住院和死亡的主要原因,特别是在全世界的婴儿和老年人中。本研究旨在通过汇总市售 RSV 疫苗 的增量净效益 (INB) 估计值来总结成本效益证据。
证据基础与方法
在这篇综述中,检索了截至 2025 年 11 月 20 日的 PubMed、Embase、Web of Science 和 Cochrane 图书馆。还检索了灰色文献和参考文献列表。分别对老年人和婴儿进行随机效应荟萃分析。使用特定人群的亚组分析和单变量元回归探讨了异质性的潜在来源。
共识与结论
在系统评价纳入的 17 项研究中,11 项参与了主要荟萃分析,另外一项研究仅参与了敏感性分析。在老年人中,汇总 INB 为 105.05 美元 [95% 置信区间 (CI):41.89–168.21 美元;I2 = 93.2%]。对于婴儿,汇总 INB 为 5.81 美元(95% CI:-35.44-47.07 美元;I2 = 89.4%)。在欧洲亚组中,老年人的汇总 INB 为 123.63 美元(95% CI:41.23-206.02 美元;I2 = 89.4%),而婴儿的 INB 为 15.00 美元(95% CI:-4.33-34.34 美元;I2 = 43.4%)。
争议与缺口
留一法敏感性分析显示,老年人中的汇总 INB 很稳健,而婴儿的估计值对研究排除很敏感。研究间的显着异质性需要对汇总估计值进行谨慎解释,并强调在制定 RSV 流行病学 政策时需要考虑当地 流行病学 和实施条件。
科研与实践启示
描述性研究结果表明,针对老年人和季节性母亲 疫苗接种 基于年龄和风险的目标可能会产生更高的 INB 估计值。

Cost-effectiveness of respiratory syncytial virus vaccines in post-marketing settings: a systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Mengyao Mu, Yang Deng, Zhanwei Du, Yi Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory infections, hospitalizations, and deaths, particularly among infants and older adults worldwide. This study aims to summarize cost-effectiveness evidence by pooling incremental net benefit (INB) estimates of marketed RSV vaccines. Methods In this review, PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched through November 20, 2025. Grey literature and reference lists were also searched. Random-effects meta-analyses were conducted separately for older adults and infants. Potential sources of heterogeneity were explored using population-specific subgroup analyses and univariable meta-regressions. Results Of the 17 studies included in the systematic review, 11 contributed to the primary meta-analysis and one additional study to sensitivity analysis only. Among older adults, the pooled INB was USD 105.05 [95% confidence interval ( CI ): USD 41.89–168.21; I 2 = 93.2%]. For infants, the pooled INB was USD 5.81 (95% CI: USD − 35.44–47.07; I 2 = 89.4%). In the European subgroup, the pooled INB among older adults was USD 123.63 (95% CI: USD 41.23–206.02; I 2 = 89.4%), while that for infants was USD 15.00 (95% CI: USD − 4.33–34.34; I 2 = 43.4%). Leave-one-out sensitivity analyses showed robust pooled INBs among older adults, whereas infant estimates were sensitive to study exclusion. Conclusions At the thresholds used in the included studies, RSV vaccination might be cost-effective for older adults in post-marketing settings, while the evidence for maternal vaccination remained inconclusive. Descriptive findings suggested that age- and risk-based targeting in older adults and seasonal maternal vaccination may yield higher INB estimates. Substantial between-study heterogeneity warrants cautious interpretation of the pooled estimates and highlights the need to consider local epidemiology and implementation conditions when developing RSV vaccination policies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory infections, hospitalizations, and deaths, particularly among infants and older adults worldwide. This study aims to summarize cost-effectiveness evidence by pooling incremental net benefit (INB) estimates of marketed RSV vaccines.
Evidence base and method
In this review, PubMed, Embase, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched through November 20, 2025. Grey literature and reference lists were also searched. Random-effects meta-analyses were conducted separately for older adults and infants. Potential sources of heterogeneity were explored using population-specific subgroup analyses and univariable meta-regressions.
Consensus and conclusions
Of the 17 studies included in the systematic review, 11 contributed to the primary meta-analysis and one additional study to sensitivity analysis only. Among older adults, the pooled INB was USD 105.05 [95% confidence interval (CI): USD 41.89–168.21; I2 = 93.2%]. For infants, the pooled INB was USD 5.81 (95% CI: USD −35.44–47.07; I2 = 89.4%). In the European subgroup, the pooled INB among older adults was USD 123.63 (95% CI: USD 41.23–206.02; I2 = 89.4%), while that for infants was USD 15.00 (95% CI: USD −4.33–34.34; I2 = 43.4%).
Controversies and gaps
Leave-one-out sensitivity analyses showed robust pooled INBs among older adults, whereas infant estimates were sensitive to study exclusion. Research and practice implications
Descriptive findings suggested that age- and risk-based targeting in older adults and seasonal maternal vaccination may yield higher INB estimates.

超越哮吼:基于表型的儿童副流感病毒类型视角。

Beyond Croup: A Phenotype-Based Perspective on Parainfluenza Virus Types in Children.
作者： Andrea Jaramillo-Cerezo, Andrea Parra-Buitrago, Rolando Ulloa-Gutierrez, Iván Felipe Gutiérrez-Tobar, Rolando Ulloa‐Gutiérrez, Rolando Ulloa-Gutiérrez, I. Gutierrez-Tobar
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是人类副流感病毒 (PIV) 及其在引起儿童呼吸道感染中的作用,重点是 PIV 类型的独特临床和流行病学特征。核心问题是 PIV 是否应被视为单一疾病实体,还是对儿科健康具有独特影响的单独病原体。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对现有文献的叙述性综合,没有明确提及系统搜索方法、数据库或荟萃分析。它提供了 PIV 的一般背景及其临床相关性,但没有报告研究选择标准或纳入的研究数量。
共识与结论
四种 PIV 类型在分类、向性、临床综合征和严重程度方面存在显着差异。报告强调,PIV-1 是哮吼最常见的病因,PIV-2 可在患有基础疾病的儿童中引起严重疾病,PIV-3 与 毛细支气管炎 和小婴儿肺炎相关,PIV-4 与下呼吸道疾病和偶尔的神经系统并发症相关。
争议与缺口
该评论指出,由于病毒培养技术的限制,PIV-4 在历史上一直未被充分认识。它还确定了在理解全谱临床表现以及 免疫应答 在不同 PIV 类型的不同疾病严重程度中的作用方面的差距。
科研与实践启示
该综述强调需要改进诊断工具,例如多重分子诊断,以更好地识别 PIV 类型并指导临床管理。它还表明制定有针对性的预防和治疗策略的重要性,因为目前没有 疫苗 或 抗病毒药物 获得用于 PIV 感染的许可。

Beyond Croup: A Phenotype-Based Perspective on Parainfluenza Virus Types in Children.

Authors: Andrea Jaramillo-Cerezo, Andrea Parra-Buitrago, Rolando Ulloa-Gutierrez, Iván Felipe Gutiérrez-Tobar, Rolando Ulloa‐Gutiérrez, Rolando Ulloa-Gutiérrez, I. Gutierrez-Tobar
Original abstract (English)
Human parainfluenza viruses (PIVs) caused an estimated 18.8 million acute lower respiratory infection episodes, 725 000 hospitalizations, and 34 400 deaths among children under 5 in 2018, ranking second only to respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Despite this burden, clinical attention has historically focused on croup, leading to the common perception that parainfluenza is a single disease entity. However, the 4 types differ substantially in taxonomy, tropism, clinical syndrome, and severity. PIV-1, the most common cause of croup, drives biennial epidemics in toddlers, whereas PIV-2 is less frequent but can cause severe disease, particularly in children with underlying conditions. PIV-3 predominates in young infants, causing bronchiolitis and pneumonia clinically like RSV infection, reflecting its early postnatal susceptibility, which may relate to earlier and more frequent viral exposure. PIV-4, historically underrecognized due to culture limitations, usually does not cause croup but is linked to lower respiratory tract disease, hypoxemia, and occasional neurologic complications. PIV infections are most severe in infants, immunocompromised children, and those in low-resource settings. Seasonal patterns vary by type and geography. Advances in multiplex molecular diagnostics now allow rapid type-specific detection, improving clinical interpretation and management. No vaccine or antiviral is licensed. Emerging therapeutics include sialidase-based antivirals with Food and Drug Administration Breakthrough designation; monoclonal antibodies capable of cross-neutralizing PIV-1, PIV-3, RSV, and human metapneumovirus; and messenger RNA vaccines showing early immunogenicity in children. Recognizing the distinct epidemiology, clinical features, and immune responses of each type is critical for informed pediatric care and the development of targeted preventive and therapeutic strategies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on human parainfluenza viruses (PIVs) and their role in causing respiratory infections in children, with an emphasis on the distinct clinical and epidemiological characteristics of PIV types. The central question is whether PIVs should be considered as a single disease entity or as separate pathogens with unique impacts on pediatric health.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature, with no explicit mention of systematic search methods, databases, or meta-analysis. It provides general background on PIVs and their clinical relevance, but does not report on study selection criteria or the number of studies included.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that the four PIV types differ significantly in taxonomy, tropism, clinical syndrome, and severity. It highlights that PIV-1 is the most common cause of croup, PIV-2 can cause severe disease in children with underlying conditions, PIV-3 is associated with bronchiolitis and pneumonia in young infants, and PIV-4 is linked to lower respiratory tract disease and occasional neurologic complications.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that PIV-4 has been historically underrecognized due to limitations in viral culture techniques. It also identifies gaps in understanding the full spectrum of clinical manifestations and the role of immune responses in varying disease severity across PIV types.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for improved diagnostic tools, such as multiplex molecular diagnostics, to better identify PIV types and guide clinical management. It also suggests the importance of developing targeted preventive and therapeutic strategies, as no vaccine or antiviral is currently licensed for PIV infections.
