成人中添加佐剂的 RSVPreF3 的 疫苗 概况:预防 RSV 相关的下呼吸道疾病以及潜在心血管和呼吸系统益处的新证据。

Vaccine profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in adults: prevention of RSV-related lower respiratory tract disease and emerging evidence of potential cardiovascular and respiratory benefits.
作者： Nancy Dezutter, Katherine Theiss-Nyland, Marie-Pierre David, Quentin Deraedt, Christian Felter, Maria João Fonseca, Susan I. Gerber, Emily K. Horn, Veronica Hulstrøm, Philip Joosten
摘要中文翻译简介:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人、患有某些慢性疾病的人和免疫功能低下患者下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD) 和住院的重要原因。RSV 可导致急性和长期的心血管和呼吸系统疾病,以及身体机能和生活质量下降。覆盖领域:佐剂RSVPreF3是第一个获得许可用于预防成人RSV-LRTD的RSV 疫苗,于2023年在美国和欧洲获得初步批准。可靠的临床试验数据显示了可接受的安全性,并证明了对 RSV-LRTD 的有效性,探索性事后分析表明 RSV 相关的慢性阻塞性肺病 (COPD)/哮喘急性加重的情况有所减少。现实世界的证据证实了这些发现,这些证据显示其对 RSV 相关住院、慢性阻塞性肺病/哮喘恶化和心血管事件的有效性。专家意见:这一新出现的证据支持佐剂RSVPreF3除了单独预防RSV-LRTD之外还能发挥更广泛的作用,以保护心血管和呼吸系统健康。RSV 疫苗接种 应与其他成人免疫接种和健康干预措施一起纳入针对老年人和慢性病患者的综合常规护理和预防管理策略中。解决辅助 RSVPreF3 的实施障碍并强调其在维持健康和独立性方面的作用对于实现广泛采用并帮助保护患者健康至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是老年人、患有某些慢性疾病的人和免疫功能低下患者下呼吸道疾病 (LRTD) 和住院的重要原因。该综述重点关注成人中佐剂 RSVPreF3 的 疫苗 概况,特别是其对 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病的预防以及潜在心血管和呼吸系统益处的新证据。
证据基础与方法
该审查基于临床试验数据和真实世界证据。可靠的临床试验数据显示了可接受的安全性,并证明了对 RSV-LRTD 的有效性,探索性事后分析表明 RSV 相关的慢性阻塞性肺病 (COPD)/哮喘急性加重的情况有所减少。现实世界的证据证实了这些发现,这些证据显示其对 RSV 相关住院、慢性阻塞性肺病/哮喘恶化和心血管事件的有效性。
共识与结论
佐剂 RSVPreF3 是第一个获得许可用于预防成人 RSV-LRTD 的 RSV 疫苗,于 2023 年在美国和欧洲获得初步批准。疫苗 已显示出针对 RSV-LRTD 的功效,并有新的证据表明其具有潜在的心血管和呼吸系统益处,包括减少 COPD/哮喘恶化以及针对 RSV 相关住院和心血管事件的有效性。
争议与缺口
该综述指出,虽然临床试验数据支持辅助 RSVPreF3 对抗 RSV-LRTD 的安全性和有效性,但探索性事后分析表明 COPD/哮喘恶化和心血管事件的减少尚不确定。这些更广泛益处的证据仍在不断涌现,需要进一步验证。
科研与实践启示
RSV 疫苗接种 应与其他成人免疫接种和健康干预措施一起纳入针对老年人和慢性病患者的综合常规护理和预防管理策略中。解决辅助 RSVPreF3 的实施障碍并强调其在维持健康和独立性方面的作用对于实现广泛采用并帮助保护患者健康至关重要。

Vaccine profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in adults: prevention of RSV-related lower respiratory tract disease and emerging evidence of potential cardiovascular and respiratory benefits.

Authors: Nancy Dezutter, Katherine Theiss-Nyland, Marie-Pierre David, Quentin Deraedt, Christian Felter, Maria João Fonseca, Susan I. Gerber, Emily K. Horn, Veronica Hulstrøm, Philip Joosten
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is an important cause of lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD) and hospitalization in older adults, individuals with certain chronic medical conditions, and immunocompromised patients. RSV can result in acute and longer-term cardiovascular and respiratory morbidity, and declines in physical functioning and quality of life. AREAS COVERED: Adjuvanted RSVPreF3 was the first RSV vaccine licensed for preventing RSV-LRTD in adults, with initial approvals in 2023 in the United States and Europe. Robust clinical trial data showed an acceptable safety profile and demonstrated efficacy against RSV-LRTD, with exploratory post-hoc analyses suggesting reductions in RSV-related chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD)/asthma exacerbations. These findings are reinforced by real-world evidence showing effectiveness against RSV-related hospitalizations, COPD/asthma exacerbations, and cardiovascular events. EXPERT OPINION: This emerging evidence supports a broader role of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 beyond preventing RSV-LRTD alone to preserve cardiovascular and respiratory health. RSV vaccination should be considered within an integrated routine care and preventive management strategy for older adults and individuals with chronic conditions, alongside other adult immunizations and health interventions. Addressing implementation barriers for adjuvanted RSVPreF3 and emphasizing its role in maintaining health and independence will be critical to enable widespread uptake and help protect patient health.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is an important cause of lower respiratory tract disease (LRTD) and hospitalization in older adults, individuals with certain chronic medical conditions, and immunocompromised patients. The review focuses on the vaccine profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 in adults, specifically its prevention of RSV-related lower respiratory tract disease and emerging evidence of potential cardiovascular and respiratory benefits.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on clinical trial data and real-world evidence. Robust clinical trial data showed an acceptable safety profile and demonstrated efficacy against RSV-LRTD, with exploratory post-hoc analyses suggesting reductions in RSV-related chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD)/asthma exacerbations. These findings are reinforced by real-world evidence showing effectiveness against RSV-related hospitalizations, COPD/asthma exacerbations, and cardiovascular events.
Consensus and conclusions
Adjuvanted RSVPreF3 was the first RSV vaccine licensed for preventing RSV-LRTD in adults, with initial approvals in 2023 in the United States and Europe. The vaccine has shown efficacy against RSV-LRTD and has emerging evidence suggesting potential cardiovascular and respiratory benefits, including reductions in COPD/asthma exacerbations and effectiveness against RSV-related hospitalizations and cardiovascular events.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while the clinical trial data supports the safety and efficacy of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 against RSV-LRTD, the exploratory post-hoc analyses suggesting reductions in COPD/asthma exacerbations and cardiovascular events are not definitive. The evidence for these broader benefits is still emerging and requires further validation.
Research and practice implications
RSV vaccination should be considered within an integrated routine care and preventive management strategy for older adults and individuals with chronic conditions, alongside other adult immunizations and health interventions. Addressing implementation barriers for adjuvanted RSVPreF3 and emphasizing its role in maintaining health and independence will be critical to enable widespread uptake and help protect patient health.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

成人 RSV 疫苗 政策评估中的“曾经接种过疫苗”的库存流量偏差:方法上的谨慎和基于流量的补救措施。

The "ever-vaccinated" stock-flow bias in adult RSV vaccine policy evaluation: A methodological caution and a flow-based remedy.
作者： Weiping Deng, Ling-Yuan Chen
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
成人 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 建议作为单剂量,无需常规重新接种;因此,累计“接种过疫苗”的覆盖率是一个每个季节都会机械上升的存量变量。
证据基础与方法
先前的指导暗示了这种库存流量的区别,但没有研究提供便携式、与仪器无关的补救措施或量化 2025-26 年季节的这种分解。
共识与结论
Using weekly 2025–26 U.S. National Immunization Survey data, we decomposed cumulative coverage into a prior-season stock and current-season flow, approximating the stock by the season-opening estimate. As of the latest available 2025–26 data (week ending 2026–02-21; mid-season snapshot), 84.9% of coverage among adults 75+ (43.2%) and 82.9% among high-risk adults 50–74 (32.8%) was attributable to prior-season stock – only 15–17% reflected current-season behavior; this stock share is an empirical approximation, not an exact decomposition.
争议与缺口
在两个探索性区域级准实验中,流量指标没有显示出持续的吸收加速(所有主要测试 p ≥ .19;短暂的季初差距,事后 p = .008,没有持续存在);这些动力不足的分析仅具有说明性,并未确定政策效果。作为季中快照,这些数字是季末价值的上限;报道主要是前季库存的结论在整个季节都适用。
科研与实践启示
RSV 政策评估应预先指定当季流量作为主要结果,并报告库存份额作为强烈建议的诊断。

The "ever-vaccinated" stock-flow bias in adult RSV vaccine policy evaluation: A methodological caution and a flow-based remedy.

Authors: Weiping Deng, Ling-Yuan Chen
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Adult respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccines are recommended as a single dose without routine revaccination; cumulative “ever-vaccinated” coverage is therefore a stock variable rising mechanically each season. Prior guidance implies this stock–flow distinction, yet no study has provided a portable, instrument-agnostic remedy or quantified this decomposition for the 2025–26 season. Using weekly 2025–26 U.S. National Immunization Survey data, we decomposed cumulative coverage into a prior-season stock and current-season flow, approximating the stock by the season-opening estimate. As of the latest available 2025–26 data (week ending 2026–02-21; mid-season snapshot), 84.9% of coverage among adults 75+ (43.2%) and 82.9% among high-risk adults 50–74 (32.8%) was attributable to prior-season stock – only 15–17% reflected current-season behavior; this stock share is an empirical approximation, not an exact decomposition. In two exploratory region-level quasi-experiments, the flow metric showed no sustained uptake acceleration (all primary tests p ≥ .19; a transient early-season gap, p = .008 post hoc, did not persist); these underpowered analyses are illustrative only, not identified for the policy effect. To our knowledge, this is the first portable flow-metric operationalization for survey and registry data, released as open code. Registry data exploratorily show the stock metric can obscure equity monitoring (Gini 0.210→0.202; range 39.7→43.5 pp; not significant). As a mid-season snapshot, these figures are an upper bound on the end-of-season value; the conclusion that coverage is dominated by prior-season stock holds through the season. RSV policy evaluation should pre-specify current-season flow as the primary outcome and report the stock share as a strongly recommended diagnostic.
View review five-element analysis

Adult respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccines are recommended as a single dose without routine revaccination; cumulative “ever-vaccinated” coverage is therefore a stock variable rising mechanically each season.

Controversies and gaps
In two exploratory region-level quasi-experiments, the flow metric showed no sustained uptake acceleration (all primary tests p ≥ .19; a transient early-season gap, p = .008 post hoc, did not persist); these underpowered analyses are illustrative only, not identified for the policy effect. RSV policy evaluation should pre-specify current-season flow as the primary outcome and report the stock share as a strongly recommended diagnostic.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

大型集会中的呼吸道感染:以朝觐和副朝为纵向案例研究的旅行医学回顾。

Respiratory Infections at Mass Gatherings: A Travel-Medicine Review with Hajj and Umrah as a Longitudinal Case Study.
作者： Ziad A Memish, Robert Sreffen
摘要中文翻译背景:大型集会将国际流动性与密集、反复接触结合在一起,可能会加剧旅行走廊中的呼吸道感染。朝觐、副朝和大壶节提供了独特的循环案例研究,而其他宗教或体育大型活动以及音乐或文化节则提供了对比鲜明的曝光系统。方法:对同行评审的研究、系统性和范围界定的评审以及权威的公共卫生指导进行重点叙述性评审。搜索和项目证据综合优先考虑 2021 年 1 月至 2026 年 8 月的出版物,并选择基础来源。我们检查了呼吸道症状、实验室检测、临床疾病、疫苗接种、抗菌药物的使用和耐药性、监测、环境调节剂以及旅行前、旅行期间和旅行后的实践。没有进行荟萃分析或正式的偏倚风险评分。结果:呼吸风险不仅仅取决于出勤率,还取决于接触强度、持续时间、通风、发声、共用住宿、旅行者易感性和病原体循环。最近的朝觐证据证实了呼吸道症状和临床咨询的巨大负担,但估计有所不同,因为分母范围从志愿者和测试样本到医疗保健遭遇。鼻病毒、流感、SARS-CoV-2、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和细菌性肺炎仍然很重要。主要针对病毒性综合症的抗生素持续滥用,耐药微生物的获得将个人护理与全球抗菌素耐药性联系起来。综合征、实验室、基于事件、基因组和废水 监测 是互补的,不可互换。高温、灰尘、空气质量、疲劳和防护基础设施的差异会改变呼吸道症状、严重程度和寻求护理的情况。比较事件共享机制,但不直接转移速率。结论:旅行医学实践应将群众集会视为涵盖准备、运输、活动和返回的纵向暴露系统。分层的 疫苗接种、基于风险的掩蔽、通风和人群流动措施、及时诊断、抗菌药物管理和明确的旅行史,可以减少呼吸道疾病发病率,同时保持分母纪律和保护性基础设施的使用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
大型集会将国际流动性与密集、反复的接触结合在一起,可能会加剧旅行走廊中的呼吸道感染。呼吸风险不仅仅取决于出勤率,还取决于接触强度、持续时间、通风、发声、共用住宿、旅行者易感性和病原体循环。
证据基础与方法
对同行评审研究、系统性和范围界定审查以及权威的公共卫生指导进行了重点叙述性审查。搜索和项目证据综合优先考虑 2021 年 1 月至 2026 年 8 月的出版物,并选择基础来源。没有进行荟萃分析或正式的偏倚风险评分。
共识与结论
最近的朝觐证据证实了呼吸道症状和临床咨询的巨大负担,但估计有所不同,因为分母范围从志愿者和测试样本到医疗保健遭遇。鼻病毒、流感、SARS-CoV-2、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和细菌性肺炎仍然很重要。
争议与缺口
高温、灰尘、空气质量、疲劳和防护基础设施的差异会改变呼吸道症状、严重程度和寻求护理的情况。比较事件共享机制,但不直接转移速率。
科研与实践启示
综合征、实验室、基于事件、基因组和废水 监测 是互补的,不可互换。分层的 疫苗接种、基于风险的掩蔽、通风和人群流动措施、及时诊断、抗菌药物管理和明确的旅行史,可以减少呼吸道疾病发病率,同时保持分母纪律和保护性基础设施的使用。

Respiratory Infections at Mass Gatherings: A Travel-Medicine Review with Hajj and Umrah as a Longitudinal Case Study.

Authors: Ziad A Memish, Robert Sreffen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Mass gatherings combine international mobility with dense, repeated contact and can amplify respiratory infections across travel corridors. Hajj, Umrah and Kumbh Mela, offer a uniquely recurrent case study, while other religious or sporting mega-events, and music or cultural festivals provide contrasting exposure systems. METHODS: A focused narrative review was conducted of peer-reviewed studies, systematic and scoping reviews, and authoritative public-health guidance. Searches and project evidence synthesis prioritized publications from January 2021 to August 2026, with selected foundational sources. We examined respiratory symptoms, laboratory detections, clinical disease, vaccination, antimicrobial use and resistance, surveillance, environmental modifiers, and pre-, during-, and post-travel practice. No meta-analysis or formal risk-of-bias scoring was undertaken. RESULTS: Respiratory risk is determined less by attendance alone than by contact intensity, duration, ventilation, vocalization, shared accommodation, traveler susceptibility, and pathogen circulation. Recent Hajj evidence confirms a substantial burden of respiratory symptoms and clinical consultations, but estimates vary because denominators range from volunteers and tested samples to healthcare encounters. Rhinovirus, influenza, SARS-CoV-2, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and bacterial pneumonia remain important. There is continued abuse of antibiotics for predominantly viral syndromes and acquisition of resistant organisms link individual care to global antimicrobial resistance. Syndromic, laboratory, event-based, genomic, and wastewater surveillance are complementary, not interchangeable. Heat, dust, air quality, fatigue, and differential access to protective infrastructure modify respiratory symptoms, severity, and care seeking. Comparative events share mechanisms but not directly transferable rates. CONCLUSIONS: Travel-medicine practice should treat mass gatherings as longitudinal exposure systems spanning preparation, transport, the event, and return. Layered vaccination, risk-based masking, ventilation and crowd-flow measures, prompt diagnostics, antimicrobial stewardship, and explicit travel histories, can reduce respiratory morbidity while preserving denominator discipline and access to protective infrastructure.
View review five-element analysis

病毒感染和神经营养素信号传导失调:对中枢神经系统的潜在影响。

Viral Infections and Dysregulation of Neurotrophin Signaling: Potential impact in the Central Nervous System.
作者： Mariya Timotey Miteva, Virginia Protto, Francesco Zanzi, Maria Candida Cesta, Marcello Allegretti, Giovanna De Chiara
摘要中文翻译病毒感染会扰乱中枢神经系统中的神经营养素信号传导,对神经元存活、突触可塑性、神经发育以及长期认知和神经精神结果产生重要影响。神经营养蛋白,包括神经生长因子、脑源性神经营养因子、神经营养蛋白-3 和神经营养蛋白-4,主要通过原肌球蛋白受体激酶(TrkA、TrkB 和 TrkC)和 p75 神经营养蛋白受体 (p75NTR) 介导其作用,激活细胞内通路,例如 PI3K-AKT、MAPK-ERK 和 PLC-γ。越来越多的证据表明,嗜神经/神经侵袭性病毒和非经典嗜神经病毒都可以通过多种机制干扰这些途径,包括直接感染神经和胶质细胞引起的机制、干扰持续 Trk 信号传导所需的内体运输以及炎症驱动的神经营养蛋白表达和受体平衡的改变。这篇综述总结了有关选定病毒如何影响实验模型和临床环境中神经营养蛋白可用性和下游信号传导的当前知识。具体而言,该审查重点关注:1)能够建立终生潜伏或持续感染的病毒,例如i)疱疹病毒(单纯疱疹病毒-1;Epstein Barr病毒;人类巨细胞病毒)和ii)人类免疫缺陷病毒;2)其他导致特殊神经病理学特征的嗜神经病毒,例如西尼罗河病毒、日本脑炎病毒、寨卡病毒和博尔纳病病毒1;3)呼吸道病毒,例如甲型流感病毒、人类呼吸道合胞病毒和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2。对于每个病毒组,我们讨论了趋同的致病机制和受体水平对神经营养蛋白信号传导的干扰。我们进一步强调了当前知识中的一些差距,包括有限的纵向人类数据和病毒共同感染和再激活的作用尚未充分探索,以及神经营养蛋白失调的因果方向性尚不清楚。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 如何影响中枢神经系统中的神经营养素信号传导,重点关注其对神经元存活、突触可塑性、神经发育以及长期认知和神经精神结果的潜在影响。
证据基础与方法
该综述对包括 RSV 在内的选定病毒及其对神经营养蛋白信号通路的影响进行了系统分析。它讨论了每个病毒组的趋同致病机制以及受体水平对神经营养蛋白信号传导的干扰。
共识与结论
越来越多的证据表明,RSV 可以通过多种机制干扰神经营养蛋白信号传导,包括直接感染神经细胞和神经胶质细胞、干扰内体运输以及炎症驱动的神经营养蛋白表达和受体平衡的改变。
争议与缺口
该综述强调了当前知识的差距,例如有限的纵向人类数据和病毒共同感染和重新激活的作用尚未得到充分探索,以及神经营养蛋白失调的因果方向性尚不清楚。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,需要进一步研究 RSV 对神经营养蛋白信号传导的长期影响、合并感染和再激活的作用,以及病毒感染和神经营养蛋白失调之间的因果关系。

Viral Infections and Dysregulation of Neurotrophin Signaling: Potential impact in the Central Nervous System.

Authors: Mariya Timotey Miteva, Virginia Protto, Francesco Zanzi, Maria Candida Cesta, Marcello Allegretti, Giovanna De Chiara
Original abstract (English)
Viral infections can perturb neurotrophin signaling in the central nervous system, with important consequences for neuronal survival, synaptic plasticity, neurodevelopment, and long-term cognitive and neuropsychiatric outcomes. Neurotrophins, including nerve growth factor, brain-derived neurotrophic factor, neurotrophin-3, and neurotrophin-4, mediate their actions primarily through tropomyosin receptor kinases (TrkA, TrkB and TrkC) and the p75 neurotrophin receptor (p75NTR), activating intracellular pathways such as PI3K-AKT, MAPK-ERK, and PLC-γ. Growing evidence suggests that both neurotropic/neuroinvasive and non-classically neurotropic viruses can interfere with these pathways through multiple mechanisms, including those elicited by direct infection of neural and glial cells, interference with endosomal trafficking required for sustained Trk signaling, and inflammation-driven alterations in neurotrophin expression and receptor balance. This review summarizes current knowledge on how selected viruses affect neurotrophin availability and downstream signaling in experimental models and clinical settings. Specifically, the review focuses on: 1) viruses capable of establishing life-long latent or persistent infection such as i) herpesviruses (herpes simplex virus-1; Epstein Barr virus; human cytomegalovirus), and ii) human immunodeficiency virus; 2) other neurotropic viruses driving peculiar neuropathological profiles, such West Nile virus, Japanese encephalitis virus, Zika virus, and Borna disease virus 1; 3) respiratory viruses, such as influenza A virus, human respiratory syncytial virus, and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2. For each virus group, we discuss convergent pathogenic mechanisms and receptor-level interference with neurotrophin signaling. We further highlight some gaps in current knowledge, including limited longitudinal human data and the underexplored role of viral co-infections and reactivations, and the unclear causal directionality of neurotrophin dysregulation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review explores how respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) affects neurotrophin signaling in the central nervous system, focusing on its potential impact on neuronal survival, synaptic plasticity, neurodevelopment, and long-term cognitive and neuropsychiatric outcomes.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of selected viruses, including RSV, and their effects on neurotrophin signaling pathways. It discusses convergent pathogenic mechanisms and receptor-level interference with neurotrophin signaling for each virus group.
Consensus and conclusions
There is growing evidence that RSV can interfere with neurotrophin signaling through multiple mechanisms, including direct infection of neural and glial cells, interference with endosomal trafficking, and inflammation-driven alterations in neurotrophin expression and receptor balance.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights gaps in current knowledge, such as limited longitudinal human data and the underexplored role of viral co-infections and reactivations, as well as the unclear causal directionality of neurotrophin dysregulation.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests the need for further research on the long-term effects of RSV on neurotrophin signaling, the role of co-infections and reactivations, and the causal relationships between viral infections and neurotrophin dysregulation.

用于传染病的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 疫苗:作用机制和转化进展

mRNA-Lipid Nanoparticle Vaccines for Infectious Diseases: Mechanisms of Action and Translational Progress
作者： Zhao Y, Cheng H, Zhou S, He L, Xiu C, Huang B, Lu X
摘要中文翻译在首次获得紧急使用授权六年后,mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗 已从一个没有许可产品的平台转变为供应全球大部分 COVID-19 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 剂量的平台,并已完成季节性流感的 3 期试验。它们的性能取决于在个体细胞类型、受体和分子相互作用水平上以异常速度解决的机制,并且在此过程中必须修改一些教科书的假设。本次审查遵循 疫苗 从配方瓶到生发中心的整个过程。我们首先描述了如何设计 RNA 以实现有效翻译并部分逃离胞质和内体 RNA 传感器,以及为什么 N1-甲基假尿苷既不是免疫沉默也不是翻译中性。然后,我们研究可电离脂质、辅助磷脂、胆固醇和 PEG-脂质如何决定颗粒行为、细胞摄取和内体释放,以及脂质和 RNA 成分如何充当可分离的佐剂信号。比较了来自小鼠模型和人类淋巴结采样的证据,并注意先天细胞因子控制方面的巨大物种差异,这限制了从小鼠到人类的外推。临床部分总结了 COVID-19、RSV 和流感 疫苗 的功效和免疫原性数据,以及针对巨细胞病毒、mpox、HIV、疟疾、尼帕病毒和艰难梭菌的候选药物的临床前和早期临床状态。安全数据以绝对术语而不是单独作为信号来讨论。我们以目前阻碍合理改进的问题作为结束语:脂质传感器的分子特性、将免疫原性与反应原性分开的困难、缺乏内体逃逸的定量描述以及不耐受单一固定免疫原的病原体的抗原设计。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的 mRNA-脂质纳米颗粒 (mRNA-LNP) 疫苗,包括其作用机制、转化进展和临床应用。核心问题是这些 疫苗 如何发挥作用以及它们在预防 RSV 感染方面更广泛应用的潜力。
证据基础与方法
该审查基于小鼠模型和人类淋巴结采样的证据。它讨论了 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 的性能,强调在将小鼠的研究结果外推到人类时,需要考虑先天细胞因子控制的物种差异。该综述还概述了临床部分,总结了 RSV 疫苗 的功效和免疫原性数据,并包括候选药物针对其他病原体的临床前和早期临床状态。
共识与结论
mRNA-LNP 疫苗 已从一个没有许可产品的平台转变为广泛用于 RSV 的平台,并已完成季节性流感的 3 期试验。它强调了了解 疫苗 性能的分子机制的重要性,特别是脂质和 RNA 成分在颗粒行为、细胞摄取和内体释放中的作用。
争议与缺口
该综述指出了一些争议和证据差距,包括脂质传感器的分子身份、区分免疫原性和反应原性的困难、缺乏内体逃逸的定量描述,以及为不耐受单一固定免疫原的病原体设计抗原的挑战。
科研与实践启示
该综述表明,未来的研究应集中于解决脂质传感器的分子身份、改善免疫原性与反应原性的分离、量化内体逃逸机制以及开发不耐受单一固定免疫原的病原体的抗原。这些努力对于mRNA-LNP 疫苗的合理改进至关重要。

mRNA-Lipid Nanoparticle Vaccines for Infectious Diseases: Mechanisms of Action and Translational Progress

Authors: Zhao Y, Cheng H, Zhou S, He L, Xiu C, Huang B, Lu X
Original abstract (English)
Six years after the first emergency use authorizations, mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccines have gone from a platform without a licensed product to one that supplies a large share of the world's COVID-19 and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccine doses and has completed phase 3 trials for seasonal influenza. Their performance depends on mechanisms that are being resolved with unusual speed at the level of individual cell types, receptors and molecular interactions, and several textbook assumptions have had to be revised in the process. This review follows the vaccine from the formulation vial to the germinal centre. We first describe how the RNA is engineered for efficient translation and for partial escape from cytosolic and endosomal RNA sensors, and why N1-methylpseudouridine is neither immunologically silent nor translationally neutral. We then examine how the ionizable lipid, the helper phospholipid, cholesterol and the PEG-lipid determine particle behaviour, cell uptake and endosomal release, and how the lipid and RNA components act as separable adjuvant signals. Evidence from mouse models and from human lymph node sampling is compared, with attention to the substantial species differences in innate cytokine control that limit extrapolation from mice to people. The clinical section summarizes efficacy and immunogenicity data for COVID-19, RSV and influenza vaccines, and the preclinical and early clinical status of candidates against cytomegalovirus, mpox, HIV, malaria, Nipah virus and Clostridioides difficile. Safety data are discussed in absolute terms rather than as signals alone. We close with the questions that currently block rational improvement: the molecular identity of the lipid sensor, the difficulty of separating immunogenicity from reactogenicity, the absence of a quantitative description of endosomal escape, and the design of antigens for pathogens that do not tolerate a single fixed immunogen.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on mRNA-lipid nanoparticle (mRNA-LNP) vaccines for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), including their mechanisms of action, translational progress, and clinical applications. The central question is how these vaccines function and their potential for broader use in preventing RSV infections.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on evidence from mouse models and human lymph node sampling. It discusses the performance of mRNA-LNP vaccines, emphasizing the need to account for species differences in innate cytokine control when extrapolating findings from mice to humans. The review also outlines the clinical section that summarizes efficacy and immunogenicity data for RSV vaccines and includes preclinical and early clinical status of candidates against other pathogens.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that mRNA-LNP vaccines have transitioned from a platform without a licensed product to one that is widely used for RSV and has completed phase 3 trials for seasonal influenza. It highlights the importance of understanding the molecular mechanisms of vaccine performance, particularly the role of lipid and RNA components in particle behavior, cell uptake, and endosomal release.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies several controversies and evidence gaps, including the molecular identity of the lipid sensor, the difficulty of separating immunogenicity from reactogenicity, the absence of a quantitative description of endosomal escape, and the challenges in designing antigens for pathogens that do not tolerate a single fixed immunogen.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on resolving the molecular identity of the lipid sensor, improving the separation of immunogenicity from reactogenicity, quantifying endosomal escape mechanisms, and developing antigens for pathogens that do not tolerate a single fixed immunogen. These efforts are critical for the rational improvement of mRNA-LNP vaccines.

当前的老年免疫方法:叙述性迷你评论

Current Approaches to Geriatric Immunizat?on: A Narrative Mini-Review
作者： Ebru Bulut, Emine Tarlabelen Karaytug
摘要中文翻译随着全球人口老龄化,实施“生命全程免疫”方法已成为维持功能能力和确保健康老龄化的关键支柱。目前的 2025-2026 年指南强调向三价流感 疫苗 的战略过渡,以及针对高危老年患者 疫苗 的常规给药。此外,临床方案优先考虑高剂量或佐剂制剂,以克服免疫衰老,并提供针对侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病、带状疱疹和 COVID-19 亚变种的强大保护。尽管取得了这些进步,但 疫苗 的获取和公众意识仍然存在显着差异,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。通过整合这些下一代 疫苗 来加强国家免疫计划对于减轻医疗保健系统的负担并减少可预防的 死亡率 至关重要。最终,向个性化免疫策略的转变旨在确保每个老年人实现最佳的血清保护和更高的生活质量。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 及其对老年人免疫的影响,特别是在针对老年人的 疫苗 开发和管理策略的背景下。核心问题是如何优化 RSV 免疫方法,以改善老龄化人群的健康状况。
证据基础与方法
该评论是一篇叙述性迷你评论,而不是一项系统或荟萃分析研究。它不报告特定的数据库、搜索日期或资格标准。证据来自当前的指南和临床方案,重点是 疫苗 制剂及其在老年 队列研究 中的应用。
共识与结论
目前的指南建议对高危老年人群进行向三价流感 疫苗 的战略过渡,并常规施用新型 RSV 疫苗。临床方案优先考虑高剂量或佐剂制剂,以增强对 RSV 相关疾病(如 毛细支气管炎 和侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病)的保护。
争议与缺口
该审查强调了 疫苗 的获取和公众意识方面的显着差异,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。这些差异代表了一个关键的证据差距,因为它们影响 RSV 免疫策略的公平实施。
科研与实践启示
通过整合下一代 RSV 疫苗 来加强国家免疫计划对于减少可预防的 死亡率 和减轻医疗保健系统的负担至关重要。向个性化免疫策略的转变旨在确保老年人获得最佳的血清保护和生活质量。

Current Approaches to Geriatric Immunizat?on: A Narrative Mini-Review

Authors: Ebru Bulut, Emine Tarlabelen Karaytug
Original abstract (English)
As the global population ages, the implementation of a "life-course immunization" approach has become a critical pillar for maintaining functional capacity and ensuring healthy aging. Current 2025-2026 guidelines emphasize a strategic transition to trivalent influenza vaccines and the routine administration of novel Respiratory Syncytial Virus vaccines for high-risk geriatric cohorts. Furthermore, clinical protocols prioritize high-dose or adjuvanted formulations to overcome immunosenescence and provide robust protection against invasive pneumococcal disease, herpes zoster, and COVID-19 subvariants. Despite these advancements, significant disparities in vaccine access and public awareness persist, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. Strengthening national immunization schedules through the integration of these next-generation vaccines is essential to alleviate the burden on healthcare systems and reduce preventable mortality. Ultimately, the shift toward personalized immunization strategies aims to ensure that every older adult achieves optimal seroprotection and a higher quality of life.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and its impact on geriatric immunization, particularly in the context of vaccine development and administration strategies for older adults. The central question is how to optimize immunization approaches for RSV to improve health outcomes in aging populations.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative mini-review, not a systematic or meta-analytic study. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria. The evidence is derived from current guidelines and clinical protocols, with an emphasis on vaccine formulations and their application in geriatric cohorts.
Consensus and conclusions
Current guidelines recommend a strategic transition to trivalent influenza vaccines and the routine administration of novel RSV vaccines for high-risk geriatric populations. Clinical protocols prioritize high-dose or adjuvanted formulations to enhance protection against RSV-related diseases such as bronchiolitis and invasive pneumococcal disease.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights significant disparities in vaccine access and public awareness, particularly in low- and middle-income countries. These disparities represent a critical evidence gap, as they affect the equitable implementation of RSV immunization strategies.
Research and practice implications
Strengthening national immunization schedules through the integration of next-generation RSV vaccines is essential to reduce preventable mortality and alleviate healthcare system burdens. The shift toward personalized immunization strategies aims to ensure optimal seroprotection and quality of life for older adults.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

2021 年至 2023 年吉达阿卜杜勒阿齐兹国王大学医院实时 PCR 检测到的呼吸道病毒:病毒学、年龄相关和临床特征的回顾性研究

Respiratory viruses detected by real-time PCR at King Abdulaziz University Hospital, Jeddah, 2021 to 2023: a retrospective study of virological, age-related, and clinical characteristics
作者： Saeed M. S. Alhamhhum, Rahaf Talal Alqawas, Khalifa Binkhamis, Soliuman Mohamed, Abdulah OS Bawazeer, Yahya Shabi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究旨在分析 2021 年至 2023 年吉达阿卜杜勒阿齐兹国王大学医院实时 PCR 检测到的呼吸道病毒,重点是人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (hRSV) 和人类鼻病毒/肠道病毒 (hRV/hEV)。核心问题是确定这些检测的病毒学、年龄相关和临床特征。
证据基础与方法
该研究是对 KAUH 的 PCR 阳性事件进行回顾性分析,使用实时 PCR 和多重面板对鼻咽标本进行分析。分析包括精确检验、95% 置信区间 (CI) 的比值比 (OR)、Benjamini-Hochberg 校正和 Firth 逻辑回归。测试分母不可用,该研究包括 143 名患者的 149 次 PCR 阳性事件,但没有特定时期的记录。
共识与结论
人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (hRSV) 和人类鼻病毒/肠道病毒 (hRV/hEV) 是最常检测到的病毒,其中 hRSV 占 40.9%,hRV/hEV 占 34.2%。16.8% 的病例中观察到了共同检测。在已知年龄的病例中,hRSV 主要见于 6 个月以下的婴儿,其中 70.2% 的病例为 hRSV 阳性。然而,经过多次检测校正后,没有发现记录的症状和病毒检测之间存在显着关联。
争议与缺口
该研究指出,在不测试分母的情况下,数据描述的是阳性检测的组成,而不是 发病率 或季节性。此外,对已有病症的分析显示与 hRSV 存在名义上的关联,但在校正后并不显着。该研究还强调,检测地点随着时间的推移而变化,这可能会影响对病毒分布模式的解释。
科研与实践启示
研究结果表明,需要基于分母的 发病率 来更好地理解 发病率 以及 hRSV 和 hRV/hEV 的季节性。他们还提供了引入婴儿 hRSV 免疫之前当地医院的参考数据,这可以为未来的公共卫生策略和 疫苗 的实施提供信息。

Respiratory viruses detected by real-time PCR at King Abdulaziz University Hospital, Jeddah, 2021 to 2023: a retrospective study of virological, age-related, and clinical characteristics

Authors: Saeed M. S. Alhamhhum, Rahaf Talal Alqawas, Khalifa Binkhamis, Soliuman Mohamed, Abdulah OS Bawazeer, Yahya Shabi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Data on respiratory viruses in western Saudi Arabia after the coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) mitigation period are limited. We described the viruses detected, their distribution over time and by age, clinical features, pre-existing conditions, and outcomes in a hospital-based series of polymerase chain reaction (PCR)-positive episodes at King Abdulaziz University Hospital (KAUH), Jeddah. Methods We retrospectively reviewed episodes in which at least one respiratory virus was detected by real-time PCR, primarily with a multiplex panel, in nasopharyngeal specimens tested clinically between January 2021 and December 2023; testing denominators were not available. Analyses used exact tests, odds ratios (OR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI), Benjamini-Hochberg correction, and Firth logistic regression. Results We analyzed 149 PCR-positive episodes in 143 patients; no records were available for December 2021, January to September 2022, or January 2023. Median age was 9.0 months (interquartile range 3.0 to 29.0; unknown for 32 episodes). Human respiratory syncytial virus (hRSV) was detected in 40.9% and human rhinovirus/enterovirus (hRV/hEV) in 34.2% of episodes; 16.8% had co-detections. Among episodes with known age, 70.2% of those in infants younger than 6 months were hRSV-positive (q = 0.005); at least 33 of all 61 hRSV episodes occurred in this age group. hRSV and hRV/hEV proportions differed between years, including within October and November, while testing sites also changed. No association between documented symptoms and virus detection remained significant after correction. Pre-existing conditions were documented in 46.3% of episodes and were nominally less frequent with hRSV (OR 0.31, 95% CI 0.14 to 0.64), but not after correction (q = 0.057). Specimens were collected in an intensive care unit in 22.4% of episodes; 9 of 143 patients (6.3%) had a recorded death at data extraction. Conclusions In this single-center series of PCR-positive episodes, hRSV and hRV/hEV were the most frequently detected viruses, and, among episodes with known age, hRSV was concentrated in young infants, most without documented pre-existing conditions. Without testing denominators, these data describe the composition of positive detections, not incidence or seasonality; they provide local hospital reference data from before infant hRSV immunization, whose impact will require denominator-based surveillance. Clinical trial number Not applicable.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study aimed to analyze respiratory viruses detected by real-time PCR at King Abdulaziz University Hospital, Jeddah, from 2021 to 2023, with a focus on human respiratory syncytial virus (hRSV) and human rhinovirus/enterovirus (hRV/hEV). The central question was to determine the virological, age-related, and clinical characteristics of these detections.
Evidence base and method
The study was a retrospective analysis of PCR-positive episodes at KAUH, using real-time PCR with a multiplex panel on nasopharyngeal specimens. The analyses included exact tests, odds ratios (OR) with 95% confidence intervals (CI), Benjamini-Hochberg correction, and Firth logistic regression. Testing denominators were not available, and the study included 149 PCR-positive episodes in 143 patients, though no records were available for certain periods.
Consensus and conclusions
Human respiratory syncytial virus (hRSV) and human rhinovirus/enterovirus (hRV/hEV) were the most frequently detected viruses, with hRSV accounting for 40.9% and hRV/hEV for 34.2% of episodes. Co-detections were observed in 16.8% of cases. Among episodes with known age, hRSV was predominantly found in infants younger than 6 months, with 70.2% of these cases being hRSV-positive. However, no significant association was found between documented symptoms and virus detection after correction for multiple testing.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that without testing denominators, the data describe the composition of positive detections rather than incidence or seasonality. Additionally, the analysis of pre-existing conditions showed a nominal association with hRSV, but this was not significant after correction. The study also highlights that testing sites changed over time, which may affect the interpretation of viral distribution patterns.
Research and practice implications
The findings suggest the need for denominator-based surveillance to better understand the incidence and seasonality of hRSV and hRV/hEV. They also provide local hospital reference data from before the introduction of infant hRSV immunization, which could inform future public health strategies and vaccine implementation.

作者： Fajia Farhath, Sushil Kumar .k, Arohi Gupta, Saurabh Doifode, Syed Manazir Ali
摘要中文翻译比较 clesrovimab、尼塞韦单抗、帕利珠单抗、母体 RSV 疫苗接种 和其他 RSV 单克隆抗体与安慰剂之间的系统评价和贝叶斯网络荟萃分析方案,以预防 24 个月以下儿童就医的 RSV 下呼吸道感染。追溯注册;参见协议第 1.4 节。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述旨在比较 RSV 免疫预防方案(clesrovimab、尼塞韦单抗、帕利珠单抗、母体 RSV 疫苗接种 和其他 RSV 单克隆抗体)与安慰剂之间的疗效和安全性,以预防 24 个月以下儿童就医的 RSV 下呼吸道感染。目标实体是人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、RSV-A、RSV-B 以及相关亚型,例如 HRSV-A 和 HRSV-B。核心问题是这些干预措施对新生儿、婴儿和幼儿的相对有效性和安全性。

该审查是系统审查和贝叶斯网络荟萃分析的协议。如协议第 1.4 节所示,它是追溯注册的。证据基础包括对干预措施之间以及与安慰剂进行比较的研究,重点是预防 24 个月以下儿童的 RSV 下呼吸道感染。
共识与结论
该审查方案概述了评估 RSV 免疫预防方案的有效性和安全性的方法。然而,所提供的证据中没有提供实质性结论或汇总估计。审查处于规划阶段,尚未报告调查结果。

科研与实践启示
审查方案不提供具体的研究或实践影响。它的重点是建立未来系统评价和网络荟萃分析的方法。

Comparative efficacy and safety of RSV immunoprophylaxis (clesrovimab, nirsevimab, palivizumab and maternal RSV vaccination) in neonates, infants and young children: protocol for a systematic review and network meta-analysis

Authors: Fajia Farhath, Sushil Kumar .k, Arohi Gupta, Saurabh Doifode, Syed Manazir Ali
Original abstract (English)
Protocol for a systematic review and Bayesian network meta-analysis comparing clesrovimab, nirsevimab, palivizumab, maternal RSV vaccination and other RSV monoclonal antibodies against each other and placebo for preventing medically attended RSV lower respiratory tract infection in children under 24 months. Registered retrospectively; see protocol section 1.4.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review aims to compare the efficacy and safety of RSV immunoprophylaxis options (clesrovimab, nirsevimab, palivizumab, maternal RSV vaccination, and other RSV monoclonal antibodies) against each other and placebo for preventing medically attended RSV lower respiratory tract infection in children under 24 months. The target entities are human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), RSV-A, RSV-B, and related subtypes such as HRSV-A and HRSV-B. The central question is about the comparative effectiveness and safety of these interventions in neonates, infants, and young children.
Evidence base and method
The review is a protocol for a systematic review and Bayesian network meta-analysis. It is registered retrospectively, as indicated in the protocol section 1.4. The evidence base includes studies comparing the interventions to each other and to placebo, with a focus on preventing RSV lower respiratory tract infection in children under 24 months.
Consensus and conclusions
The review protocol outlines a method to evaluate the efficacy and safety of RSV immunoprophylaxis options. However, no substantive conclusions or pooled estimates are provided in the supplied evidence. The review is in the planning stage and does not yet report findings.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report any controversies or evidence gaps. The review is a protocol and does not include results or interpretations at this stage.
Research and practice implications
The review protocol does not provide specific research or practice implications. It is focused on establishing the methodology for a future systematic review and network meta-analysis.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

美国研究中 RSV 活性和干扰影响人类偏肺病毒时间

RSV Activity and Interference Shape Human Metapneumovirus Timing in US Study
新闻简报Ke Li及其同事于2026年11月17日发表的一项研究发现,人类偏肺病毒(HMPV)暴发在美国出现的时间是由呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的病毒干扰决定的,而不是由区域气候条件决定的。研究小组分析了美国十个地区的实验室确诊的 HMPV 病例和特定年龄的急诊科就诊情况,发现 RSV 活动直接降低了 HMPV 传播 发病率。纳入气候变量的动态模型未能捕捉到观测到的时空模式,而包含 RSV 相关术语的模型成功地再现了 HMPV 暴发 的两年一次模式,其特征是“晚小”波和“早大”波交替。研究结果表明,RSV 干预措施可能不会显着增加 HMPV 活动,了解这些相互作用可以改善 HMPV 的季节性预测和干预策略。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究发表于 2026 年 11 月 17 日,证据中没有明确说明事件日期。
地点与对象
该研究在美国十个不同地区进行,重点关注 HMPV 的时空动态及其与 RSV 的相互作用。
事件
李柯及其同事进行的一项研究发现,人类偏肺病毒(HMPV)暴发的出现时间受到呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)病毒干扰的影响,而不是受区域气候条件的影响。
规模、影响与风险
该研究分析了美国十个地区实验室确诊的 HMPV 病例和特定年龄的急诊科就诊情况。结果表明,RSV 活动直接降低了 HMPV 传播 率,并且气候驱动模型未捕获 HMPV 暴发 的两年期模式。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是动态 传播 建模分析,研究结果基于包含 RSV 相关项的最佳拟合模型。研究人员得出结论,了解这些动态将为未来的 HMPV 季节性预测和干预策略提供信息。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

RSV Activity and Interference Shape Human Metapneumovirus Timing in US Study

News Brief
A study published on November 17, 2026, by Ke Li and colleagues found that the timing of human metapneumovirus (HMPV) outbreaks in the United States is shaped by viral interference from respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), rather than regional climate conditions. The research team analyzed laboratory-confirmed HMPV cases and age-specific emergency department visits across ten US regions, revealing that RSV activity directly reduces HMPV transmission rates. Dynamic models incorporating climate variables failed to capture the observed spatiotemporal patterns, while models including an RSV-associated term successfully reproduced the biennial patterns of HMPV outbreaks, characterized by alternating 'late-small' and 'early-large' waves. The findings suggest that RSV interventions may not significantly increase HMPV activity, and understanding these interactions could improve seasonal forecasting and intervention strategies for HMPV.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Wednesday, November 18, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health RSV Activity and Interference Shape Human Metapneumovirus Timing in US Study by November 17, 2026 in Biology, Health Reading Time: 2 mins read 0 RSV Activity and Interference Shape Human Metapneumovirus Timing in US Study Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Transmission rates of human metapneumovirus (HMPV) are shaped by viral interference from respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) rather than regional climate conditions, according to an age-stratified dynamic transmission modeling study conducted by Ke Li and colleagues. HMPV causes acute lower respiratory infections that primarily affect young children and older adults, generating seasonal outbreaks that peak annually in March or April across the United States and other temperate regions in the Northern hemisphere. To determine the poorly understood drivers of this seasonality, the research team analyzed laboratory-confirmed HMPV cases alongside age-specific emergency department visits across ten distinct US regions. The researchers tested several candidate transmission models to evaluate whether environmental factors or viral interference governed observed spatiotemporal patterns. Dynamic models incorporating climate variables into the transmission rate, including vapor pressure, precipitation, potential evapotranspiration, and minimum temperature, could not capture the timing of HMPV activity across all ten regions. In contrast, the timing of HMPV outbreaks was consistently associated with RSV activity, demonstrating that the HMPV transmission rate is directly reduced in the presence of circulating RSV. This viral interference proved essential for explaining regional variations in infection cycles. Among the candidate models analyzed, only those incorporating an RSV-associated term successfully reproduced the biennial patterns of HMPV documented in certain regions. These multi-year cycles are characterized by alternating epidemic waves classified by the authors as “late-small” and “early-large” outbreaks, a dynamic that purely climate-driven formulations failed to replicate. Using the best-fit model containing the RSV-associated term, the investigators predicted how future RSV interventions might influence HMPV spread. Although RSV interventions reduce viral competition between the two pathogens, model forecasts indicate that such measures are unlikely to result in a substantial increase in HMPV activity. The authors concluded that defining these spatiotemporal dynamics and pathogen interactions will help inform future seasonal forecasting and intervention strategies for HMPV. 鼻内 Poly I:C 佐剂增强 呼吸道合胞病毒 疫苗 对小鼠的保护 - Bioengineer.org

Intranasal Poly I:C Adjuvant Enhances Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Protection in Mice - Bioengineer.org
新闻简报Bioengineer.org 于 2026 年 11 月 16 日发表的一项临床前研究调查了使用鼻内 Poly I:C 佐剂增强 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 对小鼠的保护。该研究在雌性 BALB/c 小鼠中进行,针对阳性对照佐剂和阴性对照模拟免疫,评估了三种不同剂量的 Poly I:C。研究发现,鼻内制剂可提供实质性保护并改善粘膜免疫力,但没有报告具体病例数、死亡人数或住院人数。研究结果表明,直接在病毒进入门户产生主动的前线免疫屏障可能是下一代 RSV 疫苗 的关键目标。该研究发表在《Frontiers in Medicine》上,涉及对照试验,以评估佐剂与工程化预融合 F 抗原相结合的剂量依赖性免疫学特征和保护功效。该研究强调了粘膜 疫苗接种 策略在解决当前胃肠外 RSV 疫苗 的局限性方面的潜力,该策略主要引发全身体液抗体,而不是直接粘膜保护。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于 2026 年 11 月 16 日发表,实验设计涉及第 0 天和第 21 天的两次 疫苗接种,挑战发生在加强剂后 14 天。
地点与对象
该研究是在六至八周龄的雌性 BALB/c 小鼠上进行的,其中有五只实验 队列研究,每只六只动物,研究结果与人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染相关。
事件
据 Bioengineer.org 报道,一项临床前研究评估了鼻内 Poly I:C 佐剂与预输注 F 抗原结合使用以增强 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 对小鼠的保护。
规模、影响与风险
该研究表明,鼻内制剂为小鼠提供了实质性保护并增强了粘膜免疫,尽管所提供的证据中没有报告具体病例数、死亡数或住院人数。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是一项临床前研究,研究结果基于小鼠实验结果。该研究发表在《医学前沿》上,方法包括对照试验和免疫学评估。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Intranasal Poly I:C Adjuvant Enhances Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Protection in Mice - Bioengineer.org

News Brief
A preclinical study published on November 16, 2026, by Bioengineer.org investigated the use of an intranasal Poly I:C adjuvant to enhance respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccine protection in mice. The research, conducted in female BALB/c mice, evaluated three distinct dosages of Poly I:C against a positive control adjuvant and negative control mock immunizations. The study found that the intranasal formulation conferred substantial protection and improved mucosal immunity, though specific case counts, death counts, or hospitalization numbers were not reported. The findings suggest that generating an active front-line immunological barrier directly at the portal of viral entry could be a key objective for next-generation RSV vaccines. The research was published in Frontiers in Medicine and involved controlled trials to assess the dose-dependent immunological profile and protective efficacy of the adjuvant combined with an engineered prefusion F antigen. The study highlights the potential of mucosal vaccination strategies to address the limitations of current parenteral RSV vaccines, which primarily elicit systemic humoral antibodies rather than direct mucosal protection.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Wednesday, November 18, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Health Intranasal Poly I:C Adjuvant Enhances Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Protection in Mice by November 16, 2026 in Health Reading Time: 6 mins read 0 Intranasal Poly I:C Adjuvant Enhances Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Protection in Mice Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram A preclinical study evaluating mucosal vaccination strategies against human respiratory syncytial virus demonstrated that formulating an intranasal prefusion F candidate with fifty micrograms of the double-stranded RNA analogue Poly I:C conferred substantial protection and enhanced mucosal immunity in mice. The investigation, conducted in female BALB/c mice and published in Frontiers in Medicine, compared three distinct dosages of Poly I:C against a positive control adjuvant and negative control mock immunizations. Human respiratory syncytial virus remains a primary viral pathogen responsible for severe acute lower respiratory tract infections such as bronchiolitis and pneumonia, disproportionately impacting young infants, immunocompromised individuals, and older adults. Worldwide epidemiologic surveillance from 2019 estimated that thirty-three million episodes of associated lower respiratory tract illness occurred in children under five years of age, causing roughly 3.6 million hospital admissions and 101,400 fatalities, alongside 5.2 million infections, 470,000 hospitalizations, and 33,000 in-hospital deaths among older adults in high-income nations. Although active prophylaxis advanced substantially with regulatory approvals of prefusion F recombinant protein vaccines such as Arexvy and Abrysvo alongside the messenger RNA platform mRESVIA, existing formulations are administered through intramuscular injection. These parenteral vaccines predominantly elicit circulating systemic humoral antibodies rather than direct mucosal protection. Because the virus initiates primary infection and transmission across respiratory tract mucosal surfaces, generating an active front-line immunological barrier directly at the portal of viral entry represents an important objective for next-generation vaccine designs. Secretory immunoglobulin A within respiratory secretions can mediate immediate viral neutralization and immune exclusion during initial exposure, thereby preventing downstream dissemination into lower pulmonary structures. However, non-replicating recombinant subunit proteins administered through intranasal routes frequently demonstrate weak intrinsic immunogenicity when applied without an immunostimulatory mucosal adjuvant capable of triggering local pattern recognition receptors. To overcome mucosal hyporesponsiveness, researchers evaluate synthetic agonists of pattern recognition receptors that mimic pathogen-associated molecular patterns, such as synthetic single-stranded CpG oligodeoxynucleotides that stimulate Toll-like receptor 9 and synthetic double-stranded RNA Poly I:C that targets Toll-like receptor 3. While CpG promotes robust T-helper-1 polarization and murine immunoglobulin G2a production, Poly I:C stimulates type I interferons, activating cytotoxic T lymphocytes and mucosal responses without necessarily following a linear dose-response trajectory. Given that excessive local innate stimulation can induce tissue damage, and prior studies revealed a restricted therapeutic dose window for CpG in which fifty micrograms worsened lung histopathology without boosting efficacy, the authors designed a controlled trial to assess the dose-dependent immunological profile and protective efficacy of Poly I:C combined with engineered prefusion F antigen. Discover more vaccines Find Vaccines vaccine Vaccine Vaccines The experimental antigen consisted of a stabilized recombinant prefusion F protein derived from human respiratory syncytial virus subtype A, expressed in Chinese hamster ovary cells. To retain the metastable prefusion architecture, investigators substituted residues 98 through 144—encompassing both furin cleavage sites, the intervening p27 peptide, and a portion of the hydrophobic fusion peptide—with a flexible twelve-amino-acid glycine-serine linker. The native transmembrane and cytoplasmic domains were excised and substituted with a bacteriophage T4 fibritin foldon trimerization domain at the carboxy terminus. Additionally, six specific stabilizing point mutations were engineered into the sequence: serine-155-cysteine, serine-190-phenylalanine, valine-207-leucine, serine-290-cysteine, aspartate-486-cysteine, and aspartate-489-cysteine. Female BALB/c mice aged six to eight weeks were divided into five experimental cohorts of six animals each: an unadjuvanted phosphate-buffered saline control, a reference group receiving fifteen micrograms of protein with ten micrograms of C-type CpG, and three test groups receiving fifteen micrograms of protein with ten, twenty-five, or fifty micrograms of Poly I:C. Vaccines were administered intranasally in a total volume of fifty microliters across two distinct immunizations delivered on day zero and day twenty-one. Blood samples were gathered thirteen days after the secondary vaccination to assess circulating humoral binding and neutralizing activity. Serum binding immunoglobulin G, immunoglobulin G1, and immunoglobulin G2a titers were quantified using enzyme-linked immunosorbent assays against immobilized prefusion F protein, with endpoint half-maximal effective dilution values determined through four-parameter non-linear curve fitting. Functional neutralization was quantified using plaque reduction neutralization tests in HEp-2 cell monolayers challenged with human respiratory syncytial virus Long strain, applying the monoclonal antibody palivizumab and TrueBlue peroxidase substrate for plaque visualization, followed by half-maximal reduction titer calculation via the Kärber method. Discover more Study Biology Check Symptoms Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine Circulating antibody analyses revealed that all vaccinated animals mounted antigen-specific humoral responses, though systemic binding titers did not increase monotonically with Poly I:C dosage. The reference CpG group exhibited the highest total binding immunoglobulin G geometric mean titer at 42,781. Among the Poly I:C cohorts, the twenty-five-microgram dose generated the highest binding geometric mean titer at 22,158, whereas the ten-microgram and fifty-microgram doses produced comparable titers of 13,353 and 12,855, respectively. In contrast, functional neutralizing antibody titers demonstrated a clear upward numerical trend with increasing Poly I:C dose, yielding geometric mean titers of 262 in the ten-microgram cohort, 340 in the twenty-five-microgram cohort, and 1,173 in the fifty-microgram cohort, though differences among the three Poly I:C tiers did not achieve formal statistical significance. The ten-microgram CpG control group produced the highest overall neutralizing geometric mean titer at 1,802. Isotype differentiation showed marked divergences in helper T-cell polarization patterns between the two adjuvant classes. Serum from mice receiving CpG demonstrated elevated levels of both immunoglobulin G1 and immunoglobulin G2a, resulting in a significantly elevated G2a-to-G1 ratio indicative of a pronounced Th1-dominant humoral bias. Conversely, all Poly I:C groups produced higher concentrations of immunoglobulin G1 relative to immunoglobulin G2a, generating lower G2a-to-G1 ratios. Enzyme-linked immunospot assays performed on splenocytes stimulated ex vivo with 500 nanograms of purified prefusion F protein revealed that cellular responses did not mirror serum isotype ratios directly. The frequency of interferon-gamma-secreting splenocytes was significantly higher across all Poly I:C groups compared with CpG and mock groups, with the fifty-microgram Poly I:C dose stimulating the highest overall frequency. Splenocyte secretion of interleukin-2 and interleukin-4 showed variable patterns without linear dose correlation, whereas interleukin-5-secreting lymphocytes peaked prominently in the ten-microgram Poly I:C group and declined at higher adjuvant amounts. Fourteen days after their booster vaccination, all animals were anesthetized with isoflurane and challenged intranasally with five hundred thousand plaque-forming units of a mouse-adapted human respiratory syncytial virus Long strain. Mock-vaccinated controls demonstrated progressive clinical wasting, with body weight declining continuously to reach a nadir between post-infection days three and four before initiating slow recovery. In contrast, all four adjuvanted groups exhibited attenuated weight loss, stabilizing body mass within forty-eight hours after challenge and recovering towards baseline. The cohort receiving fifty micrograms of Poly I:C exhibited the most stable post-challenge weight trajectory among all vaccinated mice. Animals were euthanized on day five following challenge for tissue harvest, with the right lung dedicated to molecular viral quantification and mucosal antibody extraction, while the left lung underwent perfusion fixation for blinded histopathological assessment. Local mucosal immunity within lung tissue homogenates was measured by titration against coated prefusion F protein, defining positive binding at an absorbance exceeding 2.1 times negative control baseline. Lung prefusion F-specific immunoglobulin A titers rose alongside the Poly I:C dose, increasing from 1,185 in the ten-microgram cohort to 1,405 in the twenty-five-microgram cohort and 1,838 in the fifty-microgram cohort, approaching the 1,984 geometric mean titer observed with CpG, whereas mock-immunized mice registered an optical baseline titer of 497. Direct absolute quantification of viral replication by digital polymerase chain reaction targeting the viral nucleocapsid gene demonstrated significant viral suppression across all immunized groups relative to controls. Unvaccinated control lungs harbored 766.5 viral RNA copies per milligram of tissue. In comparison, viral burden measured 105.5 copies per milligram in the ten-microgram Poly I:C group, 112.3 copies in the twenty-five-microgram group, and dropped to 30.6 copies per milligram in the fifty-microgram Poly I:C group, representing an approximate twenty-five-fold reduction relative to control animals. The CpG reference group yielded 61.4 copies per milligram. Histopathological evaluation of hematoxylin-and-eosin-stained left lung tissue sections revealed extensive pathological lesions in control mice, distinguished by substantial loss of alveolar structural integrity, pronounced thickening of the alveolar septa, and dense interstitial inflammatory cellular infiltration. Immunized animals demonstrated variable degrees of pulmonary preservation, retaining open alveolar architecture with decreased septal expansion and reduced peribronchial inflammatory infiltrates. Semiquantitative lesion scoring across peribronchial inflammation, alveolar septal thickening, and alveolar interstitial infiltration confirmed lower cumulative injury scores across all vaccinated groups relative to control tissues, although scores did not display a statistically defined stepwise dose-response among the three Poly I:C concentrations. The study authors highlighted several methodological limitations that restrict immediate extrapolation of these findings. First, the experimental design omitted groups receiving unadjuvanted prefusion F protein alone or adjuvant molecules alone, precluding an isolated quantification of baseline protein immunogenicity independent of adjuvant synergy. Second, the evaluation tested three discrete Poly I:C concentrations, which narrows the empirical window necessary to declare a fully optimized clinical dose or establish comprehensive safety margins. Third, while local mucosal immunoglobulin A was quantified through binding assays in lung homogenates, the assay did not isolate or measure the specific functional neutralization potency of those mucosal antibodies. Consequently, the investigators concluded that while fifty micrograms of Poly I:C demonstrated the most favorable balance of neutralizing titer, local mucosal immunoglobulin A, splenic interferon-gamma induction, and post-challenge viral clearance within the tested parameters, its benefits must be validated in broader dose-escalation and functional neutralization studies before advancing to clinical formulation. 呼吸道合胞病毒 在吉达医院领导小婴儿检测

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Led Detections in Young Infants at Jeddah Hospital
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 16 日发表在《病毒学杂志》上的一项研究分析了沙特阿拉伯吉达阿卜杜勒阿齐兹国王大学医院 2021 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月期间检测到的呼吸道病毒。呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是最常见的病原体,占 149 例呼吸道阳性病例中的 40.9%,总共检测到 61 例。人类鼻病毒或肠道病毒是第二常见的病毒,占病例的 34.2%。研究发现,RSV 主要集中在 6 个月以下的婴儿中,该组中 70.2% 的阳性病例是 RSV。然而,经过统计校正后,没有发现症状与特定病原体之间存在显着关联。该数据集存在空白,包括 2021 年 12 月、2022 年 1 月至 9 月和 2023 年 1 月没有记录。作者强调需要基于分母的 监测 来评估未来婴儿 RSV 免疫计划的影响。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究分析了2021年1月至2023年12月期间的呼吸道病毒检测情况,报告于2026年11月16日发布。数据收集期间存在间歇性空白,包括2021年12月、2022年1月至9月和2023年1月没有记录。
地点与对象
该研究是在沙特阿拉伯吉达的阿卜杜勒阿齐兹国王大学医院进行的,重点关注儿科患者,特别是小婴儿。记录年龄的患者中位年龄为 9.0 个月,四分位数范围为 3.0 至 29.0 个月。32 次发作患者年龄未知。
事件
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 被确定为呼吸道疾病发作的主要病原体,在分析的发作中 40.9% 的 RSV 检测呈阳性,即总共 61 次检测。人类鼻病毒或肠道病毒是第二流行的病毒,占发病总数的 34.2%。该研究由克里斯蒂娜·贾维斯报道并发表在《病毒学杂志》上。
规模、影响与风险
RSV 是检测到的主要病毒,在 149 例呼吸道阳性病例中,有 61 例是 RSV。它主要集中在 6 个月以下的婴儿中,该年龄组中 70.2% 的阳性病例是 RSV。143 名患者中有 9 人死亡(6.3%)。研究指出,症状特征并不能可靠地区分特定病原体,并且 46.3% 的发作中记录了既往病史,但统计校正后未发现显着关联。
应对、状态与不确定性
该调查是回顾性的,基于使用多重诊断面板对鼻咽标本进行实时 PCR 检测。由于缺乏检测分母,研究结果仅反映阳性检测的相对组成,而不是整体疾病发病率。作者强调需要强大的 监测 系统来评估未来免疫计划的影响。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Led Detections in Young Infants at Jeddah Hospital

News Brief
A study published in the Virology Journal on November 16, 2026, analyzed respiratory virus detections at King Abdulaziz University Hospital in Jeddah, Saudi Arabia, between January 2021 and December 2023. Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) was the most frequently identified pathogen, accounting for 40.9% of 149 positive respiratory episodes, with 61 total detections. Human rhinovirus or enterovirus was the second most common, making up 34.2% of cases. The study found that RSV was heavily concentrated in infants under 6 months, with 70.2% of positive episodes in this group being RSV. However, no significant association was found between symptoms and specific pathogens after statistical correction. The dataset had gaps, including no records for December 2021, January through September 2022, and January 2023. The authors highlighted the need for denominator-based surveillance to assess the impact of future infant immunization programs against RSV.
Original source text (English)
HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER Wednesday, November 18, 2026 No Result View All Result Login HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER HOME NEWS EXPLORE CAREER Companies Jobs Lecturer PhD Studentship Postdoc Research Assistant EVENTS iGEM News Team PHOTOS VIDEO WIKI BLOG COMMUNITY FACEBOOK INSTAGRAM TWITTER No Result View All Result No Result View All Result Home NEWS Science News Biology Respiratory Syncytial Virus Led Detections in Young Infants at Jeddah Hospital by November 16, 2026 in Biology Reading Time: 4 mins read 0 Respiratory Syncytial Virus Led Detections in Young Infants at Jeddah Hospital Share on FacebookShare on TwitterShare on LinkedinShare on RedditShare on Telegram Human respiratory syncytial virus and human rhinovirus or enterovirus emerged as the most frequently identified pathogens in a retrospective series of 149 positive respiratory episodes analyzed at King Abdulaziz University Hospital in Jeddah, with respiratory syncytial virus heavily concentrated in young infants. The investigation, published in Virology Journal, detailed the virological, age-related, and clinical characteristics of positive episodes detected between January 2021 and December 2023. The study authors highlighted that comprehensive data on respiratory viral infections in western Saudi Arabia remained limited following the coronavirus disease 2019 mitigation period, establishing a clear need to describe hospital-based detections, patient characteristics, and clinical outcomes during this post-mitigation transition. To characterize the circulating pathogens, the researchers retrospectively reviewed clinical episodes in which at least one respiratory virus was confirmed through real-time polymerase chain reaction testing of nasopharyngeal specimens, primarily utilizing a multiplex diagnostic panel. Because testing denominators detailing total negative and overall test counts were unavailable in the clinical records, the investigation evaluated the relative composition and patterns among positive cases. The team applied rigorous statistical methodologies to evaluate the data, incorporating exact tests, odds ratios with corresponding 95 percent confidence intervals, Firth logistic regression modeling, and the Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate correction to adjust for multiple hypothesis testing. The final analyzed cohort encompassed 149 real-time PCR-positive episodes occurring among 143 individual patients, though the dataset was constrained by intermittent surveillance gaps across the three-year evaluation window. Specifically, hospital clinical records were unavailable for December 2021, the nine-month span from January through September 2022, and January 2023. Among the episodes with recorded patient age, the median age was 9.0 months, with an interquartile range spanning from 3.0 to 29.0 months, while patient age was unknown or unrecorded for 32 of the episodes included in the series. Virological evaluations demonstrated that human respiratory syncytial virus was the leading pathogen, identified in 40.9 percent of the analyzed episodes, representing 61 total detections. Human rhinovirus or enterovirus was the second most prevalent viral group, documented in 34.2 percent of the episodes, making these two categories the predominant drivers of positive tests. In addition, viral co-detections involving more than one respiratory pathogen were identified in 16.8 percent of the episodes. The observed proportions of both respiratory syncytial virus and rhinovirus or enterovirus differed substantially across study years, with notable variations occurring even between identical calendar months, such as October and November, while testing sites across the medical facility also shifted over time. Age-specific analyses demonstrated that human respiratory syncytial virus detections were heavily concentrated in the youngest pediatric age brackets. Among episodes with documented patient age, 70.2 percent of all positive episodes in infants younger than 6 months were positive for human respiratory syncytial virus. This striking age concentration maintained statistical significance following Benjamini-Hochberg adjustment, yielding a corrected q-value of 0.005. Furthermore, at least 33 of the 61 total respiratory syncytial virus episodes identified throughout the entire multi-year hospital series occurred within this cohort of infants under 6 months of age. The investigators also conducted detailed evaluations linking viral identity with documented clinical presentations, but observed that symptom profiles did not reliably differentiate specific pathogens. Although a range of clinical features was documented in patient medical records at the time of presentation, no statistical association between documented symptoms and specific virus detections remained statistically significant after multiple-testing correction was applied. Pre-existing medical conditions were documented in 46.3 percent of the positive episodes across the hospital cohort and showed an initial nominal divergence based on viral classification. Specifically, underlying conditions appeared less frequent among patients with human respiratory syncytial virus, yielding an unadjusted odds ratio of 0.31 with a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.14 to 0.64; however, this association did not reach statistical significance after adjustment, concluding with a corrected q-value of 0.057. Markers of acute clinical severity were substantial across the patient series: clinical specimens were collected while patients were admitted to an intensive care unit in 22.4 percent of episodes, and 9 of the 143 patients, representing 6.3 percent, had a recorded death at the time of data extraction. Addressing the broader public health context, the authors underscored that the absence of clinical testing denominators precludes interpreting these findings as measures of overall disease incidence or community seasonality, as the figures characterize only the proportional composition of positive detections. Nonetheless, the authors concluded that this single-center dataset provides vital local hospital reference data from the period immediately preceding infant respiratory syncytial virus immunization. They noted that accurately evaluating the future clinical and epidemiological impact of newly introduced infant immunization programs will require robust, denominator-based surveillance systems. Time
The study analyzed respiratory virus detections between January 2021 and December 2023, with the report published on November 16, 2026. The data collection period had intermittent gaps, including no records for December 2021, January through September 2022, and January 2023.
Location and population
The study was conducted at King Abdulaziz University Hospital in Jeddah, Saudi Arabia, focusing on pediatric patients, particularly young infants. The median age of patients with recorded age was 9.0 months, with an interquartile range from 3.0 to 29.0 months. Patient age was unknown for 32 episodes.
Event
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) was identified as the leading pathogen in respiratory episodes, with 40.9% of the analyzed episodes testing positive for RSV, representing 61 total detections. Human rhinovirus or enterovirus was the second most prevalent, accounting for 34.2% of episodes. The study was reported by Kristina Jarvis and published in the Virology Journal.
Scale, impact and risk
RSV was the primary virus detected, with 61 cases out of 149 positive respiratory episodes. It was heavily concentrated in infants under 6 months, with 70.2% of positive episodes in this age group being RSV. There were 9 recorded deaths (6.3%) among the 143 patients. The study noted that symptom profiles did not reliably differentiate specific pathogens, and pre-existing conditions were documented in 46.3% of episodes, though no significant association was found after statistical correction.
Response, status and uncertainty
The investigation was retrospective and based on real-time PCR testing of nasopharyngeal specimens using a multiplex diagnostic panel. Due to the lack of testing denominators, the findings only reflect the relative composition of positive detections, not overall disease incidence. The authors emphasized the need for robust surveillance systems to evaluate the impact of future immunization programs.

老年人中的 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV):从看似轻微的症状到住院的风险。- 越南.vn

Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in older adults: From seemingly mild symptoms to a risk of hospitalization. - Vietnam.vn
新闻简报该文章强调了 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 对老年人的风险,强调其症状最初可能看起来很轻微,但很快就会发展为严重并发症。一名患有脊柱退化的65岁女性患者在出现类似普通感冒的轻微症状三天后出现严重呼吸短促、喘息和低氧血症,被送往胡志明市大学医学中心。她需要住院治疗并接受氧疗、无创通气和雾化治疗,治疗持续约10天。在过去的2-3年里,该中心检查了大约2,600例病例,其中84例确诊为RSV感染。大多数住院患者年龄超过 60 岁,75 岁及以上的患者面临更高的风险。RSV 会加剧糖尿病、哮喘和心血管疾病等现有疾病,可能导致住院。由于症状与流感和 COVID-19 类似,医生可能会使用呼吸道样本进行检测来识别病毒。预防措施包括手部卫生、避免与有症状的人密切接触以及监测健康状况。该文章强调了主动进行风险评估和管理潜在状况以防止严重后果的重要性。

时间
The article was published on 2026-11-17.事件日期没有明确说明,但患者的症状大约在住院前三天开始,治疗 持续了大约 10 天。
地点与对象
该病例发生在越南胡志明市大学医学中心。患者为65岁女性,患有脊柱退行性变,住院患者大多数年龄在60岁以上,其中75岁及以上的风险较高。
事件
一名65岁女性脊柱退行性患者最初出现类似普通感冒的轻微症状,如咳嗽、低烧、咽痛、咳痰等。然而,三天后,她出现严重呼吸困难、喘息和低氧血症,需要住院治疗并接受氧疗、无创通气和雾化。
规模、影响与风险
过去 2-3 年里,胡志明市大学医学中心检查了大约 2,600 例 RSV 感染病例,其中记录了 84 例 RSV 感染病例。大多数住院患者年龄超过60岁,75岁及以上的患者发生严重并发症的风险较高。有些病例需要插管、机械通气和延长 治疗。RSV 会使糖尿病、哮喘、慢性阻塞性肺病和心血管疾病等基础疾病恶化,可能导致住院。
应对、状态与不确定性
该病例由胡志明市大学医学中心呼吸科报告。医生可能会要求采集呼吸道样本进行检测,以确定病原体。目前,RSV 没有特定的、常规使用的 治疗。病情较轻时,对患者进行对症治疗和监测;在严重的情况下,可能需要氧气支持或其他复苏措施。强调预防,包括手部卫生、限制与出现呼吸道症状的人的密切接触以及监测健康状况以进行早期干预。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in older adults: From seemingly mild symptoms to a risk of hospitalization. - Vietnam.vn

News Brief
The article highlights the risks of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in older adults, emphasizing that its symptoms can initially appear mild but rapidly progress to severe complications. A 65-year-old female patient with spinal degeneration was admitted to the University Medical Center of Ho Chi Minh City after developing severe shortness of breath, wheezing, and hypoxemia three days after mild symptoms resembling a common cold. She required hospitalization and treatment with oxygen therapy, non-invasive ventilation, and nebulization, with the treatment lasting about 10 days. Over the past 2-3 years, the center has examined approximately 2,600 cases, with 84 confirmed RSV infections. Most hospitalized patients were over 60, and those 75 and older faced higher risks. RSV can exacerbate existing conditions like diabetes, asthma, and cardiovascular disease, potentially leading to hospitalization. Doctors may use respiratory samples for testing to identify the virus, as symptoms are similar to influenza and COVID-19. Prevention measures include hand hygiene, avoiding close contact with symptomatic individuals, and monitoring health. The article underscores the importance of proactive risk assessment and management of underlying conditions to prevent severe outcomes.

English Login Homepage Topic News Political System Destination Event Travel Happy Vietnam Enterprise Product Heritage Museum Figure Multimedia Data Home News Economy Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in older adults: From seemingly mild symptoms to a risk of hospitalization. A 65-year-old female patient, whose only pre-existing condition was spinal degeneration, initially presented with symptoms of cough, mild fever, sore throat, and sputum production. However, approximately three days later, she developed severe shortness of breath, wheezing, and hypoxemia, requiring hospitalization and treatment with oxygen therapy, non-invasive ventilation, and nebulization. The treatment lasted approximately 10 days. Báo Pháp Luật Việt Nam•17/11/2026 Follow Vietnam.vn on Google Add Vietnam.vn on Google 0 The above case demonstrates that respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection in adults is not always mild. The disease often begins with familiar symptoms such as cough, fever, sore throat, or phlegm, making it easily mistaken for the flu and many other respiratory illnesses. In older adults, especially those with underlying health conditions or weakened immune systems, the condition can worsen rapidly. According to Assoc. Prof. Dr. Le Thuong Vu - Department of Respiratory Medicine, University Medical Center of Ho Chi Minh City, when the infection progresses to the lower respiratory tract, patients may experience progressively worsening shortness of breath, rapid breathing, rapid heartbeat, decreased blood oxygen levels, and even respiratory failure. RSV can also destabilize existing medical conditions. In people with asthma, COPD, bronchiectasis, or lung cancer, the disease can trigger an acute exacerbation; while in people with cardiovascular disease, an infection can be associated with events such as acute heart failure, arrhythmias, or myocardial infarction. Khám phá thêm Tìm hiểu lịch sử Đọc Mẹo Du Lịch Khám phá ĐNA At the Respiratory Department of the University Medical Center of Ho Chi Minh City, approximately 2,600 cases have been examined in the last 2-3 years, with 84 cases of RSV infection recorded. The majority of hospitalized patients were over 60 years old; those 75 years and older were at higher risk of severe complications. Some cases required intubation, mechanical ventilation, and prolonged treatment. Among the underlying conditions requiring special attention, diabetes is a factor that can affect the ability to fight off infections. Associate Professor Dr. Tran Quang Nam, Head of the Endocrinology Department at the University Medical Center of Ho Chi Minh City, stated that when blood sugar levels are high and not well controlled, the immune system's function can be affected, making patients more susceptible to infections, including respiratory infections such as influenza, pneumonia, and RSV. The woman unexpectedly suffered acute heart failure after experiencing symptoms she thought were the flu.(Dan Tri Newspaper) - A patient with a history of heart failure, hypertension, and coronary artery disease was admitted to the hospital due to acute heart failure accompanied by respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection, after about a week of experiencing cough, fatigue, and mild shortness of breath. Thinking it was just a common cold, a woman was hospitalized with acute heart failure.A 67-year-old woman was unexpectedly hospitalized with acute heart failure after a week of coughing, fatigue, and mild shortness of breath. Her initial symptoms resembled those of a common cold. Associate Professor, Doctor Tran Quang Nam - Head of the Endocrinology Department, inpatient examination. According to Associate Professor Dr. Tran Quang Nam, besides the commonly mentioned complications in the cardiovascular, renal, or neurological systems, infections are also a significant concern in people with diabetes and can lead to hospitalization. Therefore, good blood sugar control, maintaining a proper diet, and regular exercise should go hand in hand with proactive infection prevention, especially in older adults or those with multiple underlying conditions. Another difficulty in identifying RSV is that its symptoms are quite similar to those of influenza and COVID-19, making it difficult to determine the causative agent based solely on clinical manifestations. When necessary, doctors may order respiratory samples for testing to identify the cause and determine appropriate monitoring and treatment. Currently, there is no specific, routinely used treatment for RSV. In mild cases, patients are primarily treated symptomatically and monitored; in severe cases, oxygen support or other resuscitation measures may be required depending on the patient's condition. You may also like For older adults and those with underlying health conditions, prevention plays a crucial role. Besides hand hygiene and limiting close contact with people exhibiting respiratory symptoms, patients should monitor their health and seek medical attention promptly if they experience shortness of breath or their condition worsens. Furthermore, each health check-up or follow-up visit provides an opportunity for patients to discuss their personal risk factors and appropriate preventive measures with their doctor. RSV may begin as a common respiratory infection, but in high-risk groups, it can rapidly worsen underlying conditions and lead to serious complications. Proactive risk assessment, effective management of existing conditions, and appropriate preventive measures are crucial in limiting the risk of severe progression and contributing to maintaining health and quality of life. Moderna RSV Shot 82% Effective Against Hospitalization in Veterans 50 and Older, but Infection Protection Fell With Age - Medical Daily
新闻简报根据退伍军人健康管理局于 2026 年 11 月 14 日发表的一项研究,Moderna 的针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的 mRNA 疫苗 (RSV) mResvia 在预防 50 岁及以上美国退伍军人中与 RSV 相关的住院治疗方面有 81.8% 的效率。CIDRAP 报道的这项研究从 10 月 31 日开始追踪 疫苗接种,2026 年,随访时间长达 24 周。对记录在案的感染的保护率较低,为 60.3%,并且感染的有效性随着年龄的增长而下降,50 岁至 59 岁的退伍军人为 82.9%,而 80 岁及以上的退伍军人仅为 28.6%。疫苗 在后续赛季中的表现仍然未知,因为目前已给出一次。FDA 于 2024 年 5 月批准 mResvia 用于 60 岁及以上的成年人,并于 2025 年 6 月将其用途扩大到 RSV 肺部疾病风险较高的 18 至 59 岁成年人。VA 的另一项预印本研究估计对住院治疗的有效性为 87%,但尚未经过同行评审。该结果是临床试验之外对 mRNA 疫苗 进行的首次大型真实分析的一部分。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究于2026年11月14日发表,疫苗接种周期从2026年10月31日开始追踪,随访时间长达24周。随着RSV季节在全国范围内的到来,调查结果被报道。
地点与对象
这项研究涉及 50 岁及以上的美国退伍军人,他们通过退伍军人健康管理局接受护理。该分析包括 74,926 名接种疫苗的退伍军人和 286,425 名未接种疫苗的退伍军人。
事件
CIDRAP 报道的 JAMA Network Open 上的一项大型退伍军人事务研究表明,Moderna 针对 呼吸道合胞病毒 (mResvia) 的 mRNA 疫苗 在预防 50 岁及以上美国退伍军人中与 RSV 相关的住院治疗方面的有效性为 81.8%。对任何记录在案的感染的保护率较低,为 60.3%,并且随着年龄的增长,对感染的保护率也急剧下降。
规模、影响与风险
疫苗 对 50 岁至 59 岁的退伍军人有 82.9% 的有效率,但对 80 岁及以上的退伍军人只有 28.6% 的有效率。该研究是首次大型现实世界研究 mRNA 疫苗 在临床试验之外的表现。FDA 于 2024 年 5 月批准 mResvia 用于 60 岁及以上的成人,并于 2025 年 6 月将批准范围扩大到 RSV 肺部疾病风险较高的 18 至 59 岁成人。
应对、状态与不确定性
研究结果基于目标试验设计,该设计试图模仿使用健康记录的随机试验。CIDRAP 报道了这项研究,其结果是正在进行的分析的一部分。作为预印本发表的一项单独的 VA 分析估计,使用不同的设计对 RSV 住院治疗的有效性为 87%,但这尚未经过同行评审。mResvia 的保护是否能持续到第二季或第三季尚不清楚。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Moderna RSV Shot 82% Effective Against Hospitalization in Veterans 50 and Older, but Infection Protection Fell With Age - Medical Daily

News Brief
Moderna's mRNA vaccine against respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), mResvia, was found to be 81.8% effective at preventing RSV-related hospitalization among U.S. veterans aged 50 and older, according to a study published by the Veterans Health Administration on Nov 14, 2026. The study, reported by CIDRAP, tracked vaccinations from Oct. 31, 2026, with up to 24 weeks of follow-up. Protection against documented infection was lower, at 60.3%, and effectiveness against infection decreased with age, being 82.9% in veterans aged 50 to 59 and only 28.6% in those 80 and older. The vaccine's performance in subsequent seasons remains unknown, as it is currently given once. The FDA approved mResvia in May 2024 for adults 60 and older and expanded its use in June 2025 to adults 18 to 59 at increased risk of RSV lung disease. A separate VA preprint study estimated 87% effectiveness against hospitalization but has not been peer-reviewed. The results are part of the first large real-world analysis of the mRNA vaccine outside clinical trials.

Experts warn about opioid-like effects, addiction and concentrated 7-OH products.
